### [1] [DataEvolver: Automatic Data Preparation for Large Language Models through Multi-Level Self-Evolving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07001)
*Chao Deng,Shaolei Zhang,Ju Fan,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DataEvolver 是一个自演化的数据准备系统，能够自动构建数据处理流水线，将原始数据转化为高质量训练数据，无需人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量训练数据对LLM至关重要，但人工标注成本高昂；现有自动数据准备方法依赖于预定义流水线或人工指令，适应性和原则性指导不足。

Method: 提出DataEvolver，采用多层级机制：算子级逐步扩展算子集并解决依赖冲突以构建逻辑计划；流水线级将逻辑计划实例化为可执行代码，通过反馈循环迭代优化流水线编排，缩小准备数据与高质量示例之间的分布差异。

Result: 在七个基准测试中，DataEvolver显著提升了数据质量，相比原始数据训练，下游LLM性能平均提升10%。

Conclusion: DataEvolver为LLM与数据的迭代共同演化提供了新方向，展示了自动数据准备系统的巨大潜力。

Abstract: High-quality training data is essential to large language models (LLMs) and typically requires extensive and costly manual curation. Existing automatic data preparation methods rely on predefined pipelines or customized human instructions, which limits their adaptability to diverse data distributions and lacks principled guidance from high-quality examples. In this paper, we introduce DataEvolver, the first self-evolving data preparation system that automatically constructs pipelines to transform raw data into high-quality data. DataEvolver employs a multi-level mechanism to ensure both pipeline executability and effectiveness. At the operator level, it incrementally expands the operator set to construct a logical plan while resolving dependency conflicts. At the pipeline level, it instantiates logical plans into executable code and iteratively refines pipeline orchestration through a feedback loop that reduces the distribution gap between prepared data and high-quality examples. Experiments on seven benchmarks show that DataEvolver substantially improves data quality and achieves an average 10\% gain in downstream LLM performance compared with training on original data, highlighting new opportunities for the iterative co-evolution of LLMs and data.

</details>


### [2] [Auto-Relate: A Unified Approach to Discovering Reliable Functional Relationships Leveraging Statistical Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07060)
*Ziyan Han,Yeye He,Shuyuan Kang,Min Xie,Weiwei Cui,Song Ge,Haidong Zhang,Dongmei Zhang,Surajit Chaudhuri,Rui Mao,Jianbin Qin*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出功能关系（FR）作为表格列间关系的统一概念，涵盖算术关系、字符串变换和函数依赖。通过准确性、原子性、稳定性和完整性四个标准评估FR可靠性，并设计了Auto-Relate框架（先挖掘后验证），包含最小性测试、扰动测试和独立性测试。在58,679个真实表格上实验，Auto-Relate平均PR-AUC达0.87，比最优基线高59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格中的列间关系通常是隐式的，在导出为标准格式时容易丢失。简单地挖掘在观测表上成立的关系是不够的，因为许多关系由于巧合、冗余或数据多样性有限而是虚假的。恢复这些关系对下游任务（表格理解、数据质量改进、溯源分析）有益。

Method: 提出功能关系（FR）统一概念，涵盖算术关系、字符串变换和函数依赖。通过四个标准评估FR可靠性：准确性、原子性、稳定性、完整性。设计Auto-Relate框架：先生成准确候选FR，再通过最小性测试（验证原子性）、扰动测试（验证稳定性）和独立性测试（验证完整性）验证剩余标准。提出三种优化策略：分组下界用于早期拒绝、封闭形式加速用于算术FR、二项式界用于统计引导的提前终止。

Result: 在58,679个真实电子表格和关系表（含6,414个真实FR）上进行实验，对比18个基线。Auto-Relate在所有设置下均取得最佳性能，平均PR-AUC为0.87，比最强基线高59%。

Conclusion: Auto-Relate通过统一的FR概念和四项可靠性标准，有效恢复了表格中隐含的列间关系，显著优于现有方法，为表格理解、数据质量改进和溯源分析提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Tables in spreadsheets, computational notebooks, and databases often contain rich inter-column relationships. Yet these relationships are typically implicit and are often lost when tables are exported to standard formats. Recovering them can benefit downstream tasks, including table understanding, data quality improvement, and provenance analysis. However, simply mining relationships that hold on an observed table is insufficient, as many are spurious due to coincidence, redundancy, or limited data diversity. In this paper, we introduce functional relationships (FRs) as a unified notion for inter-column relationships in tables, subsuming arithmetic relationships, string transformations, and functional dependencies. We characterize FR reliability through four complementary criteria: accuracy, atomicity, stability, and integrity. Guided by these criteria, we propose Auto-Relate, a mine-then-verify framework that first generates accurate candidate FRs and then verifies the remaining reliability criteria through a Minimality Test, a Perturbation Test, and an Independence Test, respectively. To further improve efficiency, we develop three optimization strategies, including a group-by lower bound for early rejection, a closed-form speedup for arithmetic FRs, and a binomial bound for statistically guided early termination. We construct a large-scale benchmark suite from 58,679 real-world spreadsheets and relational tables, containing 6,414 ground-truth FRs spanning all three FR types. Extensive experiments against 18 baselines show that Auto-Relate consistently achieves the best performance, with an average PR-AUC of 0.87, 59% higher than the best competing baseline across all settings.

</details>


### [3] [Efficient $(α,β)$-core Computation and On-the-fly Query at Billion Scale with GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07148)
*Qingshuai Feng,Shunyang Li,Kai Wang,Xuemin Lin,Kongzhang Hao,Long Yuan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出基于GPU的无索引剥离算法GCC和GCC+，以及连通性感知的即席查询算法GFQ，用于高效计算二分图中的(α,β)-core并支持实时边更新查询，在11个数据集上优于现有CPU方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CPU的(α,β)-core方法需要为所有(α,β)组合构建索引结构，扩展性差且维护开销高，无法支持大规模二分图上的即席查询（如欺诈监控、推荐系统等实时应用）。

Method: 提出GCC，一种无索引的GPU剥离算法，利用warp-centric处理加速计算；提出GCC+，利用(α,β)-core的嵌套性质实现基于core的早期剪枝策略；提出GFQ，一种连通性感知算法，通过利用连通分量信息缩小计算范围，避免全图剥离来处理即席查询。

Result: 在11个数据集上的实验表明，所提出的方法在空间和时间效率上均优于现有CPU方案。

Conclusion: GPU架构可有效加速(α,β)-core计算，无索引的剥离算法结合早期剪枝策略能克服GPU内存限制，连通性感知机制能高效支持即席查询。

Abstract: In bipartite graphs, $(α,β)$-core is a widely used model for cohesive subgraph mining. Specifically, an $(α,β)$-core is a maximal subgraph in which each vertex in the upper layer has degree at least $α$, and each vertex in the lower layer has degree at least $β$. The state-of-the-art CPU-based solutions incur extensive costs to construct an index structure for all $α$ and $β$ combinations, leading to scalability challenges on large bipartite graphs. Moreover, on-the-fly queries, which aim to determine whether an edge update belongs to a target $(α,β)$-core, are essential for real-time applications such as fraud monitoring and recommendation systems. However, existing index-based methods struggle to support such queries at scale due to their high maintenance overhead. In this paper, we investigate how to leverage GPU architectures to enable efficient $(α,β)$-core computation and support on-the-fly queries. While GPUs are widely used to accelerate graph processing, their limited memory capacity makes it impractical to store large index structures. To address this issue, we propose GCC, an index-free GPU-based peeling algorithm that accelerates $(α,β)$-core computation via warp-centric processing. To further improve efficiency, we develop GCC+, which leverages the nested property of $(α,β)$-core with a core-based early pruning strategy. For handling on-the-fly queries, we propose GFQ, a connectivity-aware algorithm that significantly narrows the computation scope by leveraging connected component information, thereby avoiding full-graph peeling. Extensive experiments on 11 datasets demonstrate that our proposed techniques outperform existing CPU-based solutions in terms of both space and time efficiency.

</details>

### [4] [Robots Need More than VLA and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06556)
*Elis Karcini,Faisal Mehrban,Quang Nguyen,Mac Schwager,Arash Ajoudani,Cesar Cadena,Jan Peters,Marco Hutter,Haitham Bou-Ammar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出通用机器人智能的核心瓶颈不仅是策略学习，还在于缺乏将非结构化行为数据转化为具身机器人可用的监督信号的机制。文章识别了四个缺失组件：自动标注数据接口、人体运动重定向接口、物理基础3D推理的世界模型接口、以及从视频和语言推断任务进展的奖励接口，并提出了相应的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战当前将通用机器人智能简单视为策略扩展问题的主流观点，指出仅靠收集更多演示数据、训练更大VLA模型不足以实现广泛泛化，核心瓶颈在于缺少将世界上海量非结构化行为数据转化为机器人可用的监督信号的机制。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）形式，通过梳理机器人基础模型、跨具身数据集、视频学习、世界模型和奖励建模等领域的最新进展，识别出四个关键缺失组件（数据接口、具身接口、世界模型接口、奖励接口），并提出研究议程。

Result: 识别出四个阻碍非结构化数据用于机器人学习的核心缺失组件：(1) 自动标注非结构化行为的数据接口；(2) 将人体运动重定向到机器人动作的具身接口；(3) 基于物理的3D推理世界模型接口；(4) 从视频和语言推断任务进度和成功与否的奖励接口。

Conclusion: 未来机器人系统应不仅能从机器人演示中学习，还能从更广泛的物理世界中学习，这需要构建上述四个接口组件来解锁人类运动、互联网视频、仿真滚动物和交互演示等非结构化数据的潜力。

Abstract: Generalist robot intelligence is often framed as a policy-scaling problem: collect more robot demonstrations, train larger Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, and expect broader generalisation. In this position paper, we argue that this framing is incomplete. The central bottleneck is not only policy learning, but the absence of mechanisms that convert the world's abundant unstructured behavioural data into grounded robot supervision. Human motion, internet video, simulation rollouts, and interactive demonstrations contain rich information about tasks, goals, contacts, failures, and physical constraints, yet most of this information is not directly usable by robot policies because it lacks embodiment-specific action labels, task semantics, and reward structure. We identify four missing components for the next generation of robotics: data interfaces for autolabelling unstructured behaviour, embodiment interfaces for retargeting human motion to robot actions, world-model interfaces for physics-grounded 3D reasoning, and reward interfaces for inferring task progress and success from video and language. We survey recent progress in robot foundation models, cross-embodiment datasets, learning from video, world models, and reward modelling, and propose a research agenda for building robotics systems that can learn not only from robot demonstrations, but from the broader physical world.

</details>


### [5] [ChronoForest: Closed-Loop Multi-Tree Diffusion Planning for Efficient Bridge Search and Route Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06618)
*Jungmin Seo,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对离线导航中仅拥有短程轨迹数据却需完成长程路径规划的问题，本文提出ChronoForest系统，通过锚链树扩散规划器与在线多树协调器，实现局部桥搜索与在线路由重解耦的闭环规划。在OGBench AntMaze-Stitch数据集上，中型、大型和巨型分片成功率分别达到99.8%、99.3%和99.5%，巨型分片成功率较现有扩散方法提升最多34.5个点；在哈密顿路径组合基准上，在线重解可纠正不良时间排序并提升路径质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线场景下收集足够丰富的长程轨迹数据非常困难，而实际智能体仍需以路线级效率（而非仅有可行性）解决长距任务。现有方法在微观层面面临组合短程片段时搜索代价与路径质量的权衡，在宏观层面则面临航点排序时起点、终点和航点锚点间的成对旅行成本未知且仅靠长程时间距离估计可靠性低的问题。

Method: 提出ChronoForest闭环规划系统，包含两个核心组件：(1) 锚链树扩散规划器，利用时间距离进行短程引导与节点评估，同时使用搜索时的桥证据验证长程锚点连通性；(2) 在线多树协调器，反复重解路由。通过局部桥搜索与在线路由重解耦实现闭环规划。

Result: 在OGBench AntMaze-Stitch数据集上，中型、大型和巨型分片成功率分别达99.8%、99.3%和99.5%，巨型分片成功率较现有扩散方法提升最多34.5个百分点。在哈密顿路径组合基准上，在线重解可纠正不良时间排序并提升路径质量，同时计算成本远低于穷举规划。

Conclusion: ChronoForest有效解决了仅依赖短程离线轨迹进行长程路径规划中的微观组合权衡与宏观航点排序两大难题，通过闭环规划机制在保持高效的同时显著提升了长距导航的成功率与路径质量。

Abstract: How can we plan long-horizon routes that reach designated goals, visit required waypoints, and remain short when only short-horizon offline trajectories are available? This problem matters in offline navigation because collecting sufficiently rich long-horizon data is difficult, yet real agents must still solve long-range tasks with route-level efficiency rather than mere feasibility. The difficulty is twofold: at the microscopic level, composing many short-horizon segments creates a trade-off between search cost and path quality, while at the macroscopic level, waypoint ordering requires comparing pairwise travel costs among start, goal, and waypoint anchors that are unknown before planning and increasingly unreliable when estimated only from long-range temporal distance.
  In this paper, we propose ChronoForest, a closed-loop planning system that couples local bridge search and online route re-solving through an anchor-chaining tree diffusion planner and an online multi-tree orchestrator. ChronoForest uses temporal distance for short-range guidance and node evaluation, while using search-time bridge evidence to validate long-range anchor connectivity and repeatedly re-solve the route. On OGBench AntMaze-Stitch, ChronoForest achieves 99.8%, 99.3%, and 99.5% success on the medium, large, and giant splits and improves giant-stitch success by up to 34.5 points over prior reported diffusion-based results. On Hamiltonian route-composition benchmarks, online re-solving corrects poor temporal orderings and improves route quality while remaining substantially cheaper than exhaustive planning.

</details>


### [6] [What Matters When Cotraining Robot Manipulation Policies on Everyday Human Videos?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06627)
*Richard Li,Aditya Prakash,Andrew Wen,Saurabh Gupta,Yilun Du,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何利用互联网日常视频（而非专门录制的演示视频）与机器人数据协同训练操作策略。通过一个包含532段高质量手部标注视频的数据集，发现手部姿态质量影响迁移效果，但即使手部标注精确，运动模式差异仍会阻碍迁移，除非视觉和策略网络针对每种具身形态进行特化。提出的协同训练方法在低机器人数据条件下，六个操作任务的平均成功率提升了29.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略的协同训练多依赖精心编排的演示视频和专用硬件捕获的3D手部姿态，而互联网日常视频数量更丰富，但其对机器人迁移的有效因素尚不明确。本文旨在探究哪些因素能够促成从互联网视频到机器人的有效迁移。

Method: 构建了一个包含532段人类视频（28小时高质量三角测量手部标签、自然运动）的新数据集；对比不同手部姿态质量对迁移的影响；提出视觉和策略网络针对每种具身形态进行特化的协同训练方案。

Result: 手部姿态质量影响迁移效果；即使手部标注精确，运动模式差异仍构成阻碍；所提出的协同训练方案在低机器人数据条件下，六个操作任务的平均绝对成功率提升29.7%。

Conclusion: 从互联网视频到机器人的迁移可行且有效，关键在于视觉与策略网络需针对具身形态特化；高质量手部姿态有助于迁移，但运动模式差异需通过网络特化来解决。

Abstract: Human video datasets used for cotraining robot manipulation policies largely consist of curated demonstrations where motions are orchestrated to resemble robot behavior and 3D hand poses are captured with specialized hardware. A more plentiful source of data is everyday Internet video, but it is an open question what factors enable transfer from such videos to robots. We investigate this using a new dataset of 532 human videos with 28 hours of high-quality triangulated hand labels and natural motions. We find that hand pose quality affects transfer, but even with accurate hands, the inherent motion gap hinders transfer unless the vision and policy networks specialize to each embodiment. Our cotraining recipe yields consistent improvements, with an absolute success rate gain of $29.7\%$ in the low-robot-data regime across six manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [7] [On the Hardness of Optimal Motion on Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06686)
*Tzvika Geft*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，证明了树结构上多智能体路径规划问题（MAPF）及其变体（标记/彩色、距离/最大完工时间/流动时间）均为NP-hard，包括经典的石子移动问题（Pebble Motion）和堆栈重排问题（Stack Rearrangement），揭示了这些基础运动模型共同的难解性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAPF的许多变体已知是难解的，但树结构上若干基本情形（包括经典的Pebble Motion问题）的复杂性几十年悬而未决，本文旨在解决这些开放问题，并寻找不同运动模型之间的统一复杂度边界。

Method: 通过建立MAPF问题与堆栈重排问题（Stack Rearrangement）之间的归约关系，证明堆栈重排本身是NP-hard，进而归约到树结构上的标记/彩色MAPF及其各优化目标，所有结果基于极简树结构（细分星图）即可成立。

Result: 证明了树结构上标记和2-彩色MAPF在三种目标（距离、最大完工时间、流动时间）下均为NP-hard；解决了Pebble Motion在树上几十年悬而未决的复杂性；给出了2-彩色Pebble Motion在任何图类上的首个难解性结果；证明了Stack Rearrangement为NP-hard。

Conclusion: 树结构上标记和2-彩色MAPF的所有标准目标均为NP-hard，且这一难解性边界通过堆栈重排问题统一了多个基础运动模型，揭示了它们共同的复杂度壁垒。

Abstract: This paper presents a simple framework that settles the complexity of Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) on trees across standard objectives--distance, makespan, and flowtime--for both labeled and colored variants. In MAPF, agents occupy the vertices of a graph and must move to target vertices without collisions while optimizing a given objective. In the labeled case, the agents are distinct and have respective targets; in the colored case, agents of the same color are interchangeable. While many MAPF variants are known to be intractable, several basic cases on trees have remained open. We prove NP-hardness on trees for both labeled and 2-colored MAPF under all three objectives. In particular, we resolve the classical Pebble Motion problem, where one pebble moves at a time to an adjacent empty vertex and the goal is to minimize the total number of moves. Despite being one of the most basic discrete motion models, its complexity on trees had remained open for several decades. Moreover, for colored Pebble Motion, we give the first hardness result on any graph class, already with two colors, which is tight.
  All of these results are established through the hardness of Stack Rearrangement, itself posed as an open problem, which asks to optimally rearrange items stored in stacks, and which we also prove to be NP-hard. Notably, the connection to stacks yields hardness already on very simple trees--subdivided stars--across all problems. Together, these results reveal a common tractability barrier that permeates several fundamental motion models, thereby unifying and strengthening prior hardness results.

</details>


### [8] [Optimal Control Approach for Non-prehensile Ball Juggling Using a 7-DoF Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06704)
*Joel Ramadani,Vasilije Rakčević,Riddhiman Laha,Arne Sachtler,Valentin Le Mesle,Achim J. Lilienthal,Sami Haddadin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型的框架，用于控制7自由度机械臂使用工具进行非抓取式抛球杂耍。该框架采用两阶段最优控制方法离线生成杂耍轨迹，并组织轨迹实现实时误差修正，无需在线求解最优控制问题。通过仿真和Franka Emika Panda机器人实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非抓取式物体操作技能对于现实世界机器人交互至关重要，但高速、混合动力学特性的杂耍任务尤为困难。由于被操作物体缺乏自校正能力，其稳定性完全依赖于施加的力，系统对控制输入敏感且对时机要求严格，亟需一种系统化的控制方法。

Method: 提出一个两阶段最优控制框架：第一阶段离线计算可行杂耍运动模式；第二阶段将离线轨迹组织起来，实现无需在线求解最优控制问题的实时误差校正。该方法应用于7自由度机械臂使用工具进行非抓取式抛球杂耍。

Result: 在仿真环境中评估了控制器的性能，并使用Franka Emika Panda机器人进行了真实实验，验证了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该模型驱动框架能够有效实现机械臂的非抓取式球体杂耍，通过离线规划和实时校正相结合的方式解决了混合动力学系统的稳定控制问题。

Abstract: Non-prehensile object manipulation skills are important for real-world robot interactions, enabling highly dynamic tasks such as balancing a glass on a tray or the controlled sliding of items on a table. Among such tasks, those characterised by high-speed manipulation requirements and general sensitivity of the resulting hybrid dynamics are particularly hard to accomplish. Within these, juggling can be seen as a highly challenging maneuver to be solved. The key to robotic juggling is achieving dynamic stabilisation of an underactuated object. Since the object does not possess the ability of self-correction, its stability is entirely dependent on the forces applied to it. This creates a system that is sensitive to control inputs, where timing is critical to continuously counteract deviations and maintain the desired behavior. We develop a systematic method to control a 7-degree-of-freedom manipulator performing non-prehensile ball juggling with a tool. Our primary contribution is a model-based framework for generating juggling trajectories and stabilizing a periodic juggling motion for this hybrid system. The framework incorporates a two-stage optimal control approach to compute the underlying feasible motion patterns required for stable juggling. Offline-computed trajectories are then organised to enable real-time error correction without solving optimal control problems online. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the resulting controller by first evaluating its performance in a simulation environment and performing an experiment using a Franka Emika Panda robot.

</details>


### [9] [SCOUT: Semantic scene COverage via Uncertainty-guided Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06721)
*Junyu Mao,Sara Ayoubi,Vishnu D. Sharma,Ilija Hadžić,Matthew Andrews*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOUT是一个在线语义探索框架，通过结合主动遍历与概率场景图构建，使机器人能够在长期运行中主动理解环境，将语义场景完整性作为操作目标而非被动副产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图管线将感知视为固定数据集的后处理阶段，解耦了场景表示与决定观察内容的决策过程，导致机器人无法在长期运行中主动理解环境。

Method: SCOUT基于先验2D占据地图和带位姿的RGB-D观测，增量构建不确定性感知的3D场景图。节点维护融合几何和开放词汇目标标签的后验信念，边编码结构关系。不确定性引导的遍历规划器通过平衡语义确定性增益、几何覆盖增益和移动成本选择视点，使机器人能在需要时重新审视模糊目标，场景不完整时探索未知空间。

Result: 实现了语义场景完整性作为操作目标的自主探索系统，能够在室内环境中巡逻、更新和推理，减少人工干预。

Conclusion: SCOUT将语义场景构建从被动后处理转变为主动操作目标，推动了能够在演化环境中自主运行的智能体发展。

Abstract: Robots that operate over extended periods should not merely visit space; they should progressively understand it. Yet most 3D scene graph pipelines treat perception as a post-processing stage over a fixed dataset, decoupling scene representation from the decisions that determine what is observed in the first place. We present SCOUT, an online semantic exploration framework that closes this loop by coupling active traversal with probabilistic scene graph construction. Given a prior 2D occupancy map and posed RGB-D observations, SCOUT incrementally builds an uncertainty-aware 3D scene graph whose nodes maintain fused geometry and posterior beliefs over open-vocabulary object labels, while edges encode structural relations such as on, inside, belong, and next to. These beliefs are fed back to an uncertainty-guided traversal planner, which selects viewpoints by balancing expected semantic certainty gain, geometric coverage gain, and travel cost. In this way, the robot revisits ambiguous objects when additional evidence matters and expands into unseen free space when the scene remains incomplete. The resulting system treats semantic scene completeness as an operational objective rather than a passive by-product of semantic mapping, moving toward autonomous agents that can patrol, update, and reason about evolving indoor environments with minimal human intervention.

</details>


### [10] [IDDMBSE: Integrating Data-Driven and Model-Based Systems Engineering for Trusted Autonomous Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06727)
*John S. Baras,Sai Sandeep Damera,Ryan Matheu,Clinton Enwerem,Praveen M. S. Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出IDDMBSE，一种将MBSE V流程与数据驱动循环深度融合的系统工程方法论，并通过PERFECT、TRADES-X、VERITAS三件套开源工具链实现，在可信自主地面机器人全生命周期开发中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主CPS处于MBSE与数据驱动ML/AI的交叉领域，但现有SE方法论无法原生统一两者，缺乏端到端的集成框架。

Method: 提出IDDMBSE方法论，在传统MBSE V流程每一步嵌入数据驱动循环；构建PERFECT（SysML到ROS架构映射）、TRADES-X（模型+数据混合优化）和VERITAS（形式化+数据+运行时联合验证）三工具链；基于SysML v2/KerML重构以增强语言原生可组合性。

Result: 在可信自主地面机器人全生命周期中验证，涵盖传感器选型、风险敏感路径规划、行为树任务验证、保形预测鲁棒感知及多机器人协同，并在Isaac Sim复杂地形测试平台中完成评估。

Conclusion: IDDMBSE为自主CPS提供了首个原生集成MBSE与数据驱动方法的SE方法论与开源工具链，未来将向SysML v2迁移以实现更紧密的ML/AI集成。

Abstract: Autonomous cyber-physical systems (CPS) sit at the intersection of Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) and data-driven Machine Learning and Artificial Intelligence (ML/AI), yet no integrated Systems Engineering (SE) methodology natively spans both. We address this gap with IDDMBSE, an Integrated Data-Driven and Model-Based Systems Engineering methodology that extends the rigorous MBSE V-process with a data-driven loop at every step, anchored in SysML, the autonomy stack, and a hybrid model-based plus data-driven trade-off architecture. We instantiate IDDMBSE as an interoperable, open-source tool chain: PERFECT, which maps SysML system architectures to executable ROS autonomy stacks for scalable performance evaluation; TRADES-X, which decomposes design-space exploration into a model-based optimization stage followed by a data-driven evaluation stage; and VERITAS, which combines formal, data-driven, and runtime verification into a single assurance workflow. We demonstrate IDDMBSE on a Trusted Autonomous Ground Robot across its development lifecycle, spanning sensor-suite selection, risk-sensitive path planning, behavior-tree task verification, conformal-prediction-based robust perception, and assured multi-robot coordination, all exercised in a contested-terrain Isaac Sim test range that we release with the tool chain. We close by sketching how IDDMBSE is being re-formulated on SysML v2 / KerML foundations to enable language-native composability and tighter ML/AI integration.

</details>


### [11] [Learning All-Terrain Locomotion for a Planetary Rover with Actively Articulated Suspension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06790)
*Arthur Bouton,Tristan D. Hasseler,Michael Paton,Travis Brown,Jacob Levy,William Reid,Joshua Martin,Hari Nayar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ERNEST是一款四轮行星探测车，配备主动万向悬挂系统，通过单一神经网络控制器实现强化学习训练的自主障碍穿越策略，在多种复杂地形下成功零样本迁移到物理样机，并在20°干沙坡上降低运输成本37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行星探测车的被动悬挂系统在复杂非结构化地形（如松软土壤、陡坡、岩石场）上通过性受限，需要一种能够主动重构车轮姿态、动态分配负载的悬挂系统，并建立统一的控制框架来消除显式地形分类和控制器切换的依赖。

Method: 设计了具有偏航和横滚两个自由度的主动万向悬挂系统；基于高保真DARTS仿真引擎（刚性接触动力学+Bekker-Wong地面力学）构建强化学习框架；采用策略整合策略将不同地形特化智能体的经验合并为单一神经网络；通过域随机化、传感器噪声注入和系统辨识实现零样本迁移。

Result: 在岩石场、凸起陷阱、轮高台阶、沙波纹和沙坡等多种地形上实现自主穿越；在20°干沙坡上运输成本降低37%；在湿沙坡上被动悬挂完全无法移动时主动悬挂仍能成功通过。

Conclusion: 主动万向悬挂结合强化学习训练的统一神经网络控制器能够显著提升行星探测车在极端地形中的自主穿越能力，并通过零样本迁移在物理样机上验证了有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents ERNEST, a four-wheeled planetary rover concept equipped with a two-degree-of-freedom Active Gimbal Suspension that combines yaw and roll actuation to enable wheel reconfiguration, steering, and active load redistribution. A single neural network controller, trained to track a desired path across challenging terrain, fully unlocks the capabilities of this actuated suspension system for autonomous obstacle negotiation. A reinforcement learning framework is developed using the high-fidelity DARTS simulation engine, which combines rigid-contact dynamics and Bekker-Wong terramechanics, enabling the emergence of locomotion strategies adapted to loose-soil conditions. To obtain a single unified controller across heterogeneous terrains, a policy consolidation strategy merges the experience of terrain-specialized agents into one neural network, eliminating the need for explicit terrain classification and controller switching. The resulting controller operates on a combination of proprioceptive and exteroceptive feedback, including sparse stereo-derived terrain elevation, chassis attitude, joint states, and force-torque measurements. Zero-shot transfer to the physical rover is achieved through domain randomization, sensor noise injection, and model-to-real system identification. Experimental results demonstrate autonomous traversal of rock fields, a bump trap, a wheel-high step, sand ripples, and sandy slopes. On a 20° sandy slope, the learned controller reduces the cost of transport by 37% on dry sand despite the additional actuation, and achieves superior performance on wet sand where the passive suspension becomes completely immobilized.

</details>


### [12] [AxisGuide: Grounding Robot Action Coordinate System in RGB Observations for Robust Visuomotor Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06761)
*Jiyun Jang,Yujin Sung,Woosung Joung,Daewon Chae,Sangwon Lee,Sohwi Kim,Jinkyu Kim,Jungbeom Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AxisGuide方法，通过在视觉观察中显式渲染机器人基座坐标系轴，帮助模仿学习策略理解动作坐标系统，从而提升视觉运动操控策略在分布外场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模行为克隆训练的视觉运动操控策略虽具备强语义场景理解能力，但在分布偏移下（如物体置于未见位置）低级动作执行可靠性显著下降，其根源在于策略缺乏对机器人基座坐标系在图像空间中含义的理解。

Method: AxisGuide利用相机参数和末端执行器位姿，在每个相机视图中渲染机器人基座坐标系轴（+x, +y, +z），并将这些显式的坐标轴线索作为少量额外通道叠加到RGB观测中，形成增强的视觉输入。

Result: 在LIBERO仿真环境和真实世界实验中，AxisGuide显著提升了任务成功率和泛化能力，验证了显式动作坐标线索对学习可靠且可迁移的通用视觉运动策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 桥接语义场景理解与动作坐标解释是提升视觉运动操控策略分布外泛化能力的关键，AxisGuide提供了一种轻量级且有效的引导方案。

Abstract: Visuomotor manipulation policies trained via large-scale behavior cloning have achieved strong semantic scene understanding, yet often fail to reliably execute correct low-level actions under distribution shifts. For example, even in a simple pickup task with identical scene layouts, camera viewpoints, and illumination, performance can degrade substantially when the object is placed at unseen locations. We argue that this gap arises from insufficient action understanding, namely the inability to interpret the robot's base-frame action coordinate system in image space. To address this issue, we introduce AxisGuide, a lightweight guidance method that bridges semantic scene understanding and action-coordinate interpretation. Using camera parameters and end-effector poses, AxisGuide renders the robot base-frame axes in each camera view and augments RGB observations with a small set of cue channels that explicitly visualize the meaning of the +x, +y, and +z motions in image space. Extensive evaluations in both the LIBERO simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that AxisGuide yields substantial performance gains and improved generalization, highlighting the effectiveness of explicit action-coordinate cues for learning reliable and transferable generalist visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [13] [Lane Change Trajectory Planning for Personalized Driving Comfort and Mobility Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06805)
*Haoxuan Dong,Dongjun Li,Ziyou Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经网络驱动的换道轨迹规划器，采用三阶多项式轨迹生成器与双头共享骨干架构，通过统计门控机制（基于误差胜者逻辑回归）自适应切换头部分支，在保证全工况可行性的同时实现个性化舒适性与机动性偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 换道涉及纵向与横向运动的强耦合，且存在显著的人-车及工况间变异性，现有方法难以兼顾个性化偏好与全工况下的轨迹可行性保障。

Method: 提出神经网络规划器，包含：(1) 三阶多项式轨迹生成器；(2) 共享骨干网络后接双头结构——一个头保障全工况操作可行性，另一个头捕获驾驶员对舒适性或机动效率的个性化偏好；(3) 基于误差胜者逻辑回归的统计门控机制，自适应切换合适的头部以适应不同驾驶工况。

Result: 代表性案例与蒙特卡洛仿真表明，该规划器在个性化数据充足时实现个性化舒适与机动性；在数据不足或不可用时，基线头部能保证可行轨迹生成。

Conclusion: 所提方法在个性化与泛化能力之间取得了有效平衡，实现了上下文感知的换道轨迹规划。

Abstract: Lane changing entails simultaneous longitudinal and lateral motions that affect driving comfort and mobility efficiency. Because these motions are tightly coupled and subject to substantial inter-vehicle variability, trajectory planning for lane-change maneuvers is characterized by a highly personalized nature. This study proposes a neural network-driven planner that integrates a third-order polynomial trajectory generator with a learning module that infers optimal trajectory parameters across diverse driving conditions. Using a shared backbone with dual heads, one head ensures all-condition operational guarantees, while the other captures driver-specific preferences for comfort or mobility efficiency. A head-gated switching mechanism, realized through a statistical gate based on error-winner logistic regression, adaptively selects the appropriate head under varying driving conditions, which enables context-aware lane-change trajectory planning. Representative cases and Monte Carlo simulations show that the proposed planner achieves personalized comfort and mobility during lane changes, while the baseline ensures feasible trajectories under driving conditions where personalized data are insufficient or inaccessible.

</details>


### [14] [Multi-Robot Planning and Control from CCTV Camera Networks in a Real Warehouse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06762)
*Luke Robinson,Benjamin Ramtoula,Anas Izaaryene,Paul Newman,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅利用环境摄像头网络（CCTV）和边缘计算实现仓库中多机器人协同控制的方法，系统在未标定的像素级拓扑相机图上运行，通过分层规划器选择相机序列并规划图像空间运动，采用优先级-联合策略协调机器人，并在真实仓库中完成首次现场验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将移动机器人的感知和计算转移到环境中的摄像头和边缘设备上，以实现可扩展的自主性，并将这一思路从单机器人扩展到多机器人协同场景。

Method: 利用分布式CCTV网络和边缘计算，在未标定的像素级拓扑相机图上运行系统；采用分层规划器为每个机器人选择相机序列并规划图像空间运动；使用优先级-联合策略协调机器人，将重叠相机区域视为一次仅由一个机器人持有的共享资源，以防止碰撞和死锁。

Result: 在真实仓库中部署4台机器人和30个摄像头（覆盖6条27米通道），报告了任务时间和协调统计数据。

Conclusion: 这是首次仅利用外部摄像头网络和离板计算实现多机器人规划与协调的现场演示，机器人无需携带任何任务专用导航硬件。

Abstract: Off-board control of mobile robots from cameras embedded in the environment offers a practical path to scalable autonomy, moving sensing and compute off the robots. We extend this idea from the single-robot case to coordinated fleets in a real warehouse, driving multiple robots with only a distributed CCTV network and edge compute. The system operates entirely in image space over an uncalibrated, pixel-wise topological camera graph, enabling wide-area operation with flexible camera placement. A hierarchical planner selects a camera sequence per robot and plans its image-space motion through each view, coordinating robots with a prioritised-then-joint strategy and treating overlapping camera regions as shared resources held by one robot at a time to prevent collisions and deadlocks. We validate the approach in a real warehouse with four robots and 30 cameras across six 27 m aisles, reporting mission times and coordination statistics. To our knowledge, this is the first field demonstration of multi-robot planning and coordination using only an external camera network and off-board compute, with robots carrying no task-specific navigation hardware.

</details>


### [15] [Re-imagining ISO 26262 in the Age of Autonomous Vehicles: Enhancing Controllability through Transferability and Predictability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07437)
*Chaitanya Shinde,Hadi Hajieghrary,Paul Schmitt,Adam Shoemaker,Bodo Seifert,Steve Kenner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对ISO 26262标准中适用于人类驾驶的Controllability概念在自动驾驶车辆（AV）中缺失驾驶员的问题，将其分解为Transferability（可转移性）和Predictability（可预测性）两个可审计的证据维度，并提供了形式化定义与量化框架，以扩展标准对L4/L5无人驾驶系统的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISO 26262的Controllability概念基于人类驾驶员范式，但在自动驾驶车辆（SAE L4/L5）中缺乏人类驾驶员，导致该标准无法直接适用于无人驾驶系统，需要重新定义可审计的替代维度。

Method: 将Controllability分解为Transferability（系统将控制权移交给后备安全机制的能力）和Predictability（外部智能体预测AV行为的能力），从人机交互原理出发形式化定义Predictability，并提供量化数学框架；引入设计能力与可实现能力之间的差距（designed-versus-achievable gap），以区分架构层面的后备声明与场景条件下的实际后备能力。

Result: 提出的度量指标与ISO 26262和ISO/PAS 21448（SOTIF）兼容，使后备和安全交互声明在ODD切片上可证伪、可追溯；该扩展保留了ISO 26262的结构，同时将其适用性延伸至SAE L4/L5无人驾驶系统。

Conclusion: Transferability和Predictability作为Controllability的补充维度而非替代，能够在不破坏现有标准框架的前提下，使ISO 26262有效覆盖无人驾驶系统的功能安全评估。

Abstract: The ISO 26262 standard defines functional safety for road vehicles through risk assessments based on Severity, Exposure, and Controllability, grounded in a human-driven vehicle paradigm. In the context of autonomous vehicles (AVs), the absence of a human driver necessitates revisiting these principles. This paper decomposes the Controllability placeholder into two auditable evidence dimensions of ISO 26262 by introducing two measurable sub-concepts: Transferability and Predictability. Transferability extends Controllability to capture AV systems' ability to hand off control to dedicated fallback safety mechanisms, while Predictability captures how easily external agents can anticipate AV behavior. Predictability is formally defined from human-robot interaction-inspired principles, and a mathematical framework is provided to quantify it. A designed-versus-achievable gap is introduced to distinguish architectural fallback claims from scene-conditioned achievable fallback capability. The proposed metrics align with ISO 26262 and ISO/PAS 21448 (SOTIF), rendering fallback and interaction claims falsifiable and traceable across ODD slices. These dimensions complement rather than replace existing standards, and the enhancements preserve the structure of ISO 26262 while extending its applicability to driverless automated systems operating at SAE Levels 4 and 5.

</details>


### [16] [A Multi-Operator Mixed-Reality Interface for Multi-Robot Control and Coordination: Co-Located and Private Workspace Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07013)
*Omotoye Shamsudeen Adekoya,Antonio Sgorbissa,Carmine Tommaso Recchiuto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合现实的HORUS接口，支持多操作员协作控制机器人团队。系统提供共置共享工作区和独立私有工作区两种模式，结合注册驱动场景构建、轻量级同步和每机器人控制租约机制。36名参与者的人机实验表明，两种模式的任务表现相当，但共置共享工作区显著提升了协作感知、共享理解和交接清晰度，是更受偏好的协作模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多操作员控制机器人团队时，不仅需要共享任务信息，还需要维护共享态势感知并防止冲突干预，现有系统在这方面存在不足。

Method: 基于先前HORUS接口扩展，提出混合现实界面；支持共置共享工作区和私有工作区两种互补模式；采用注册驱动场景构建、轻量级共享会话同步和每机器人控制租约机制实现协作监控、任务分配和遥操作。

Result: 36名参与者（18对）使用三台Nova Carter移动机器人在两个搜索环境中实验。两种模式客观任务表现相似，但共置共享工作区在感知协作、共享理解和交接清晰度上显著优于私有工作区，且是首选协作模式。

Conclusion: 物理共置混合现实工作空间能够改善操作员之间的协调，即使底层机器人控制工具保持不变，也有利于提升协作质量。

Abstract: Multi-operator control of robot teams requires not only access to the same mission information, but also mechanisms for maintaining shared awareness and preventing conflicting interventions. Building on our previous HORUS interface (Holistic Operational Reality for Unified Systems) we present a mixed-reality interface that extends single-operator multi-robot supervision to collaborative multi-operator use. The system supports two complementary modes: a co-located shared workspace, in which operators observe and manipulate the same mini-map in the same physical location, and a private-workspace mode, in which operators work on the same mission through independently placed local workspaces. The architecture combines registration-driven scene construction, lightweight shared-session synchronization, and per-robot control leases to support collaborative monitoring, tasking, and teleoperation while preventing conflicting commands. We evaluated the approach in a human-subject study with 36 participants (18 pairs) controlling three Nova Carter mobile robots in two search environments. The performance of the objective task was comparable across the two modes, indicating that both modes supported effective mission execution. However, the co-located shared workspace significantly improved perceived collaboration, shared understanding, and handoff clarity, and was the preferred collaborative mode. These results indicate that physically co-locating the MR workspace improves how operators coordinate even when the underlying robot-control tools remain unchanged.

</details>


### [17] [Three-dimensional hydro-cluttered locomotion by an undulatory robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06829)
*Tianyu Wang,Matthew Fernandez,Galen Tunnicliffe,Nikolas Cornell,Justin Duong,Donoven Dortilus,Zhaochen J. Xu,Patricia Meza,Sean Lublinsky,Darsh Parikh,Jianfeng Lin,Emily Grace,Daniel I. Goldman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AquaMILR的细长无肢机器人，通过可编程身体柔顺性、深度调节和惯性诱导滚动等机制，实现了在充满障碍物的水下环境（如水下 mangrove 根部区域）中的高效三维运动，将环境复杂性从挑战转化为运动资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境中存在大量刚性或柔性障碍物（"水杂乱"环境），传统水下机器人在此类场景中难以避免与障碍物接触，而现有模型难以模拟这种接触主导的运动模式，亟需能利用而非避免接触的机器人设计原理。

Method: 设计了AquaMILR机器人，融合双侧缆绳驱动、可编程身体柔顺性、分布式深度调节、耐腐蚀外壳及机载电源。通过系统机器人物理实验和野外红树林测试，研究身体柔顺性编程、深度调节和惯性滚动在不同杂乱约束强度下的运动表现。

Result: 可编程身体柔顺性能够调节身体变形并将身体-环境交互转化为快速稳健的前进运动；深度调节实现三维空间可达性，使机器人能够绕过障碍物并恢复前进；惯性诱导滚动作为自发性恢复机制，在卡滞场景中帮助机器人解脱。野外红树林测试验证了这些原理在实际操作中的有效性。

Conclusion: 建立了水杂乱环境下的运动原理和设计范式，表明水下机器人可以将环境复杂性利用为运动资源，而非将其视为障碍。

Abstract: Aquatic robots have expanded human access to underwater environments, yet many underwater spaces contain obstacles that can disrupt open-water locomotion. In "hydro-cluttered" environments, water is interspersed with rigid and flexible clutter, making body-obstacle contact unavoidable. Operating in these spaces requires robots that can regulate and exploit contact, but this regime remains difficult to model or simulate. Building on recent advances in mechanical intelligence in terradynamically capable limbless robotics, we develop principles for 3D aquatic locomotion using AquaMILR, an elongate limbless robot that combines bilateral cable-driven actuation, programmable body compliance, distributed depth regulation, corrosion-resistant enclosures, and onboard power and electronics for untethered field operation. Systematic robophysical experiments reveal that programmable body compliance regulates body deformation and converts body-environment interactions into fast, robust, forward progression across increasing hydro-clutter constraint strength. Depth regulation provides three-dimensional access, allowing the robot to bypass clutter, recover from obstruction, and continue through otherwise inaccessible routes. In potential jamming scenarios, emergent inertia-induced rolling acts as a spontaneous recovery mechanism, freeing the robot from clutter that would otherwise lead to failure and allowing locomotion to continue without additional control. Tests of the robot in an aquatic mangrove field demonstrate that these principles transfer to practical operation, enabling navigation and onboard visual inspection of inaccessible root zones. These results establish principles for hydro-cluttered locomotion and a design paradigm in which aquatic robots exploit environmental complexity as a locomotor resource.

</details>


### [18] [STRIPS-WM: Learning Grounded Propositional STRIPS-style World Models from Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06832)
*Abhiroop Ajith,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出STRIPS-WM框架，从图像转换中学习基于图像的STRIPS风格世界模型，将高维视觉观察抽象为符号化动作模型（包含稀疏前提条件和增删效果），使机器人能够直接从起始和目标图像执行经典规划，在视觉重排任务中优于视觉 rollout、潜在图搜索和潜在符号基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在进行长时域视觉操作时，成功规划依赖于与动作相关的事实（当前可执行什么动作、动作后发生什么变化），但高维图像中包含大量无关视觉细节。现有方法难以从原始视觉经验中获取符号化操作表示（前提条件和效果），因此需要一种能从视觉转换中自动学习抽象符号化世界模型的方法。

Method: STRIPS-WM分三步：(1) 从图像转换数据中诱导出有限抽象转换图；(2) 学习潜在二元谓词和每个动作标签对应的接地命题算子，形成具有稀疏前提条件和增删效果的符号化动作模型；(3) 将学习到的谓词蒸馏到视觉编码器中，实现直接从新起始和目标图像进行经典规划。

Result: 在视觉重排任务上的实验表明，STRIPS-WM在图像到规划的成功率上优于视觉 rollout、潜在图搜索和潜在符号基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了从视觉转换中学习结构化符号化世界模型的可行性，为机器人长时域视觉操作规划提供了一种有效且可泛化的框架。

Abstract: Robots performing long-horizon visual manipulation observe high-dimensional images, but successful plans depend on action-relevant facts: what can be done now and what changes afterward. A useful planning representation should discard irrelevant visual details while preserving action applicability and effects. Classical task planners exploit this structure through symbolic operators with preconditions and effects, but obtaining such representations from raw visual experience remains challenging. We study a visual task-planning setting in which a robot receives only image transitions: the current image, executed high-level action, and the resulting image. At test time, given a start image and a goal image, the robot must produce a sequence of high-level actions that reaches the goal. To address this problem, we introduce STRIPS-WM, a framework for learning image-grounded STRIPS-style world models directly from visual transitions. STRIPS-WM first induces a finite abstract transition graph from images, then learns latent binary predicates and one grounded propositional operator per action label. The learned operators form a symbolic action model with sparse preconditions and add/delete effects. Finally, the learned predicates are distilled into a visual encoder, enabling classical planning directly from novel start and goal images. Experiments on visual rearrangement tasks show that STRIPS-WM improves image-to-plan success over the tested visual rollout, latent graph-search and latent-symbolic baselines.

</details>


### [19] [Think Like a Pilot: Fine-Grained Long-Horizon UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06836)
*Xiangyi Zheng,Xiangyu Wang,Qinan Liao,Zimu Tang,Yue Liao,Dongyue Lyu,Guodong Wang,Junjie Liu,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FLIGHT基准和FLIGHT VLA异步架构，解决无人机在细粒度长程指令导航中任务推理与高频连续控制难以兼顾的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN基准通常使用离散或粗粒度动作，而无人机VLA任务聚焦于短时原子机动，缺乏结合多阶段指令与密集6-DoF轨迹的长程混合导航与推理任务设定。

Method: FLIGHT基准包含细粒度VLN和长程Flow两个数据集拆分；FLIGHT VLA采用异步架构，用低频Streaming Pilot VLM进行任务状态推理，高频扩散动作模型实现连续控制，并由显式的Pilot Reasoning文本监督。

Result: 在闭环评估中，FLIGHT VLA在所有FLIGHT基准上一致超越代表性VLN和VLA基线，在多阶段任务完成率、子目标遵循度和终端控制上表现更优；其训练的Streaming Pilot Reasoning VLM也提升了无人机视频推理能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的异步解耦架构能有效兼顾任务推理与实时控制，验证了设计的有效性。

Abstract: Language-guided UAV agents must execute long-horizon semantic instructions while producing smooth, physically feasible continuous flight commands, yet existing Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) benchmarks typically use discrete or coarse actions and existing UAV Vision-Language-Action (VLA) tasks focus on short, atomic maneuvers. To address this gap in UAV task settings, we introduce \textbf{FLIGHT}, a \textbf{F}ine-grained \textbf{L}ong-horizon \textbf{I}nstruction-\textbf{G}uided benchmark for \textbf{H}ybrid UAV navigation and reasoning \textbf{T}asks, which combines multi-stage instructions with dense 6-DoF trajectory annotations across two dataset splits: Fine-grained VLN and Long-horizon Flow. To endow the UAV agent with the capability of real-time in-flight reasoning over task execution status and mission planning, while simultaneously accommodating high-frequency, real-time precise control, we further propose \textbf{FLIGHT VLA}, an asynchronous architecture that decouples a low-frequency Streaming Pilot Vision-Language Model (VLM) for task-state reasoning from a high-frequency diffusion action model for continuous control, supervised by explicit \textbf{Pilot Reasoning} texts that summarize the current flight state and anticipate the next subgoal. In closed-loop evaluation, FLIGHT VLA consistently surpasses representative VLN and VLA baselines on our FLIGHT benchmarks, achieving stronger multi-stage completion, subgoal adherence, and terminal control. Its trained Streaming Pilot Reasoning VLM further improves UAV video reasoning, validating the effectiveness of our design.

</details>


### [20] [What Is My Robot Thinking? Design Considerations for Transparent and Trustworthy Shared Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06870)
*Atharv Belsare,Zohre Karimi,Connor Mattson,Rushiil Nakka,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究了共享自主系统中界面透明度（反馈模态和信息丰富度）如何影响人机协作与信任，发现提供反馈能显著改善意图对齐并减少干预，视觉优于听觉，信息稀疏/丰富偏好取决于任务复杂度，完全披露信念分布并无一致收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享自主系统中机器人行为依赖内部意图推断，但推断目标与用户目标之间的不匹配会损害协作与信任，因此需要探索界面透明度设计如何缓解这一问题。

Method: 在基于视觉的共享自主系统中，通过N=25参与者的用户研究，操纵反馈模态（视觉vs听觉）和信息丰富度（稀疏vs丰富），在两个辅助操作任务上评估协作与信任。

Result: 提供反馈显著改善意图对齐、减少纠正性干预；参与者偏好视觉反馈；信息丰富度偏好取决于任务复杂度；完全披露信念分布并未持续改善对齐或信任。

Conclusion: 有效透明度主要通过目标可理解性增强协作，而信任取决于任务适恰的信息暴露而非最大化披露，并据此提出了设计指南。

Abstract: Assistive robots operating under shared autonomy must balance user control with autonomous assistance. Because robot actions depend on internal intent inference that is not directly observable, mismatches between inferred and intended goals can undermine coordination and trust. We investigate how interface-level transparency, including feedback modality (visual vs. auditory) and information richness (sparse vs. rich), shapes interaction in a vision-based shared autonomy system. In a user study with N=25 participants across two assistive manipulation tasks, we evaluate how these designs influence coordination and trust. Providing feedback significantly improves intent alignment and reduces corrective intervention, indicating that making the inferred goal legible accelerates convergence in shared control. Participants preferred visual over auditory feedback, while preferences for sparse versus rich information depended on task complexity. We also found that revealing the full belief distribution did not consistently improve alignment or trust. Together, these findings indicate that effective transparency enhances coordination primarily through goal legibility, while trust depends on task-appropriate information exposure rather than maximal disclosure. Based on these results, we outline guidelines for designing transparent shared autonomy systems.

</details>


### [21] [Neuro-Symbolic Learning for Long-Horizon Task Planning Under Complex Logical Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06877)
*Qiwei Du,Zitong Zhan,Shaoshu Su,Bowen Li,Yi Du,Zhipeng Zhao,Taimeng Fu,Sebastian Scherer,Jiaoyang Li,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对任务规划中神经符号方法因离线监督导致的训练-测试不匹配（暴露偏差）问题，提出将物体重要性学习建模为基于命令学习的双层优化问题，并在下层规划中引入并行修复、重启、回滚（3R）策略以提供稳定自适应反馈，在多个基准和实物机器人上实现了规划失败率降低80.04%、时间减少57.14%的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号方法在物体重要性学习时依赖离线监督，导致训练时模型在完整搜索空间上学习，而部署时规划器在模型自身预测的不完美剪枝空间上运行，产生暴露偏差，严重制约规划效率与成功率的提升。

Method: 将物体重要性学习形式化为基于命令学习的双层优化问题：上层优化神经评分器，下层在评分剪枝后的符号规划空间中求解。为稳定下层规划对上层训练的反向传播，引入3R策略（并行修复、重启、回滚）以提供可靠的自适应反馈。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的基准任务上实现SOTA性能：失败率降低80.04%，规划时间减少57.14%；并在四足移动机械臂平台（仿真与实物）上验证了框架的部署可行性。

Conclusion: 通过双层优化范式配合3R恢复策略，有效解决了神经符号规划的训练-测试不匹配问题，为高效且可部署的神经符号任务规划提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Task planning often suffers from severe efficiency bottlenecks when robots must reason over long-horizon action sequences under complex logical constraints, including object affordances, spatial relationships, and sequential action dependencies. Recent neuro-symbolic methods improve planning efficiency by learning object-importance scores to prune task-irrelevant objects, but they typically rely on fixed offline supervision generated from full search spaces. This creates a train-test mismatch: at deployment, the planner operates in pruned search spaces induced by the model's own imperfect predictions, leading to exposure bias and degraded planning performance. To address this challenge, we formulate object-importance learning for task planning as an imperative learning-based bilevel optimization problem. The upper level optimizes a neural scorer, while the lower level solves a symbolic planning problem in the score-pruned search space. To stabilize this learning process, we introduce a 3R strategy into the lower-level planning, using parallel Repair, Restart, and Rollback recovery to provide reliable and adaptive feedback for upper-level learning. Experiments on three challenging benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, including an 80.04% reduction in failure rate and a 57.14% reduction in planning time. We further validate the framework on a quadruped-based mobile manipulator in simulation and the real world, demonstrating its potential for efficient and deployable neuro-symbolic task planning.

</details>


### [22] [A Cross-view Fusion Framework for Robust 6-DoF Grasp Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06878)
*Kangjian Zhu,Haobo Jiang,Jianjun Qian,Jin Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨视图融合框架，通过引入辅助视图和后期融合策略，结合自监督对比学习和跨视图对齐圆柱集成模块，显著提升了角落视角下6-DoF抓取姿态估计的鲁棒性，在GraspNet-1Billion基准和实际应用中取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6-DoF抓取姿态估计方法在角落视角下存在严重的遮挡问题，而传统的多视图重建方法耗时且与抓取任务无关，亟需一种高效且鲁棒的跨视图融合方案来解决角落视角的遮挡挑战。

Method: 提出跨视图融合框架，包含三个核心创新：1）自监督对比学习策略，通过跨视图点对匹配/非匹配约束来正则化点云特征的空间一致性和方向区分性；2）跨视图对齐圆柱集成模块，先按相似度对齐跨视图点与特征以增强噪声鲁棒性，再将其注册到圆柱坐标系以强调旋转对称几何，最后交替使用局部自注意力和种子交叉注意力实现视图内交互和跨视图交互；3）采用后期融合策略避免耗时的多视图重建。

Result: 在GraspNet-1Billion基准测试和真实世界应用中均取得了强性能表现，验证了框架的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 跨视图融合能够有效缓解角落视角的遮挡问题，自监督对比学习策略和圆柱集成模块分别提升了特征一致性和抓取相关几何的表示能力，为6-DoF抓取姿态估计提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a cross-view fusion framework that enhances the robustness of 6-DoF grasp pose estimation in corner views. Our framework alleviates occlusion by incorporating an auxiliary view and avoids the time-consuming, task-agnostic multi-view reconstruction through a post-fusion strategy. To enhance cross-view fusion, we propose a self-supervised contrastive learning strategy that leverages cross-view associations to regularize point cloud features. In brief, a cross-view point pair is considered a match if the two points correspond to the same 3D location, and a non-match if they represent distinct grasp directions. The learning strategy significantly enhances the spatial consistency and direction distinctiveness of point features, thereby facilitating cross-view fusion and improving estimation robustness. Furthermore, we propose a cross-view-aligned cylinder integration module to fuse grasp-relevant geometry into a comprehensive representation. Specifically, the module first aligns the cross-view points and features according to their similarity to enhance the robustness against noise. Subsequently, these points are registered into the cylindrical coordinate frame, emphasizing the rotation-symmetric geometry which is important for grasping. Finally, local self-attention and seed cross-attention layers are alternately employed, respectively enabling interactions within single views and across views, which supports fine-grained representation of grasp-relevant geometry. Our framework achieves strong performance on the GraspNet-1Billion benchmark and in real-world applications. Code is available at https://github.com/KJZhuAutomatic/Cross-view-Grasp.

</details>


### [23] [ActionMap: Robot Policy Learning via Voxel Action Heatmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06904)
*Pei Yang,Hai Ci,Yanzhe Chen,Qi Lv,Han Cai,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ActionMap，一种基于体素热图的动作解码头，可替代现有VLA模型中的单点预测器，通过利用动作空间的几何结构，在LIBERO和真实机器人操作任务上显著超越原有方法，且更数据高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的动作解码器几乎都是单点预测器（自回归token箱、L1回归或流匹配去噪），将动作空间视为无结构空间，未能利用邻近动作之间的几何邻近性进行训练。

Method: 提出ActionMap，一种体素热图动作头，可插入现有VLA替换原生动作解码器。对于每个新动作，该头预测动作空间上的体素热图，每个体素直接存储对应动作的概率。

Result: 在LIBERO仿真和真实Franka机器人操作任务上，ActionMap在匹配训练步数下超越两种架构不同的主干网络（如相比OpenVLA-OFT的L1回归头，在LIBERO四套件平均提升8.2%），收敛速度相当或更快，且在低训练数据量下显著更数据高效。

Conclusion: 跨主干网络的一致性表明动作表示是VLA性能的真正杠杆，独立于进一步的主干网络或训练配方扩展。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced rapidly across backbones, training recipes, and data scale, yet the action decoder, which converts the backbone's hidden state into a continuous control signal, has barely changed and remains a single-point predictor across the majority of current VLAs. Whether implemented via autoregressive token bins, L1 regression, or flow-matching denoising, the resulting decoder treats the action space as unstructured, leaving the geometric proximity of neighboring actions unexploited during training. To advance this, we introduce ActionMap, a voxel heatmap action head that drops into an existing VLA in place of its native action decoder. For each new action, the head predicts a voxel heatmap over the action space, where each voxel directly stores the probability of the corresponding action. Across LIBERO simulation and real-world Franka manipulation, our heatmap head surpasses two architecturally distinct backbones at matched training steps (e.g., +8.2% over OpenVLA-OFT's L1 regression head on the LIBERO four-suite average), converges at comparable or faster rates on both backbones, and remains markedly more data-efficient at low training data. The cross-backbone consistency indicates that action representation is a real lever for VLA performance, distinct from further backbone or recipe scaling. Project Page: https://github.com/showlab/ActionMap.

</details>


### [24] [T-GMP: Terrain-conditioned Generative Motion Priors for Versatile and Natural Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06944)
*Junhong Guo,Hao Hu,Chen Chen,Haoxuan Han,Linao Gong,Xin Yang,Zhicheng He,Yao Su,Fenghua He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出地形条件生成运动先验（T-GMP），通过条件变分自编码器从少量专家状态-地形演示中学习地形条件潜在运动流形，结合对抗学习和立足点惩罚，实现适应多种地形的类人自然运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法依赖固定运动先验，限制了人形机器人运动策略对多变环境的适应性；同时实现拟人自然性和鲁棒地形穿越仍是基础挑战。

Method: 1. 构建T-GMP模块：使用CVAE从少量专家演示中学习地形条件潜在运动流形；2. 集成到对抗学习管线，提出Foothold Penalty（立足点惩罚）；3. 判别器根据局部地形特征动态调节自然性约束。

Result: 在穿越成功率、运动平滑度上优于现有基线方法，同时保持仿生自然和物理协调的运动。

Conclusion: T-GMP有效解决了人形机器人运动策略的适应性问题，实现了风格平滑转换和统一策略对地形变化的适应。

Abstract: Achieving both anthropomorphic naturalness and robust terrain traversal remains a fundamental challenge in humanoid locomotion. Existing Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches typically rely on fixed motion priors, limiting their adaptability to varying environments. We propose Terrain-conditioned Generative Motion Priors (T-GMP), a module that captures a terrain-conditioned latent motion manifold from a few expert state-terrain demonstrations using a Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE). The learned priors enable smooth style transitions, facilitating a unified policy that adapts to terrain variations. We integrate T-GMP into an adversarial learning pipeline with our proposed Foothold Penalty, where a discriminator dynamically modulates naturalness constraints conditioned on local terrain features, guiding the generation of versatile and human-like motions. Experimental results demonstrate that our method outperforms existing baselines in traversal success rate and motion smoothness, while preserving biomimetically natural and physically coordinated motions.

</details>

*Yu Guan,Zekun Qi,Chenghuai Lin,Xuchuan Chen,Dairu Liu,Wenyao Zhang,Jilong Wang,Xinqiang Yu,He Wang,Li Yi*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的运动数据能在训练初期引导跟踪策略走向更优的优化轨迹，因此需要系统性研究数据质量对物理模拟中人形运动跟踪的影响。

Result: 使用AMASS不到3%的数据训练即可达到优于全量数据集训练的跟踪性能；数据清洗后的网络数据同样有效。

Abstract: We argue that high-quality motion data can steer tracking policies toward better optimization trajectories early in training. In this work, we introduce LIMMT (Less Is More for Motion Tracking). To our knowledge, this is the first data-centric study for physics-based humanoid motion tracking. We go beyond simply removing low-quality and erroneous clips, but define motion data quality through three dimensions: physics feasibility, diversity, and complexity. We show that even training with under 3% of AMASS yields better tracking performance than training with the full dataset. We further conduct data cleaning on the estimated web-sourced mocap data. Extensive experiments and analyses validate the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为直肠指检训练用的传感器化前列腺体模设计有效的力传感布局方案，解决在有限的感知配置下如何提高力可观测性的问题。

Abstract: This work presents a compliance-based sensor placement method for force sensing on a sensorized prostate phantom designed for Digital Rectal Examination training. The phantom combines three internal pneumatic chambers, used as intrinsic pressure sensors, with ten surface displacement markers. A finite-element simulation dataset is generated by applying external forces at sampled surface locations, from which a compliance matrix relating force inputs to pressure and displacement responses is constructed. Based on this matrix, we propose a weighted greedy selection strategy that maximizes local force reconstructability while prioritizing the clinically relevant posterior contact region and avoiding marker placement directly within the Region of Interest. Compared with a global QR-based placement strategy, the proposed method increases the mean reconstructability score in the target region by 22.5%. These results suggest that region-aware sparse sensor placement can improve force observability in soft robotic medical phantoms while maintaining a limited and practical sensing configuration.

</details>


### [27] [Mission-Level Runtime Assurance Framework for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06996)
*Chieh Tsai,Salim Hariri*
