### [47] [Enhancing Malware Detection with Generative AI: Using Variational Autoencoders to Boost Machine Learning Classifiers' Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06501)
*Mohammad Alharbi,Jeremy Straub*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出使用变分自编码器（VAE）生成合成恶意软件样本，以解决数据稀缺问题，并通过随机森林、XGBoost和序列模型等分类器验证，证明数据增强后分类器在准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件的不断演进对网络安全构成严重威胁，而新的或较少见的恶意软件类型存在数据稀缺问题，限制了检测模型的训练效果和泛化能力。

Method: 使用变分自编码器（VAE）生成高质量、多样化的合成恶意软件样本，将增强后的数据集分别用于训练随机森林、XGBoost和序列模型（Sequential Model）三种机器学习分类器，并与仅使用原始数据训练的分类器性能进行对比。

Result: 采用VAE增强数据集训练的分类器在准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数上均显著优于仅使用原始数据训练的分类器，检测各类恶意软件的性能均有所提升。

Conclusion: 生成式AI（特别是VAE）可有效缓解恶意软件数据稀缺问题，提升检测模型的鲁棒性和适应性，为构建更具弹性的恶意软件检测系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: The advancement of malware poses obstacles for cybersecurity, necessitating the development of advanced detection techniques. This paper proposes an approach to enhance malware detection through the use of a generative artificial intelligence model. Specifically, variational autoencoders (VAEs) are used with the random forest, XGBoost and sequential model machine learning classifiers. Generated synthetic malware samples are used to address the critical issue of data scarcity for new or less common malware types. This approach can be used to augment datasets to improve classifier robustness.
  The proposed methodology uses VAEs to create high-quality diverse synthetic datasets that closely mimic real-world malware data. The effectiveness of these augmented datasets is evaluated by comparing the performance of the machine learning classifiers when they are trained with the original data and when they are trained with the synthetic data-augmented datasets. The results demonstrate a notable improvement in the accuracy, precision, recall and F1-scores of the classifiers, when they are trained using the augmented datasets. The enhanced performance for detecting various malware classes shows the potential of this approach to facilitate adaptation to evolving malware threats effectively. This work demonstrates the utility of generative AI for cybersecurity. It also provides a foundation for future research aimed at developing more resilient and adaptive malware detection systems.

</details>


### [48] [Subtle Injection for Ground-truth Inference of LLM Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06502)
*Abraham Itzhak Weinberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SIGIL 提出了一种通过在受保护文本中注入不可察觉的“金丝雀序列”来检测 LLM 是否使用了该文本进行训练的方法，基于 Neyman-Pearson 假设检验框架实现正式的错误率控制，在 36,000 次实验中达到 0.892 的整体 AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 在未授权情况下使用抓取的网络数据进行训练，内容所有者需要一种取证方法来证明其文档是否被纳入模型训练集。

Method: 提出 SIGIL 框架，定义五种金丝雀策略（词汇罕见、词汇短语、句法、语义、代码模式），基于 Neyman-Pearson 假设检验框架设计成员推理分数（MIS），并通过模拟器校准参数进行大规模实验。

Result: 整体 AUC 0.892，在 0.1% 到 10% 注入率下 AUC 从 0.831 升至 0.947；检测率 33%-78%；代码模式 AUC 最高（0.903），句法结构最低（0.875）；所有四个实验因素对 MIS 均有显著独立影响（p<0.001）；即使 100% 释义改写仍保持 AUC>0.86。

Conclusion: SIGIL 能够有效且鲁棒地检测 LLM 训练数据中的受保护内容，即使在表面改写后仍可通过语义泄漏保持较高检测性能。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trained on scraped web corpora without authorisation, content owners require forensic methods to prove that their documents were included in a model's training set. We propose \textbf{SIGIL} (\textbf{S}ubtle \textbf{I}njection for \textbf{G}round-truth \textbf{I}nference of \textbf{L}LM training data), a framework that embeds imperceptible \emph{canary sequences} into protected text and code such that any LLM trained on those documents exhibits statistically detectable behavioural signatures when probed with targeted queries.
  SIGIL defines five canary strategies -- lexical-rare, lexical-phrase, syntactic, semantic, and code-pattern -- and a \emph{Membership Inference Score} (MIS) grounded in the Neyman-Pearson hypothesis testing framework with formal false-positive rate (FPR) control. Simulator parameters are calibrated against the empirical membership inference literature, yielding realistic heterogeneous results across $36{,}000$ trials: overall AUC $= 0.892$, rising from $0.831$ at $0.1\%$ injection to $0.947$ at $10\%$. Detection rates range from $33\%$ to $78\%$ across model-size and injection-rate conditions. Code Pattern canaries achieve the highest AUC ($0.903$, Cohen's $d = 1.84$); Syntactic Structure the lowest ($0.875$, $d = 1.63$). All four experimental factors -- injection rate, model size, canary strategy, and mixture ratio -- have significant independent effects on MIS ($p < 0.001$). SIGIL maintains AUC $> 0.86$ even under $100\%$ paraphrasing ($\text{AUC} = 0.864$), confirming robustness through semantic leakage that survives surface-level rewriting.

</details>


### [49] [MalTree: Tracing Malware Evolution from Embeddings at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06570)
*Akash Amalan,Georgios Smaragdakis,Tom J. Viering*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出MalTree框架，将生物信息学中的系统发育技术（UPGMA和Neighbor-Joining）应用于恶意软件进化建模，通过结构、行为和图像特征自动推断恶意软件家族间的进化关系，达到87%的时间一致性，并发现不同家族突变速率差异可达10倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统恶意软件检测主要依赖监督学习，模型对已知样本有效但无法应对不断演化的威胁。逆向工程确定恶意软件进化关系耗时数月甚至数年。需要一种自动化、可扩展的方法来建模恶意软件的进化关系，从而支持主动防御。

Method: 借鉴生物信息学的系统发育技术（UPGMA和Neighbor-Joining），利用结构、行为和图像三种特征对恶意软件进行表示，通过VirusTotal时间戳进行时间一致性验证，评估推断的进化树是否反映真实的出现时间顺序。

Result: MalTree达到了87%的时间一致性，即推断的进化关系与真实出现时间线高度吻合。发现某些恶意软件家族的突变速度比其他家族快10倍以上。案例研究（如Mirai僵尸网络）证实推断的进化关系与已知威胁情报一致。

Conclusion: 该框架为恶意软件分析从逐样本分类转向谱系感知的进化建模奠定了基础，提示防御策略应根据家族特定的进化节奏进行定制。

Abstract: Malware detection remains largely reactive: machine learning models trained on known samples degrade as threats evolve. Understanding evolutionary relationships among malware families can inform proactive defense, but traditional reverse engineering can take months to years to uncover such lineage relationships. We propose MalTree, a framework that applies bioinformatics inspired phylogenetic techniques (UPGMA and Neighbor-Joining) at scale to model malware evolution automatically using structural, behavioral, and image-based features. We introduce temporal validation using VirusTotal timestamps to assess whether inferred trees reflect actual evolutionary order. MalTree achieves 87% temporal consistency, indicating that inferred evolutionary relationships closely align with real-world emergence timelines. Our analysis shows that some families mutate over 10 times faster than others, suggesting that detection strategies should be tailored to family-specific evolutionary tempos. Case studies, including the Mirai botnet, confirm that inferred relationships from our phylogenetic tree align with documented threat intelligence. Our framework provides a foundation for shifting malware analysis from sample-by-sample classification toward lineage-aware evolutionary modeling.

</details>


### [50] [AgileOS: A GPU Operating System Layer for Protected CUDA Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06697)
*Zhuoping Yang,Yiyu Shi,Alex Jones,Peipei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgileOS 是一个 GPU 操作系统层，通过在库边界虚拟化 CUDA，为 GPU 服务提供类似操作系统的保护机制，防止不信任的应用内核直接访问服务元数据、设备队列、MMIO 区域和库内部状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 GPU 应用越来越多地与存储系统、网络设备、厂商库和 GPU 常驻服务交互，而非仅执行隔离的计算内核。然而，当前的 CUDA 编程模型默认将 CUDA 上下文、设备指针、运行时句柄等直接暴露给不信任的应用内核，缺乏操作系统级的保护机制。

Method: AgileOS 在 CUDA 库边界进行虚拟化：应用链接客户端侧 CUDA Runtime/Driver 及库 shim，而可信运行时工作线程持有真实 CUDA 上下文并仲裁受支持的操作。通过指针验证和基于 PTX 注入的内存访问守卫，分离用户分配与受保护模块/MMIO 范围。

Result: 原型实现了客户端拦截器、工作线程 CUDA 处理器、虚拟化 CUDA 对象表、受保护 AgileOS 模块、GPU 内存管理器（分离用户分配与受保护模块/MMIO 范围）、选定的可信库适配器以及 PTX 级内核内存守卫。

Conclusion: AgileOS 提供了一种模块化且灵活的设计，支持多种受保护服务及 cuFFT、PyTorch 等现有库，为 GPU 服务提供操作系统级的隔离保护。

Abstract: Modern GPU applications increasingly interact with storage systems, network devices, vendor libraries, and GPU-resident services rather than executing only isolated compute kernels. This shift creates a need for operating-system-like protection around GPU services, where service metadata, device queues, memory-mapped I/O regions, and library-internal state should not be directly exposed to untrusted application kernels. However, today's CUDA programming model, by default, still gives each application direct ownership of its CUDA context, device pointers, runtime handles, module loading path, and kernel launches, leaving protected GPU services to build their own ad hoc interfaces and isolation mechanisms.
  This paper presents the initial design and prototype scope of AgileOS, a GPU operating-system layer for protected CUDA services. AgileOS virtualizes CUDA at the library boundary: applications link against client-side CUDA Runtime, Driver, and selected library shims, while a trusted runtime worker owns the real CUDA context and mediates supported operations. To protect service state and module interfaces, AgileOS also defines a GPU memory-management model that separates user allocations from protected module/MMIO ranges, using pointer validation and memory access guards via PTX injection. AgileOS is modularized and flexible, supporting a range of protected services and existing libraries such as cuFFT and PyTorch. The prototype includes client-side interceptors, worker-side CUDA handlers, virtualized CUDA object tables, protected AgileOS modules, a GPU memory manager that separates user allocations from protected module/MMIO ranges, selected trusted library adapters, and the PTX-level kernel memory guard.

</details>


### [51] [The Custody Envelope Threshold: Authority-Scaled Admission of External Artifacts in Institutional Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06767)
*Amadeus Brandes*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出监护权信封阈值（Custody Envelope Threshold），一个基于权限缩放的工件准入模型，用于解决现代基础设施中外部维护工件的治理难题，并通过四条件序数量表进行可操作化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基础设施依赖大量外部维护的工件（如包依赖、CI/CD动作、容器镜像等），这些工件易于获取但难以被机构正式接纳、治理和撤销，存在安全与治理鸿沟。

Method: 提出监护权信封阈值概念，建立基于对象身份、入口路径和撤销能力的四条件序数量表，结合引用监控推理、最小权限原则和交易成本经济学进行分析。

Result: 将框架应用于包依赖、GitHub Actions、容器镜像、Terraform提供者与模块、开发者扩展和开放模型工件，并以MCP服务器作为留出验证集。设计了验证方案、确定性预测函数和OSF复制包。

Conclusion: 高审查机构会趋向于对高权限工件实施更强的监护权封闭，该框架可系统评估工件的准入策略。

Abstract: Modern infrastructure depends on externally maintained artifacts such as package-registry dependencies, CI/CD actions, container images, Terraform providers and modules, developer extensions, model artifacts, and AI tool servers. These artifacts are easy to fetch but difficult for institutions to admit, govern, and revoke. This paper proposes the Custody Envelope Threshold, an authority-scaled model of artifact admission. It argues that direct institutional admission is defensible only when object identity, ingress path, and revocation capacity are sufficiently closed relative to the execution authority delegated to the artifact. When this threshold is not met, institutions tend to proxy, policy-mediate, vendor-mediate, internalize, quarantine, or reject the artifact. The framework is operationalized as a four-condition ordinal instrument and connected to reference-monitor reasoning, least privilege, and transaction cost economics. It is applied to package dependencies, GitHub Actions, container images, Terraform providers and modules, developer extensions, and open model artifacts, with Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers treated as held-out evidence. The paper also specifies a validation design, deterministic prediction function, and OSF replication package for testing whether high-scrutiny institutions converge toward stronger custody closure for high-authority artifacts.

</details>


### [52] [What Your Posts Reveal: A Benchmark and Agentic Framework for User-Level Privacy Leakage on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06784)
*Zifan Peng,Yini Huang,Aiwen Lu,Qiming Ye,Peixian Zhang,Jingyi Zheng,Yule Liu,Xuechao Wang,Xinlei He,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SopriBench是一个多模态隐私泄露基准，包含50个用户资料和1,569张图片；提出隐私暴露评分(PES)作为评估指标；并引入Argus框架，通过溯因推理实现跨帖子累积泄露推理，相比最强基线提升25% PES。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏统一的多模态用户级隐私泄露基准，且评估指标仅限于二元准确率，无法捕捉暴露严重程度。

Method: 构建SopriBench基准（基于Rednote和Instagram的50用户/1,569图片的合成数据），提出PES指标（按上下文敏感性加权粒度的暴露评分），并设计Argus框架（基于溯因推理的免训练智能体，通过形成假设、验证证据、聚合跨帖子线索来推断隐私）。

Result: Argus达到0.55 PES，比最强基线提升25%，在跨帖子泄露推理上提升最大。

Conclusion: SopriBench和PES为多模态隐私泄露评估提供了统一基准和更精细的度量；Argus证明了免训练溯因推理框架在累积隐私泄露推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Public social media posts can reveal private information through weak cues scattered across text, images, or metadata. Such leakage is often cumulative and cross-post: cues that appear harmless in isolation may jointly expose a user's home, workplace, or routine. However, current research lacks a unified benchmark for user-level multimodal privacy leakage and an evaluation metric that captures exposure severity beyond binary accuracy.
  To address these gaps, we propose SopriBench, a synthetic benchmark guided by leakage patterns abstracted from a private reference corpus of Rednote and Instagram accounts, covering 50 user profiles and 1,569 images with attributes, contextual sensitivity, granularity, leakage type, inference difficulty, and supporting evidence. We further introduce the Privacy Exposure Score (PES), which weights value granularity by contextual sensitivity. Inspired by abductive reasoning, we introduce Argus, a training-free agentic framework for cumulative leakage inference. Argus forms hypotheses from accumulated evidence, verifies supporting evidence, and aggregates cross-post cues into privacy profiles, achieving 0.55 PES, a 25% improvement over the strongest baseline, with the largest gain on cross-post leakage.

</details>


### [53] [AMD-FCG: An Enhanced Function Call Graph Dataset with Integrated Topological Features for Malware Detection and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06815)
*Parthajit Borah,Sakshi Singh,D. K. Bhattacharyya,J. K. Kalita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了AMD-FCG，一个增强版的函数调用图数据集，通过整合恶意软件的拓扑特征来提升恶意软件检测效率，无需动态分析和大量处理即可用于开发更高效的检测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件结构复杂、行为多样，现有检测方法需要可靠且自适应的解决方案，而基于函数调用图的模式分析需要大规模数据集来覆盖多种恶意软件家族。

Method: 构建增强版的函数调用图数据集AMD-FCG，整合恶意软件的拓扑特征，包含多种恶意软件和良性应用样本，无需动态分析和大量预处理。

Result: AMD-FCG数据集能够简化检测流程，提高检测效率和准确性，为网络安全专业人员提供更高效的工作流。

Conclusion: AMD-FCG可用于开发和部署更高效、创新的恶意软件检测系统，消除对动态分析和大量处理的需求。

Abstract: As malware illustrates a complex structure and behavior, detection of these has been a significant challenge in the domain of cybersecurity along with related services in daily life. So, it becomes crucial to have a reliable and adaptive solution to address the issue. Among the several detection methods developed over the years, one of the most reliable ones is studying and analyzing the structural and behavioral patterns of malware. These patterns of sophisticated malware can be obtained with the help of Function Call Graphs (FCGs). However, to effectively cover numerous groups of families of malware, it is required to have a sufficiently large dataset for the system to operate on. In order to ensure accuracy and robustness of the system, the dataset should comprise samples of different malwares and a benign application for secure execution of the detection process. This paper introduces AMD-FCG, an enhanced Function Call Graph dataset integrated with topological features of malwares. The framework enhances the detection procedure, streamlining the workflow for cybersecurity professionals and also eliminating the need for dynamic analysis and extensive processing. Therefore, it can be used to develop and deploy more efficient and innovative malware detection systems.

</details>


### [54] [On the Incentive Compatibility of Block Propagation in Bitcoin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06860)
*Fumichika Maeda,Akira Sakurai,Taishi Nakai,Kazuyuki Shudo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了比特币矿工在区块传播中的激励问题，发现矿工没有动力转发其他矿工生成的区块；在"先见"规则下，非多数矿工有动力更快接收和传播自己的区块；不同区块冲突解决规则在传播激励和挖矿公平性之间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币系统依赖矿工传播区块，但矿工是否有激励去传播区块尚未被充分研究。如果矿工缺乏传播激励，将构成比特币激励设计的根本缺陷。

Method: 建立区块链网络模型，推导不同冲突解决规则下矿工奖励的解析表达式，刻画区块传播延迟、算力分布和冲突解决规则对挖矿奖励的联合影响。

Result: 矿工没有挖矿奖励层面的激励去转发其他矿工生成的区块；在先见规则下，非多数矿工有激励更快接收和传播自己的区块；不同规则在传播激励与挖矿公平性之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: 先见规则提供最强的降低传播延迟的激励，但同时最大程度地恶化了挖矿公平性。

Abstract: Bitcoin is permissionless and does not rely on any central administrator, which gives it strong censorship resistance. At the same time, it is important to incentivize miners to behave in ways that align with the interests of the system as a whole. This paper asks whether miners are individually incentivized to propagate blocks, one of the most fundamental processes in Bitcoin. Miners collectively maintain the blockchain by generating blocks and disseminating them across the network. If miners have an incentive not to propagate some blocks, this would indicate a fundamental flaw in Bitcoin's incentive design. Although prior work has studied how propagation delays affect forks and mining rewards, it has not fully characterized miners' incentives to improve block propagation under different tie-breaking rules. To address this gap, we derive analytical reward expressions for each tie-breaking rule based on a blockchain network model that captures the effect of forks on mining fairness. These expressions explicitly characterize how block propagation delays, hashrate distribution, and tie-breaking rules jointly determine mining rewards. We then use them to analyze miners' incentives to improve block propagation. Our results show, for example, that miners have no mining-reward incentive to relay blocks generated by other miners. By contrast, under the first-seen rule, every non-majority miner is incentivized to receive other miners' blocks more quickly and to propagate its own blocks more quickly. Finally, we compare tie-breaking rules and identify a trade-off between propagation incentives and mining fairness. In particular, the first-seen rule provides the strongest incentives to reduce propagation delays, but it also worsens mining fairness the most.

</details>


### [55] [FDM: A Framework for Decision-making to build ML-based Malware detection systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06894)
*Tadiwa Vhito,Jakapan Suaboot,Warodom Werapun,Norrathep Rattanavipanon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出决策框架（FDM），通过加权配置兼容性评分（WCCS）将五个操作参数映射到九个配置维度的推荐，用于构建基于机器学习的恶意软件检测系统。实验表明最优ML配置高度依赖部署上下文，XGBoost在资源受限场景表现最佳，深度学习模型在特定任务中优势反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为恶意软件检测中的ML配置选择这一多准则复杂问题提供系统化的决策框架，满足不同部署场景下的操作约束。

Method: 提出FDM框架，使用WCCS多准则评分函数，将五个操作参数（平台约束、资源预算、响应延迟、更新频率、检测灵敏度）映射到九个配置维度的排序推荐。在三个数据集（私有Windows API、Malimg图像基准、Android静态API）上进行了四项实验验证。

Result: (i) XGBoost在二分类中准确率97.46%，内存<70MB，优于LSTM/BiLSTM（2.8GB）；(ii) 多分类中经典模型（XGB 79.03%）优于深度模型（BiLSTM 72.27%），二分类排名反转；(iii) 类增量学习保持99.13%准确率，仅下降0.65pp；(iv) 迁移学习平均加速2.14倍；(v) 自编码器预处理加速14倍，准确率仅下降0.86pp。

Conclusion: 最优ML配置是上下文相关的，验证了FDM框架的核心前提及其对网络安全从业者的实用价值。

Abstract: Selecting appropriate machine learning (ML) configurations for malware detection is a complex, multi-criteria problem. Model choice, feature engineering, and update mechanisms must jointly satisfy operational constraints that vary across deployment contexts. This paper proposes the Framework for Decision-making (FDM) to build ML-based malware detection systems. The FDM formalises this selection process using the Weighted Configuration Compatibility Score (WCCS), a multi-criteria scoring function mapping five operational parameters (platform constraint, resource budget, response latency, update frequency, and detection sensitivity) to ranked recommendations across nine configuration dimensions. To validate the framework, four experiments were conducted on three datasets (a private Windows API dataset, the public Malimg image benchmark, and an Android static API dataset). Key results include: (i) XGBoost achieved the best accuracy-to-resource ratio in binary classification (97.46 % test accuracy, <70 MB RAM), outperforming LSTM/BiLSTM which consumed up to 2.8 GB; (ii) in multi-class classification, classical models (XGBoost 79.03 %) outperformed recurrent deep models (BiLSTM 72.27 %), reversing the binary ranking; (iii) class-incremental learning with EfficientNetB0 maintained 99.13 % accuracy with only 0.65 pp degradation across 11 incremental steps; (iv) transfer learning reduced training time by 2.14 times on average for image-based malware data without significant accuracy cost; and (v) autoencoder pre-processing yielded a 14 times training speedup at a cost of only 0.86 pp accuracy. These findings confirm that the optimal ML configuration is context-dependent, validating the FDM's core premise and demonstrating its practical utility for cybersecurity practitioners.

</details>


### [56] [Blockchain Infrastructure for Intelligent Cyber--Physical--Social Systems:Post-Quantum Security, Interoperability, and Trustworthy Data Economies in the Era of Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06895)
*Song Guo,Huawei Huang,Dongping Liu,Aoyu Zhang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本教程探讨了区块链作为协调层，在具身人工智能（基于世界模型的机器人技术）与量子计算威胁的双重转型中，构建抗量子、可互操作且数据可信的去中心化智能系统基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI的部署对区块链基础设施提出了数据溯源、跨组织治理和激励兼容共享的迫切需求，同时量子计算进展（2025年诺贝尔物理学奖与图灵奖）威胁了保障这些数据经济的密码学原语，因此需要能够抵御量子攻击的加密敏捷架构。

Method: 通过五个集成模块展开：具身AI与世界模型需求分析、量子硬件现实与基于证据的安全迁移、基于BrokerChain协议的可扩展跨分片架构、实现Croissant元数据标准和机器人学习溯源的可信数据经济、以及多模态云部署的行业生态系统集成，并以AWS Braket演示展示超导、离子阱和中性原子量子硬件上的ECDSA到后量子签名转换。

Result: 提供了开源框架和路线图，用于构建抗量子、可互操作且数据可信的系统，将区块链确立为从金融底层到网络-物理-社会系统基础设施的统一协调层。

Conclusion: 区块链作为连接量子硬件现实与具身AI数据需求的统一基础设施，能够支撑下一代去中心化智能环境。

Abstract: The deployment of embodied artificial intelligence via world-model-based robotics presents a transformative opportunity for blockchain infrastructure, establishing urgent demand for trustworthy data provenance, cross-organizational governance, and incentive-compatible sharing across decentralized ecosystems. Simultaneously, quantum computing advances recognized by the 2025 Nobel Prize in Physics and the Turing Award threaten the cryptographic primitives securing these data economies, creating an interdependent imperative: long-lived verification for embodied AI depends on crypto-agile architectures capable of withstanding quantum adversaries. This tutorial examines blockchain as the coordination layer bridging this dual transition, from financial substrate to foundational Cyber-Physical-Social Systems infrastructure that simultaneously secures against quantum cryptanalysis and enables scalable, trustworthy data economies. The session opens with an immersive AWS Braket demonstration engaging participants with superconducting, trapped-ion, and neutral-atom hardware to assess cryptographic threat timelines and witness ECDSA-to-post-quantum signature transitions. Five integrated modules progress from embodied AI and world-model requirements through quantum hardware reality and evidence-based security migration, to scalable cross-shard architectures via BrokerChain protocols, trustworthy data economies implementing Croissant metadata standards and robotic learning provenance, and industry ecosystem integration for multi-modal cloud deployment. By bridging quantum hardware realities with embodied AI data requirements, this tutorial charts blockchain as unified infrastructure for next-generation decentralized intelligent environments, providing open-source frameworks and roadmaps for architecting quantum-resistant, interoperable, and data-trustworthy systems.

</details>


### [57] [DPAgent-in-the-Middle: Agentic Defense and Repair Against AI-Groomed Deceptive Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06914)
*Zewei Shi,Ruoxi Sun,Haoyang Li,Seong Oun Hwang,Feng Liu,Minhui Xue,Xingliang Yuan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AI Grooming威胁模型，并开发DPAgent框架，通过多智能体协作在真实网页环境中主动检测和修复隐私欺骗模式，检测率达90.98%，可修复77%的欺骗界面，大规模研究发现98%的网站存在至少一种隐私欺骗模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对网页界面中隐私欺骗模式的防御手段存在碎片化、静态化的问题，且容易受到大语言模型的操纵。同时，数据空洞（data voids）为攻击者注入误导性内容提供了土壤，这些内容可被AI系统学习，放大欺骗设计和模型误行为。因此需要一种主动、动态且能应对新兴AI威胁的防御机制。

Method: 形式化定义了AI Grooming威胁模型，攻击者利用数据空洞注入看似良性但恶意的样本来污染模型推理。提出DPAgent框架，协调四个专门智能体，结合潜在空间净化与防御性提示，在真实网页环境中主动探测、检测和修复隐私欺骗用户界面。

Result: DPAgent检测90.98%的groomed样本；隐私欺骗模式检测达到SOTA水平（micro F1=0.816）；仅需访问基线方法约10%的页面即可探索超过80%的模式类型；成功修复77%的欺骗界面。对485个网站的大规模研究发现98%含有至少一个隐私欺骗模式，其中90%以上可被DPAgent缓解。

Conclusion: DPAgent作为一种智能体中间人防御机制，能够有效保障网页UI供应链安全，抵御欺骗性设计和基于数据空洞利用的新兴AI威胁，同时用户研究证实其能降低隐私风险且不影响浏览体验。

Abstract: Privacy deceptive patterns in web interfaces systematically manipulate users into disclosing personal data, yet existing defenses are fragmented, static, and increasingly vulnerable to manipulation by large language models. Moreover, data voids, areas of information scarcity within the web ecosystem, create fertile ground for adversaries to inject misleading content that can be scraped and learned by AI systems, thereby amplifying both deceptive design and model misbehavior. In this paper, we formalize a new threat model, AI grooming, where attackers exploit data voids to seed benign-looking but malicious samples that corrupt model reasoning and normalize deceptive practices. To address this threat in privacy deceptive patterns, we present DPAgent, an agentic and reasoning-aware framework that orchestrates four specialized agents to mitigate the AI Grooming threat via a proactive defense that combines latent space purification with defensive prompting and operates directly in live web environments to proactively explore, detect, and repair privacy deceptive user interfaces before they reach end users. Extensive evaluations show that DPAgent detects 90.98% of groomed samples, achieves state-of-the-art privacy deceptive pattern detection with a micro F1 of 0.816, explores over 80% of pattern types while visiting only about 10% of the pages required by baselines, and successfully repairs 77% of detected deceptive interfaces. A large-scale study of 485 websites in the wild reveals that up to 98% contain at least one privacy deceptive pattern, over 90% of which can be mitigated by DPAgent. User studies further confirm that DPAgent effectively reduces privacy risks while preserving browsing experience. Our results demonstrate the promise of agent-in-the-middle defenses for securing the web UI supply chain against deceptive design and emerging AI threats rooted in data void exploitation.

</details>


### [58] [HAVE: Host Active Verification Engine for Closing the Contextual Reality Gap in Security Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06968)
*Vincenzo Sammartino,Marco Pasquini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Host Active Verification Engine (HAVE)，通过部署安全受限的主机代理在快照隔离的伯努利试验中进行最大似然估计，测量经验性妥协概率$\hat{p}$，以解决安全数字孪生（SDT）中CVSS评分导致的上下文现实差距问题。实验表明，HAVE在假阳性场景下将$P_{\text{reach}}$降低38.2%，在假阴性场景下提升132.4%，净校正达+124.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全数字孪生（SDT）依赖理论CVSS评分分配横向移动概率，导致上下文现实差距：未确认的缓解措施使风险被高估，逻辑缺陷绕过内存安全防御使风险被严重低估。

Method: HAVE扩展通过部署安全受限的主机代理，在快照隔离的伯努利试验中采用最大似然估计测量经验性妥协概率$\hat{p}$，利用Wilson区间宽度置信权重$\alpha_w$通过贝叶斯混合规则（与Beta-Binomial后验形式相关）将$\hat{p}$传播到蒙特卡洛模拟中。

Result: 在四类漏洞、三个安全级别和两个生产二进制文件上，HAVE在假阳性场景下减少$P_{\text{reach}}$ 38.2%，在假阴性场景下增加132.4%，净校正+124.1%；后HAVE估计在不同校准指数$\kappa$下仅变化1.12倍，而CVSS基线变化4.6倍。

Conclusion: HAVE有效缩小了SDT中的上下文现实差距，显著提升威胁模拟中横向移动概率评估的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Security Digital Twins (SDTs) provide continuously updated virtual replicas of infrastructure for threat simulation, yet they rely on theoretical CVSS scores to assign lateral-movement probabilities -- creating the Contextual Reality Gap: risk is overestimated where unacknowledged mitigations neutralize exploits, and drastically underestimated where logic flaws bypass all memory-safety defenses. We present the Host Active Verification Engine (HAVE), an SDT extension that deploys a safety-constrained host agent to measure the empirical probability of compromise $\hat{p}$ via maximum-likelihood estimation over snapshot-isolated Bernoulli trials. A Wilson interval-width confidence weight $α_w$ propagates $\hat{p}$ into Monte Carlo simulations via a Bayesian blending rule formally related to the Beta-Binomial posterior. Evaluation across four vulnerability classes, three security tiers, and two production binaries shows HAVE reduces $P_{\text{reach}}$ by 38.2% in false-positive scenarios and increases it by 132.4% in false-negative scenarios, with a net +124.1% correction; post-HAVE estimates vary by only $1.12\times$ across calibration exponents $κ$, versus $4.6\times$ for CVSS-only baselines.

</details>


### [59] [The Sound of Malware: A Memory Forensics Approach for Android Malware Analysis via Audio Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07005)
*Silvia Lucia Sanna,Massimo Palozzi,Leonardo Regano,Riccardo Lazzeretti,Giorgio Giacinto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于内存取证和音频波形映射的安卓恶意软件检测框架\approachname，通过将字节码和内存快照转换为音频信号，利用频谱描述符、CNN和Transformer进行检测，在CICMalDroid2020和VirusTotal数据集上达到98.0%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安卓恶意软件分析面临鲁棒分类和隐身攻击检测的双重挑战，先进威胁采用代码混淆、动态加载、加壳甚至隐写操作来规避传统的静态和动态特征，导致基于签名的系统失效，依赖显式语义指标（如权限、API调用、控制流结构）的机器学习模型可靠性下降。

Method: \approachname采用内存取证与信号处理相结合的思路：将静态字节码和早期执行内存快照通过直接的二进制到音频波形映射转换为音频波形，保留底层结构模式，无需反汇编或特征工程；随后使用手工设计的频谱描述符、卷积神经网络和Transformer嵌入对生成的信号进行处理。

Result: 在CICMalDroid2020数据集和VirusTotal恶意软件样本上进行实验，\approachname达到98.0%的准确率，优于静态音频化方法和当前最先进的竞争方法。

Conclusion: 将恶意软件分析从语义程序建模转向基于信号的表示是可行的，通过二进制到波形映射结合深度学习模型可以有效检测经过混淆和隐写处理的安卓恶意软件。

Abstract: Android malware analysis is currently facing increasing challenges in achieving robust classification and detecting stealth attacks. Modern threats employ advanced evasion strategies such as code obfuscation, dynamic loading, packing, and even steganographic manipulation of traditional static and dynamic features. These techniques reduce the effectiveness of signature-based systems and degrade the reliability of Machine Learning models that depend on explicit semantic indicators such as permissions, API calls, or control-flow structures. In this work, we propose \approachname, a memory forensics malware detection framework that shifts the analysis perspective from semantic program modeling to signal-based structural representation. Both static bytecode and early-execution memory snapshots are transformed into audio waveforms through direct binary-to-waveform mapping, preserving low-level structural patterns without requiring disassembly or feature engineering. The resulting signals are processed using handcrafted spectral descriptors, Convolutional Neural Networks, and transformer-based embeddings. Experiments on CICMalDroid2020 dataset and VirusTotal malware demonstrate that \approachname achieves up to 98.0\% accuracy, outperforming static sonification and competitive state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [60] [Fast Bounded-Independence Functions and Their Duals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07009)
*Martijn Brehm,Yuval Ishai,Nicolas Resch*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究可由线性大小电路高效计算的"快速"函数，在密码学应用驱动下，构建了最优代数度的t-wise独立哈希函数、改进的快速码族（达到Gilbert-Varshamov界且失败概率可忽略），以及支持最优组合列表解码的快速线性函数族。这些成果首次实现了电路复杂度与参与方数量线性相关的完美安全多方计算协议，以及具有最优渐进电路复杂度的加密矩阵-向量乘积协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密码学应用中，需要同时满足高效计算（线性大小电路）和随机函数性质（如独立哈希、编码性能等）的函数。现有工作在代数度优化、编码构造的失败概率、通用性以及更强的随机性性质（如列表解码）等方面存在不足，本文旨在系统性地改进和推广这些结果。

Method: 1) 对于任意常数t，构造代数度为log₂ t的快速t-wise独立哈希函数，同时优化电路大小和代数度。2) 简化并改进ITCS 2026的快速码构造，使其失败概率可忽略、支持一般域和码率、支持系统编码和快速通用编码器。3) 构造快速线性函数族，使得任意t个线性无关的输入映射为均匀且统计独立的输出（此前仅对t=1已知），从而支持最优组合列表解码。

Result: 得到了代数度与电路大小同时最优的t-wise独立哈希函数；失败概率可忽略、更通用的快速码族（达到GV界）；支持最优组合列表解码的快速线性函数族；首个电路复杂度与参与方数量线性相关的完美安全多方计算协议；具有最优渐进电路复杂度的加密矩阵-向量乘积协议。

Conclusion: 本文在快速函数（线性大小电路可计算）的研究中取得了系统性进展，在哈希函数、编码理论和密码学协议三个方向上实现了理论突破，为高效密码学原语的构造提供了新的工具和基础。

Abstract: We continue the study of {\em fast} functions, computable by linear-size circuits, that share useful properties of random functions. Motivated by cryptographic applications, we generalize and improve on previous results in this area, obtaining the following results:
  - For any constant $t$, we construct a fast $t$-wise independent hash function with algebraic degree $\log_2 t$ (over $\mathbb F_2$), simultaneously optimizing both asymptotic circuit size and degree.
  - We simplify and improve a recent construction (ITCS 2026) of a family of fast codes with fast duals, both meeting the Gilbert-Varshamov bound. Unlike the previous construction, our construction has negligible failure probability, can accommodate general fields and rates, supports a systematic encoding, and admits fast universal encoders.
  - We strengthen the above to support stronger random-like properties, such as optimal combinatorial list-decoding. This is achieved by constructing, for any constant $t$, a family of fast linear functions that map any $t$ linearly independent inputs to uniform and statistically independent outputs. Prior to our work, this was only known for $t=1$.
  We demonstrate the usefulness of the above results to cryptography. This includes the first nontrivial protocols for perfectly secure multiparty computation whose circuit complexity scales linearly with the number of parties, as well as protocols for computing encrypted matrix-vector products with optimal asymptotic circuit complexity.

</details>


### [61] [MalSkillBench: A Runtime-Verified Benchmark of Malicious Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07131)
*Wenbo Guo,Wei Zeng,Chengwei Liu,Xiaojun Jia,Yijia Xu,Lei Tang,Yong Fang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MalSkillBench，首个运行时验证的恶意Agent技能基准测试，包含3,944个恶意技能（跨越108个三维分类单元），通过封闭式生成-验证-反馈流水线构建。研究发现：代码注入验证率达94.5%但提示注入仅75.8%；最强检测器在代码注入上召回率达98.4%，但在提示注入和Agent控制攻击上完全失效；仅使用野外样本评估会使排名波动高达66个召回点。现有供应链扫描器和提示注入防御各自只能看到技能的一半，且任何组合都无法恢复代码-指令关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码Agent（如Claude Code、Gemini CLI）正通过第三方技能扩展自身，但这些技能同时包含自然语言指令、可执行脚本和工具权限，构成了既非纯代码也非纯提示的混合型供应链依赖风险。现有检测工具从未在此混合空间内基于验证过的真实数据进行评估，其有效性未知，且仅依赖野外样本的评估存在偏差。

Method: 构建了封闭式生成-验证-反馈（Generate-Verify-Feedback）流水线，在Docker沙箱中通过系统调用监控和LLM裁判验证恶意行为，生成3,214个恶意技能；辅以703个野外样本和4,000个匹配的良性技能，形成完整基准。采用三维分类体系（108个单元格）对技能进行标注。

Result: 代码注入验证率达94.5%，提示注入仅75.8%；野外样本高度同质化（86.6%为单一行为，81%来自两个账户）；最强技能特定检测器在代码注入上召回率98.4%，但在提示注入和Agent控制攻击上完全失效；仅用野外样本评估导致排名波动高达66个召回点；供应链扫描器和提示注入防御各自仅能覆盖技能的一半信息。

Conclusion: 检测恶意技能需要联合推理任务意图、代码和指令三者关系。现有单一检测方法均无法有效覆盖混合型风险，该基准揭示了当前防御体系的系统性盲区。

Abstract: AI coding agents such as Claude Code and Gemini CLI increasingly extend themselves with third-party skills: markdown packages bundling natural-language instructions, executable scripts, and tool permissions. Because a skill is at once code and agent-facing instruction, it introduces a supply chain dependency whose risk is neither pure code nor pure prompt. Detection tools have never been measured against verified ground truth spanning this hybrid space, leaving their effectiveness unknown and wild-only evaluations biased.
  We present MalSkillBench, the first runtime-verified benchmark of malicious agent skills: 3,944 malicious skills labeled along a three-dimensional taxonomy of 108 cells. Of these, 3,214 come from a closed-loop Generate-Verify-Feedback pipeline admitting only samples whose malicious behavior fires inside a Docker sandbox under system-call monitoring and an LLM judge; we add 703 in-the-wild and 4,000 matched benign skills. Our measurements are consistent: code injection reaches 94.5% verification yield but prompt injection only 75.8%, the same fragility that later makes it hard to detect; the wild sample is narrow, dominated by one cryptocurrency-theft campaign (86.6% one behavior, 81% from two accounts) with a small but architecturally new tail attacking the agent control plane; the strongest skill-specific detector reaches 98.4% recall on code injection yet collapses on prompt-injection and agent-control attacks, and wild-only scoring swings the ranking by up to 66 recall points; supply-chain scanners and prompt-injection defenses each see only half of a skill, and no combination recovers the code-instruction relationship. Detecting malicious skills therefore requires reasoning jointly over task intent, code, and instructions. We release the dataset, pipeline, baselines, and results.

</details>


### [62] [From Privacy to Workflow Integrity: Communication-Graph Metadata in Autonomous Agent Interoperability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07150)
*Bijaya Dangol*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了智能体通信协议（如A2A、MCP）中元数据泄露的威胁：通信图（谁联系谁、何时、频率）在智能体系统中不仅是隐私问题，更是工作流完整性问题。攻击者可从被动元数据中恢复任务类别、推断待执行工作流并采取行动。论文定义了元数据保护所需属性，评估了多种传输方案，并通过真实A2A捕获的生成模型验证：基于元数据的分类器能以远超随机水平的准确率恢复任务类别，而所提保护属性可将其压制回随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体通信协议（A2A、MCP）虽对消息内容进行端到端加密，但通信图（元数据）完全暴露在明文传输中。在智能体系统中，由于端点带有能力标签、工作流结构化且耦合实际动作，元数据泄露比传统隐私问题更严重——可推断待执行工作流、预测即将发生的行动，使攻击者能以机器速度在工作流完成前采取行动。因此威胁从隐私升级为工作流完整性。

Method: 1) 提出智能体通信图的威胁模型，定义元数据在不同传输层（传输层、引导层）的隐私属性；2) 评估候选传输方案（SimpleX/SMP、Tor、混合网络）的元数据保护能力；3) 以A2A协议为案例，分析其身份假设与元数据保护的张力；4) 基于真实A2A流量捕获构建生成模型，训练分类器从被动元数据（仅工作流开头部分）恢复任务类别，并测试所提保护属性对分类性能的抑制效果。

Result: 从被动元数据（仅工作流开头部分）出发，分类器能以远超随机水平的准确率恢复任务类别；应用所提传输层和引导层保护属性后，分类性能被压制回随机水平。在固定预算下，利用元数据泄露选择攻击目标的对手，其优势接近于全知攻击者（相对于元数据盲攻击者），而所提保护属性同样能有效抑制这一优势。

Conclusion: 智能体通信元数据泄露是从隐私到工作流完整性的严重威胁；通过传输层和引导层的元数据保护属性设计，可有效消除这一威胁；但现有协议（如A2A）的身份假设与元数据保护存在张力，需要在协议设计中纳入元数据保护考量。

Abstract: Agent-interoperability protocols such as A2A and MCP standardize what agents say to one another, but assume address-based transport over HTTP(S). Such transports protect message content, increasingly with end-to-end encryption. What they leave in the clear is the communication graph: which agent contacts which, when, and how often. In agent systems this graph is more consequential than a privacy framing suggests. Endpoints are often capability-labeled, workflows are structured and chained, and interactions are coupled to real actions, so an observer recovers more than past relationships. It can infer the pending workflow, the task being assembled and the action likely to follow. At machine speed, it can act on that inference before the workflow completes. The threat is therefore one of workflow integrity, not privacy alone: predictive leverage over autonomous action. We give a threat model for the agent communication graph; identify what makes agent metadata distinctively revealing (semanticity, prospectivity, actuation); define transport- and bootstrap-layer privacy properties and weigh candidate transports (SimpleX/SMP, Tor, mixnets) against them; and present an A2A case study in which a metadata-protecting binding is expressible but surfaces the protocol's identity assumptions. We test these on a generative model anchored to a real A2A capture. From passive metadata alone, with no payloads, a classifier recovers a task's class well above chance, from only the workflow's opening; applied together, the properties drive that recovery sharply back toward chance. Beyond what an observer can recover, we measure the leverage of acting on the leak: from a workflow's opening and under a fixed budget, an adversary choosing which workflows to act on realizes in this model most of a clairvoyant attacker's advantage over a metadata-blind one, and the same properties suppress it.

</details>


### [63] [Synthetic APTs: the Collapse of TTP-Based Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07158)
*Francesco Balassone,Víctor Mayoral-Vilches,María Sanz-Gómez,Paul Zabalegui-Landa,Stefan Rass,Davide Quarta,Daniel Sanchez-Prieto,Marina Oteiza-Álvarez,Almerindo Graziano,Lauren Min Kim,MinSeok Choi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI驱动的对手模拟是否挑战了基于TTP的网络威胁情报归因假设。通过CSI框架配置五种APT组织，在两类网络靶场中进行20次实验，发现企业网络全部被攻陷（攻击者甚至自主利用防御方的Velociraptor作为C2通道），而军事网络全部被成功防御。结论认为AI时代降低了APT级攻击门槛，个体也能像国家行为体一样行动，从根本上动摇了基于TTP的归因基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证AI驱动的对手模拟是否能够挑战传统基于TTP的威胁归因方法，探究AI时代攻击归因的有效性。

Method: 利用CSI框架配置五种APT（APT28、APT29、APT41、APT44、Lazarus Group）作为攻击代理，在CYBER RANGES提供的企业网络和军事基础设施两类靶场中，与AI驱动的防御代理进行20次对抗实验，使用Wazuh、Velociraptor、Elasticsearch等防御工具。

Result: 企业网络10次实验全部被攻陷（每实验2-12台主机受损），军事网络10次实验全部成功防御或陷入僵局；8次企业实验中攻击者自主将防御方的Velociraptor武器化为C2通道，该行为未编码于任何威胁情报档案中。

Conclusion: AI时代降低了APT级攻击的操作门槛，非国家行为体也能模仿已知威胁行为体，从而从根本上瓦解基于TTP的归因方法。

Abstract: Cyber Threat Intelligence CTI attribution relies on identifying the Tactics, Techniques, and Procedures TTPs that distinguish one threat actor from another. This approach presupposes that each adversary leaves a recognizable operational fingerprint. This work investigates whether AI driven adversary emulation challenges that presupposition. We deploy agents from our Cybersecurity SuperIntelligence CSI framework, configured as five Advanced Persistent Threat APT groups, APT28, APT29, APT41, APT44, and Lazarus Group, against AI driven Defender agents across two cyber ranges provided by CYBER RANGES, equipped with defensive software Wazuh, Velociraptor, Elasticsearch and active AI driven defenders: an enterprise network and a military infrastructure. Across 20 experiments using two defender models, a binary pattern emerges: all 10 Enterprise range experiments resulted in compromise 2 to 12 hosts per experiment, while all 10 Military range experiments were successfully defended or resulted in stalemates, regardless of APT profile or defender model. In 8 of 10 Enterprise experiments, attackers independently weaponized the defender's own Velociraptor endpoint management platform as a command and control channel, a convergent behavior not encoded in any threat intelligence profile. We argue that in the AI era, wherein agents can be deployed provided the right models are available and subject to the right scaffolding and agentic configuration, the entry barrier for operating like a nation state APT collapses: beyond nation states, individuals can now act like commonly identified threat actors, and with it, fundamentally undermine TTP based attribution.

</details>


### [64] [Rethinking IoT Intrusion Detection: Augmenting Routing Metrics with Radio Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07282)
*Yichang Sun,Andreas Johnsson,Sourasekhar Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究在基于LSTM的入侵检测系统中，将无线发送（TX）和接收（RX）射频特征与标准RPL路由层特征相结合，以提高对RPL-based IoT网络的攻击检测性能。实验结果表明，在DIS-Flooding、Local Repair和Worst Parent三种攻击下，融合TX/RX特征可使F1分数提升约4%，其中对Worst Parent攻击的提升最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于机器学习的RPL-IoT网络入侵检测系统仅依赖路由层特征，无法全面反映网络行为，导致检测性能受限。本文旨在探究引入TX/RX射频特征能否弥补这一不足，提升LSTM-based IDS的检测效果。

Method: 采用LSTM-based IDS作为检测模型，将TX/RX射频特征与标准RPL特征集融合作为输入特征。在三种攻击场景（DIS-Flooding、Local Repair、Worst Parent）及不同网络规模下进行实验评估。

Result: 融合TX/RX特征相比仅使用路由层特征，整体检测性能的F1分数提升约4%，其中Worst Parent攻击的检测增益最为明显。

Conclusion: 引入TX/RX射频特征能够有效增强LSTM-based IDS对RPL-IoT网络攻击的检测能力，验证了跨层特征融合对提升入侵检测系统性能的有效性。

Abstract: Machine learning-based intrusion detection systems (IDS) for RPL-based IoT networks often rely solely on routing layer features, which provide only a partial view of network behaviour. In this work, we investigate whether incorporating Transmit (TX) and Receive (RX) radio features alongside the standard RPL feature set can improve detection performance in an LSTM-based IDS. We evaluate the proposed approach across three different attack types, namely DIS-Flooding, Local Repair, and Worst Parent under varying network sizes. The results show that incorporating TX and RX improves the IDS's overall detection performance by up to ~4% in F1-score compared with using routing-layer features alone, with the most notable gain observed for the Worst Parent attack.

</details>


### [65] [Authorized and Verifiable Searchable Encryption Based on Public Key Equality Test for Cloud Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07319)
*Xiuping Li,Kaiwen Wang,Xiaolin Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AVPKEET方案，实现无需可信第三方的密文文件级授权与公开可验证性，并基于此构建AVSE可搜索加密系统，支持一次性不可传递令牌、批量操作和细粒度访问控制，在标准假设下证明安全性，令牌仅168字节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于PKEET的可搜索加密方案缺乏密文文件级授权、公开可验证性及SE级支持，存在功能与隐私之间的冲突。

Method: 提出AVPKEET（授权且可验证的PKEET）方案，实现非传递、不可重放的密文文件授权与公开可验证性；进而提出AVSE方案，支持一次性不可传递令牌（绑定用户和nonce）、批量操作及ALL/PARTIAL/SINGLE三种细粒度访问控制。

Result: 在标准假设下证明OW-CCA2安全、令牌不可伪造和验证可靠性；AVSE令牌仅168字节，同时提供密文文件级授权与公开验证，开销在可接受范围内。

Conclusion: AVPKEET及AVSE方案在功能完备性（授权、验证、细粒度控制）和效率（紧凑令牌）上优于现有方案，适用于云存储部署。

Abstract: Cloud storage revolutionizes data management but raises conflicts between functionality and privacy. Public Key Encryption with Equality Test (PKEET), an advanced cryptographic technique, can enable multi-user searchable encryption (SE) through cross-key ciphertext comparison without shared keys. However, existing PKEET-based SE schemes lack ciphertext-file-level authorization, public verifiability, or SE-level support. This paper first proposes a novel PKEET scheme, AVPKEET (Authorized and Verifiable PKEET). It enables non-transferable and non-replayable authorization of ciphertext files, while supporting public verifiability, all without the need for trusted third parties. Then we propose an AVPKEET-based SE scheme, denoted as AVSE (Authorized and Verifiable SE), featuring one-time non-transferable tokens bound to users and nonces, batch operations, and fine-grained access control (ALL, PARTIAL, SINGLE). We prove OW-CCA2 security, token unforgeability, and verification soundness under standard assumptions. Experiment results demonstrate that AVSE achieves the most compact token size (168 bytes) while uniquely providing both ciphertext-file-level authorization and public verification, with acceptable overhead for cloud storage deployment.

</details>


### [66] [Defending Jailbreak Attacks on Large Language Models via Manifold Trajectory Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07335)
*Hangtao Zhang,Yucheng Zhao,Sishun Liu,Ziqi Zhou,Zeyu Ye,Wei Wan,Minghui Li,Shengshan Hu,Yanjun Zhang,Yi Liu,Leo Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Manifold Trajectory Kinetics (MTK)，一种通过追踪提示词在LLM各层中邻域结构演变来检测越狱提示的方法，解决了传统方法在伪恶意提示和自适应攻击下失效的问题。实验表明，MTK在四种LLM和十种攻击下，对伪恶意提示达到95%真阳率（假阳率2-5%），自适应攻击下保持85%真阳率，并扩展至视觉语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱检测方法依赖固定的度量空间（如原始输入、梯度、隐藏特征），假设良性和越狱提示在该空间线性可分。但这一假设在以下两种情况被打破：(i)伪恶意提示——意图良性但包含安全相关关键词；(ii)自适应攻击——针对检测器进行显式优化。因此需要更鲁棒的检测方法。

Method: MTK将LLM视为将输入转化为输出的动力学系统，通过追踪提示词在各层中邻域结构的演化轨迹来检测越狱。良性提示在整个推理过程中始终接近良性邻域，而越狱提示呈现特征性轨迹：从接近恶意种子开始，随后策略性地转向良性邻域以规避拒绝。

Result: 在四种LLM和十种越狱攻击下：伪恶意提示达到95%越狱真阳率，在良性提示上假阳率5%，在伪恶意提示上假阳率2%；自适应攻击下保持85%真阳率。此外，MTK在视觉语言模型上同样表现出色。

Conclusion: MTK通过分析数据流形的邻域结构而非寻找通用度量空间，有效解决了伪恶意提示和自适应攻击两个关键失败模式，在多种LLM和攻击场景下展现出强鲁棒性，并可扩展至多模态模型。

Abstract: Jailbreak prompts can bypass alignment guardrails in large language models (LLMs) and elicit unsafe outputs, making reliable deployment-time detection critical. Prior detection approaches largely rely on a fixed metric space, e.g., raw inputs, gradients, or hidden features, in which benign and jailbreak prompts are linearly separable. We show this assumption breaks under (i) pseudo-malicious prompts that are benign by intent but contain safety-related keywords, and (ii) adaptive attacks that explicitly optimize against the deployed detector. To overcome this limitation, we shift our focus from identifying a universal metric space to analyzing the more robust neighborhood structure of the underlying data manifold. We present Manifold Trajectory Kinetics (MTK), which treats an LLM as a kinetic system transforming inputs into outputs and detects jailbreaks by tracking how a prompt's neighborhood structure evolves across layers. Benign prompts remain close to benign neighborhoods throughout inference, whereas jailbreak prompts exhibit a characteristic trajectory that begins near malicious seeds and later strategically shifts toward benign neighborhoods to evade refusal.Across four LLMs and ten jailbreak attacks, MTK achieves strong robustness to both failure modes: on pseudo-malicious prompts, it attains a jailbreak true positive rate of 95% at a false positive rate of 5% on benign prompts and 2% on pseudo-malicious prompts, and under adaptive attacks, it maintains a true positive rate of 85%. We further demonstrate the superior performance of MTK for jailbreak detection in vision-language models. Our code is available at https://github.com/Rookie143/mtk.

</details>


### [67] [Empirical Evaluation of Large Language Models for Migration of Code Fragments to Post-Quantum Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07341)
*Javier Pallarés de Bonrostro,Ana I. González-Tablas,María Isabel González Vasco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了微调大型语言模型（LLM）辅助后量子密码学（PQC）代码迁移的有效性，基于800对合成数据集评估了四种模型，发现微调后的GPT-4.1-mini在静态相似度（0.9072）和功能正确率（92.5%）上表现最佳，但在大规模复杂项目中仍存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PQC迁移框架主要提供组织层面的指导，而实际代码层面的迁移仍然高度依赖人工，且容易出错。为此，本文探索能否通过训练LLM来自动化完成前量子密码代码片段向后量子密码的迁移，同时保持功能正确性。

Method: 构建了一个包含800对Python代码片段的合成数据集，涵盖6种密码学家族及多原语混合场景，每对均通过类别特定功能测试验证。评估了GPT-4.1零样本、以及微调后的GPT-3.5-turbo、GPT-4.1-mini和CodeLlama-7B-Instruct四种模型，并在6个开源仓库上进行了补充验证。

Result: 微调后的GPT-4.1-mini表现最佳：平均静态相似度0.9072，动态功能正确率92.5%，显著优于零样本基线。在开源仓库验证中，该方法能成功迁移局部密码模块，但在涉及复杂依赖和跨模块交互的大型项目中仍存在局限。

Conclusion: 微调后的LLM可以作为未来密码敏捷迁移流水线的实用组件，但需要结合自动化验证和依赖感知的校验机制。

Abstract: The transition to post-quantum cryptography (PQC) requires not only replacing vulnerable cryptographic primitives, but also refactoring the surrounding software logic. While existing PQC migration frameworks provide organizational guidance, practical code-level remediation remains largely manual and error-prone. This paper evaluates whether large language models (LLMs) can be trained to assist in the migration of pre-quantum cryptographic code fragments to post-quantum counterparts while preserving functional correctness. To this end, we introduce a reproducible experimental framework built around a synthetic dataset of 800 paired Python code fragments covering six cryptographic families and combined multi-primitive cases. Each pair is validated through category-specific functional tests, enabling both dataset quality control and objective evaluation of model-generated migrations. Four models are assessed: GPT-4.1 in a zero-shot setting, and fine-tuned versions of GPT-3.5-turbo, GPT-4.1-mini, and CodeLlama-7B-Instruct. The results show that domain-specific fine-tuning is essential for reliable cryptographic migration. The fine-tuned GPT-4.1-mini model achieves the best overall performance, with a mean static similarity of 0.9072 and a dynamic functional correctness rate of 92.5%, substantially outperforming the zero-shot baseline. A complementary validation on six open-source repositories further shows that the approach can produce useful migrations in localized cryptographic modules, while also revealing limitations in larger projects with complex dependencies and cross-module interactions. These findings suggest that fine-tuned LLMs can serve as practical components in future crypto-agile migration pipelines, provided they are coupled with automated verification and dependency-aware validation.

</details>


### [68] [On the Shoulders of Giants: Empowering Automated Smart Contract Auditing via the GiAnt Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07363)
*Xiaoting Zhang,Zhipeng Gao,Yiran Lv,Xing Hu,Feifei Niu,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GiANT框架，通过从Code4rena真实审计报告中蒸馏漏洞知识，自动构建高质量智能合约审计数据集GiAnt Corpus（含7,711个漏洞发现），解决了现有数据集依赖人工标注、粒度不足的问题。数据集在信息提取上达到4.76/5的平均质量评分（κ=0.88），并基于该数据集对4个LLM在漏洞检测等任务上建立了性能基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能合约审计数据集存在两大局限性：人工标注导致的可扩展性瓶颈，以及数据粒度和多样性的不足。亟需一个自动化框架来高效构建高质量、细粒度的审计数据集，以支持安全工具评估和研究。

Method: 提出GiANT框架，采用分治策略结合Chain-of-Thought（思维链）技术，从Code4rena真实审计报告中提取结构化漏洞信息，并引入LLM-as-a-judge机制进行严格的质量保证。最终在388份真实审计报告上运行，生成包含7,711个漏洞发现（覆盖五个严重级别）的GiAnt Corpus数据集。

Result: 手动评估显示信息提取可靠，平均质量得分4.76±0.37（满分5），评分者间一致性κ=0.88。利用该数据集对4个SOTA LLM在漏洞检测、代码总结、缓解建议和自动Gas优化任务上进行了基准测试，建立了性能基线。

Conclusion: GiANT成功实现了智能合约审计数据集的自动化构建，解决了人工标注的瓶颈问题，所生成的高质量数据集为未来智能合约自动审计研究提供了有价值的数据基础。

Abstract: High-quality smart contract auditing datasets are crucial for evaluating security tools and advancing smart contract security research. Two major limitations of existing datasets are the manual-induced scalability bottleneck and the deficiency in data granularity and diversity. To address these limitations, we propose GiANT, an automated framework designed to curate smart contract auditing datasets by distilling vulnerability insights from real-world auditing reports. GiANT employs a divide-and-conquer strategy coupled with the Chain-of-Thought technique to extract structured vulnerability information from Code4rena reports, followed by an LLM-as-a-judge mechanism to perform rigorous quality assurance. To evaluate GiANT's effectiveness, we run it on 388 real-world audit reports and generate the GiAnt Corpus comprising 7,711 vulnerability findings across five severity levels. Manual assessment of the dataset demonstrates exceptional reliability in information extraction, achieving a mean quality score of $4.76\pm0.37$ (out of 5) with inter-rater agreement $κ$ of 0.88. We further validate the practicality of our dataset by benchmarking 4 state-of-the-art LLMs on vulnerability detection, code summarization, mitigation recommendation, and automated gas optimization tasks, to establish performance baselines, thereby providing a valuable data foundation for future research in automated smart contract auditing.

</details>


### [69] [Lost in Migration: Exposing Android Framework Vulnerabilities in Parallel Java-Kotlin Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07420)
*Rui Li,Wenrui Diao,Debin Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了Android框架中Java-Kotlin并行实现的安全影响，设计并实现了ParaDroid分析框架，在AOSP Android 14-16中发现了329对并行方法及37个有安全风险的差异，其中3个漏洞和2个bug已获确认，并分配了2个CVE编号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android在从Java向Kotlin迁移过程中，AOSP中出现了同一组件既有Java实现又有Kotlin实现的并行代码现象。尽管功能目的相同，但语义上的细微差异可能揭示周围安全逻辑中的缺陷，而此前尚无系统研究探讨这一现象的安全影响。

Method: 设计并构建ParaDroid分析框架，通过将代码标准化为字节码级中间表示、重构类到源的映射关系，并利用大型语言模型推演方法语义，实现大规模识别并行方法并比较其行为差异。

Result: 在AOSP Android 14-16上评估，ParaDroid识别出329对并行方法，发现37个有安全风险的语义差异。负责任披露后，3个漏洞和2个bug已获确认，2个CVE编号已分配。

Conclusion: Java-Kotlin并行代码路径为发现现代Android系统中的安全缺陷提供了一个实际有效的检测面。

Abstract: Android has adopted Kotlin alongside Java across apps and core system components. During this shift, we observe parallel implementations in the Android Open Source Project (AOSP) where the same component is implemented in both Java and Kotlin. In principle, their functional purposes are identical. In practice, subtle semantic divergences can appear. Such divergences are not vulnerabilities by themselves, but they provide useful clues that may reveal flaws in surrounding enforcement logic. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first systematic study of Java-Kotlin parallel implementations in the Android framework and examines their security implications. We design and build ParaDroid, an analysis framework that identifies parallel methods at scale and compares their behaviors. ParaDroid normalizes code into a bytecode-level intermediate representation, reconstructs class-to-source mappings, and uses large language models to reason about method semantics and identify behavioral divergences. Evaluated on AOSP Android 14-16, ParaDroid identified 329 parallel method pairs and 37 vulnerable divergences. We responsibly disclosed the exploitable issues to the Android Security Team. Three vulnerabilities and two bugs have been confirmed, and two CVE IDs have been assigned. Our results demonstrate that parallel Java-Kotlin code paths provide a practical surface for discovering security flaws in modern Android.

</details>


### [70] [Verifiable and Confidential DNN Inference on Low-End Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07470)
*Mohamed Khalil Kiri,Ivan De Oliveira Nunes,Aurélien Francillon,Norrathep Rattanavipanon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VECODI是一个框架，通过引入SHANGRI-LA执行抽象（基于TrustZone-M TEE），在低端边缘设备上实现机密且可验证的DNN推理，具有较小的TCB、内存占用和运行时开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低端边缘设备上部署DNN推理面临两大挑战：保护模型机密性（防止被攻陷的边缘系统窃取）和实现可验证推理（验证推理结果正确性），现有方法要么依赖TEE导致高成本和与应用相关的TCB，要么在非可信环境中执行缺乏安全性。

Method: 提出SHANGRI-LA执行抽象，在TrustZone-M TEE上建立介于安全世界和非安全世界之间的第三运行时环境；VECODI利用SHANGRI-LA在非安全世界中执行不受信任的推理代码，同时使用最小化的与应用无关的安全世界支持来保护模型机密性和实现推理结果的可验证性。

Result: 在真实NUCLEO-L552ZE-Q开发板上实现并开源原型，实验结果表明VECODI具有较小的TCB、内存占用和运行时开销。

Conclusion: VECODI为低端边缘设备上的安全推理提供了一种实用的方案。

Abstract: Deploying deep neural network (DNN) inference on low-end edge devices raises two key challenges: protecting model confidentiality against a potentially compromised edge system and enabling verifiable inference without incurring prohibitive overhead. Existing approaches either house partial models and inference software within trusted execution environments (TEEs), resulting in high cost and an application-dependent trusted computing base (TCB), or execute in untrusted environments, providing little security.
  In this work, we present VECODI, a framework for verifiable and confidential DNN inference on constrained edge devices. At its core, VECODI introduces SHANGRI-LA, a new execution abstraction on TrustZone-M TEEs that establishes a third runtime environment with privileges strictly between the Secure and Non-Secure Worlds. VECODI leverages SHANGRI-LA to execute untrusted inference code in the Non-Secure World while using minimal application-agnostic Secure-World support to protect model confidentiality and enable verifiability (with respect to proper execution of inference code and model parameters) of inference results. We realize VECODI on a real-world NUCLEO-L552ZE-Q development board and open-source its prototype. Our results show VECODI's small TCB, memory footprint, and runtime overhead, making it a practical option for secure inference in low-end edge devices.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [71] [Belief-Aware Scheduling for Predictive Wildfire Hazard Mapping under Sparse-Window Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06917)
*Xun Shao,Kohsuke Yamakawa,Cheah Wai Shiang*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文针对边缘节点监测野火时下行链路容量受限的问题，提出将问题形式化为部分可观测的序贯分配问题，通过结构化信念、调度器与三大动作轴（感知、表示、传输）的协同设计，在物理校准的合成环境中验证了信念与调度问题正确建模时，轻量级架构即可取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘节点监测野火时，下行链路受限于窗口周期P、每窗口容量C等约束，无法传输全部观测数据；接收端需基于有限传输内容预测H步后的灾害图。现有研究多关注神经架构选择，但未系统解决"如何构建结构化信念以支撑接收端预测任务，并通过调度器维护该信念"这一核心设计问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为部分可观测的序贯分配问题，包含三个耦合的每个区域动作轴（感知、表示、传输）；从H步前向算子的输入需求推导结构化信念的每个组件；构建物理校准的合成环境以独立控制窗口周期P、每窗口容量C、预测视野H及燃料组成；设计轻量级跨区域注意力编码器（40k参数）。

Result: 非短视的活动 paced 参考与均匀 pacing 之间的差距在窗口周期稀疏性上呈单峰分布，峰值在中等间隔处；消融实验表明结构化信念的主宰组件在默认景观（时间陈旧性）与结构化景观（静态风险先验）之间翻转，而逐单元强度信念在两者中均冗余；40k参数轻量级跨区域注意力编码器在默认景观上超过FAIR活动paced参考约28%，在结构化景观上约11%；更深的Transformer编码器在平均预测损失上未改善，且训练种子方差更高。

Conclusion: 在该任务类与实验设定下，当信念与调度问题被正确构造时，适度的架构归纳偏置便已足够，无需更深层网络。

Abstract: An edge node monitoring a wildfire observes more than a duty-limited or windowed down-link can carry. The receiver must predict the H-step-ahead hazard map from whatever the link delivers. We argue the operative design problem is not which neural architecture to use but how to derive a structured belief sufficient for the receiver's prediction task and maintain it through a scheduler that anticipates future transmission opportunities. We formalize this as a partially observed sequential allocation problem with three coupled per-region action axes (sensing, representation, transmission), and derive each component of the structured belief from the H-step forward operator's input requirements. Identifying these mechanisms requires independent control over the window period P, per-window capacity C, predictive horizon H, and fuel composition, which is not separable in real-landscape data; we therefore evaluate on a physics-calibrated synthetic environment. Three empirical observations support the principle: the gap between a non-myopic activity-paced reference and uniform pacing is unimodal in window-period sparsity, peaking at intermediate spacing; ablating the structured belief, the dominant operative component flips between a default landscape (temporal staleness) and a structured landscape (static-risk prior), while the per-cell intensity belief is redundant in both; and a 40 k-parameter lightweight cross-region attention encoder exceeds the FAIR activity-paced reference by ~28% on the default landscape and ~11% on the structured landscape. A deeper Transformer encoder does not improve over the lightweight encoder in mean predictive loss and exhibits higher training-seed variance. Within this task class and regime, a modest architectural inductive bias suffices when the belief and the scheduling problem are correctly posed.

</details>


### [72] [The Three-Ring Architecture: Governing Agents in the Era of On-Platform Organisations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07119)
*Sergio Alvarez-Telena,Marta Diez-Fernandez*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出三环架构（Three-Ring Architecture）作为企业级AI代理治理的基础设施：环1为现有生产架构，环2为基于策略的代理联邦层（M2），环3为LLM前沿智能层。核心论点：环2是环3非确定性风险的必要治理条件，LLM能力越强，治理需求越大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前企业AI部署面临结构性失败——组织获得了代理能力却没有治理基础设施，预期将重蹈第一波AI部署的覆辙（去中心化智能缺乏联邦层导致95%项目失败率）。

Method: 形式化定义了三种环及各自风险特征：环2（策略代理）运行在确定性框架内，后果可追溯、权限可执行、偏差可恢复；环3（LLM代理）是非确定性行动者，偏差在复杂组织中传播且无法回溯追踪。

Result: 环2在技术严格意义上构成了代理型企业的操作系统，在组织层面执行计算OS在设备层面的功能：资源抽象、进程协调、权限执行、复合智能的稳定平台。

Conclusion: 环2不是有用补充而是控制与合规的必要条件；每一轮LLM能力提升都是该架构的结构性顺风车——非确定性行动者的能力越强，偏离时产生的后果越大，治理需求随能力扩展而扩展。

Abstract: The current phase of enterprise AI deployment faces a structural failure: organisations are acquiring agentic capability without the infrastructure to govern it. The result is expected to reproduce the error of the first wave of AI deployment: decentralised intelligence without a federation layer leading to a 95% project failure rate. This paper formalises the Three-Ring Architecture as the governing infrastructure of the on-platform organisation. Ring 1 is the existing production architecture; Ring 2 is the M2 federation layer built on strategies-based agentic AI; Ring 3 is the LLM-based frontier intelligence layer. Ring 2 constitutes, in the technically exact sense, the operating system of the agentic enterprise - performing at the organisational level what a computing OS performs at the device level: resource abstraction, process coordination, permission enforcement, and a stable platform for compounding intelligence. A central contribution is the formal distinction between Ring 2 and Ring 3 risk profiles. Strategies-based agents operate within a deterministic framework: their consequences are traceable, their permissions enforceable, their deviations recoverable. LLM-based agents introduce a categorically distinct risk: a non-deterministic actor whose deviations propagate through complex organisational systems without retrospective traceability. Ring 2 is not a useful addition - it is a necessary condition of control and compliance. A further consequence: every improvement in LLM capability is a structural tailwind for this architecture. More capable non-deterministic actors produce larger consequences when they deviate. The governance requirement scales with capability. The architecture has been validated across a decade of deployment in financial services, government, procurement, and compliance among other sectors.

</details>


### [73] [Distributed Persistence Domain for Persistent Memory Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07159)
*Khan Shaikhul Hadi,Andres David Delgado,Naveed Ul Mustafa,Mark Heinrich,Hao Zheng,Yan Solihin*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式持久域（DPD）抽象，通过在CXL交换机中集成持久性支持来显著降低持久内存池化的延迟，同时保证正确性和崩溃一致性。模拟结果显示，相比易失性CXL交换机，平均加速33%，读转发优化下最高加速36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CXL内存池化引入显著的持久性延迟，因为持久操作必须穿越整个CXL结构（包括交换机、链路和协议层）才能到达远程持久内存。现有方案缺乏对分布式结构中持久性正确性的保障。

Method: 提出分布式持久域（DPD）抽象，形式化定义分布式持久域的概念，识别正确性风险，推导设计需求。在此基础上设计持久CXL交换机架构，支持持久性操作、读转发和写合并，同时保证崩溃一致性。

Result: 使用SPLASH-4和YCSB基准测试进行模拟，相比易失性CXL交换机平均加速33%，读转发优化下最高加速36%。

Conclusion: 将持久性支持拓展到CXL交换机级别是提升持久内存池化可扩展性的有效方向，提出的DPD抽象和持久CXL交换机设计能在显著降低延迟的同时保证正确性。

Abstract: Compute Express Link (CXL) enables memory pooling over disaggregated memory, offering the potential to improve resource utilization in persistent memory systems. However, integrating persistence semantics into CXL-based memory pooling introduces substantial latency, which limits system scalability. This overhead arises because persist operations must traverse the entire CXL fabric, including switches, links, and protocol layers, before reaching remote persistent memory. To this end, we argue that extending CXL switches with persistence support is a promising direction for improving the scalability of persistent memory pooling. However, moving persistence support into the network breaks the traditional correctness assumptions of centralized persistence domains. In particular, enabling persistence within distributed structures, such as CXL switches, can introduce stale reads and writes if not carefully coordinated.
  In this paper, we propose Distributed Persistence Domain (DPD), a new abstraction for persistent memory pooling that enables persistence support at the CXL switch level. We first formalize the concept of a distributed persistence domain and use DPD as a framework to identify the correctness hazards that arise when persistence structures are distributed across the CXL fabric. Based on this analysis, we derive the design requirements needed to guarantee correctness. Building on these insights, we present Persistent CXL Switch, a CXL switch architecture that incorporates persistence support to significantly reduce persist latency, enable read forwarding, and coalesce writes, while preserving correctness and crash consistency. We evaluated our system design using both SPLASH-4 and YCSB benchmarks. Simulation results show an average speedup of 33% over volatile CXL switches, and up to 36% speedup with read forwarding optimization across all workloads.

</details>

### [74] [LinkNav: Surfacing Interconnected Information in Scientific Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06650)
*Sebastian Joseph,Jennifer Healey,Junyi Jessy Li,Ani Nenkova*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: LinkNav 是一种增强学术论文阅读体验的方法，通过让语言模型生成阅读过程中可能产生的问题，并在文档其他位置搜索答案，建立非相邻段落之间的显式连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术论文中相关但非相邻的段落之间缺乏显式连接，读者在阅读时容易忽略这些跨段落关联。

Method: 使用语言模型为当前段落生成可能引发的问题，然后在文档其他位置搜索答案段落，建立文档内部的跨段落连接。

Result: 答案检测管道具有高精度，能够为文档生成合理数量的连接；连接段落平均相隔10个段落的距离。

Conclusion: LinkNav 能够有效建立学术论文中非相邻段落之间的显式连接，帮助读者发现可能被忽略的关联信息。

Abstract: We present LinkNav, an enhanced experience for reading academic papers which makes explicit connections between related but non-adjacent passages. To create the experience, we instruct a language model to generate questions that may arise while reading a passage and then search for answer passages elsewhere in the document, forming intra-document connections when answers are found. We confirm that these building blocks work well to power the experience, with an answer detection pipeline that works with high precision, resulting in a reasonable number of connections being made for a document. On a dataset of academic papers, we find that connected passages are on average ten segments away from each other, making explicit connections that a reader may have otherwise missed.

</details>


### [75] [Adversarial Co-Thinking: Calibration and Triangulation Across Multiple GenAI Tools in HCI Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06702)
*Pia Tukkinen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将GenAI工具深度嵌入学术论文起草全过程（而非仅用于后期润色）的影响。作者通过自身使用Claude、ChatGPT和Gemini三个工具平行撰写论文的实践，提出了一种名为"对抗性共思"（adversarial co-thinking）的工作模式：利用过往同行评审校准工具，并将各工具的输出相互对质以检验而非盲从。作者认为，让默认倾向赞美的AI工具产生真正批判性反馈是核心实践挑战，关键技能在于评估而非生成。这种模式是高技能认知实践，能放大既有专长，也可能掩盖能力缺失。当前披露框架难以捕捉这种转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当GenAI工具完全嵌入学术论文的早期起草阶段（而非仅限于后期润色）时，高强度多工具GenAI工作流与传统学术写作有何不同，以及这种转变对学术实践带来的挑战。

Method: 作者采用自我实验法，从论文第一句话开始，同时使用Claude、ChatGPT、Gemini三个GenAI工具并行起草，并持续将自己的预期贡献与各工具的输出进行对比分析。

Result: 发现一种反复出现的模式——"对抗性共思"（adversarial co-thinking）：利用过往同行评审校准工具，将各工具的输出相互对质以检验而非盲从。核心挑战在于让默认赞美模式的工具产生真正的批判性反馈。

Conclusion: 对抗性共思是高技能认知实践，能放大既有专长但也能掩盖能力缺失；当前披露框架难以捕捉这种从"生成"到"评估"的技能转变；提出关于自主性、监督、公平获取和披露的四项命题供研讨。

Abstract: This paper examines what happens when GenAI tools are fully embedded in the drafting of an academic paper rather than confined to late-stage polishing. To investigate how an intensive multi-tool GenAI workflow differs from conventional academic writing, I drafted this paper from the first sentence in parallel with three GenAI tools - Claude, ChatGPT, and Gemini - comparing their outputs against my own intended contribution. Across this process, a recurring pattern took shape that I call adversarial co-thinking: using past peer reviews to calibrate the tools, then setting their outputs against one another to be tested rather than deferred to. I argue that surfacing genuine critique from tools that default to praise is a central practical challenge of working with these tools, and that the skill at stake is evaluative rather than generative. Adversarial co-thinking is a high-skill epistemic practice: it can amplify expertise where it exists, but it can also mask its absence. I further argue that current disclosure frameworks are poorly equipped to capture this shift. The paper offers four propositions for workshop discussion concerning autonomy, supervision, equity of access, and disclosure.

</details>


### [76] [Exploring Reinforcement Learning for Fluid Transitions Between Clinical Mental Healthcare and Everyday Wellness Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06800)
*Tony Wang,Qian Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探索使用强化学习（RL）构建整合临床与健康干预的数字健康系统，通过上下文Bandit动态选择日记提示，在4周探索性研究（N=38）中发现：RL优化干预序列的益处多在干预结束后显现；RL生成干预的高参与用户参与度随时间加深，而恒定干预用户易倦怠退出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康问题具有波动性，但临床干预与健康干预通常分离运作，导致护理过渡时频繁出现中断。作者探索RL作为构建数字健康系统的方法，以主动提供连贯的临床与健康干预服务。

Method: 构建上下文Bandit模型，动态从临床和健康干预库中选择日记提示，优化总体健康目标（持续日记记录）。部署为期4周的探索性研究（N=38）。

Result: 1) RL优化干预序列的许多益处仅在干预结束后显现；2) RL生成干预的高参与用户参与度随时间加深，而恒定干预用户倾向于倦怠并在后期退出。

Conclusion: 提出了两个关键设计问题：连贯的临床-健康护理旅程是否应包含"退步期"？何时应降低干预强度以防止倦怠，何时应维持强度以最大化治疗效果？

Abstract: Mental health struggles wax and wane, yet clinical and wellness interventions typically operate separately, causing frequent breakdowns at care transitions. We explore reinforcement learning (RL) as a means to build digital health systems that deliver clinical and wellness interventions proactively, as part of a coherent care journey. We ask: what complexities does designing such a system involve? We built a contextual bandit that dynamically selects journaling prompts from clinical and wellness repertoires to optimize for an overarching health goal (sustained journaling) and deployed it in a four-week exploratory study (N=38). We found that, first, many benefits of RL-optimized intervention sequences appeared only after interventions ended, raising the question: Should systems that offer coherent clinical-wellness care journeys include stepping-back periods? If so, when and how? Second, participants most engaged with RL-generated interventions deepened their engagement over time, while those most engaged with a constant intervention tended to burn out and drop out later. It raises the question: When should a system blending clinical and wellness interventions reduce intensity to prevent burnout in versus sustain it to maximize treatment gains?

</details>


### [77] [Personality Anchoring for Social Simulation: Linking Personality, Social Behavior, and Interaction Success with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06936)
*Vahid Sadiri Javadi,Aksa Aksa,Fryderyk Róg,Lucie Flek,Johanne R. Trippas*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过CHARISMA框架的人物性格锚定方法，利用知名电影角色和公众人物作为智能体，模拟了1,010场对话，研究宜人性（Agreeableness）配对如何影响社交互动结果。研究发现：高宜人性配对的目标达成率（62%）远高于低宜人性配对（6%），且宜人性通过合作策略选择部分中介影响结果，但还存在超越可观察对话行为的其他路径。重复模拟结果高度一致（ICC=0.89），验证了该方法作为性格操作化策略的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究难以系统性地探究个体间性格配置如何在不同社交情境中共同影响社会行为，缺乏方法论上的有效手段。

Method: 采用CHARISMA框架改编的模拟流程，利用电影角色和公众人物作为心理有依据的智能体，通过性格锚定方法进行多LLM社会模拟，开展1,010场对话的大规模实证研究。

Result: 二元宜人性组合与共同目标达成呈单调正相关：同质高宜人性配对成功率为62%，同质低宜人性配对仅6%。宜人性通过合作策略选择部分中介目标达成，但即使在相同主导策略内仍能预测结果。重复模拟ICC=0.89，性格表达跨场景稳定。

Conclusion: 性格锚定是一种有效的性格操作化策略，宜人性对社交结果的影响既有通过合作策略的间接路径，也有超出可观察行为的直接路径。

Abstract: Social interactions are shaped by the interplay of dispositional traits and situational context, yet systematically investigating how personality configurations between individuals jointly influence social behavior across diverse social contexts remains methodologically challenging. We address this gap by introducing a simulation pipeline adapted from the CHARISMA framework, which employs well-known movie characters and public figures as psychologically grounded agents for multi-LLM social simulation using a method we term personality anchoring. We present a large-scale empirical study examining how dyadic Agreeableness composition influences social interaction outcomes across 1,010 simulated conversations. Our results reveal a monotonic relationship between dyadic Agreeableness composition and shared goal achievement, with Homogeneous-Agreeable pairs achieving success 10 times the rate of Homogeneous-Disagreeable pairs (62% vs. 6%). Behavioral mediation analysis reveals that Agreeableness shapes goal achievement partially through cooperative strategy selection, though it continues to predict outcomes within the same dominant strategy, indicating pathways beyond observable conversational behavior. Robustness analyses confirm high consistency of results across repeated simulations (ICC = 0.89) and stable personality expression across diverse scenarios, validating personality anchoring as a viable operationalization strategy.

</details>


### [78] [CANote: Empowering Fact-checking Note Writing Through Scaffolded and Provenance-based Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07101)
*Shuning Zhang,Jingruo Chen,Yuwei Chuai,Dai Shi,Yifan Wang,Xin Yi,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CANote是一个AI辅助的辟谣笔记撰写系统，通过证据关联和结构化协同起草机制，帮助用户（尤其是非专业用户）写出与专家水平相当的高质量辟谣内容，但存在用户所有权和掌控感降低的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包事实核查机制（如X平台的Community Notes）对遏制 misinformation 传播至关重要，但撰写高质量、基于证据的辟谣笔记给贡献者带来了巨大负担。

Method: 提出CANote系统，采用证据关联（从社交媒体帖子中提取子声明，并提供子声明与检索证据之间的显式链接）和结构化协同起草（生成中立的结构化草稿以支持人类推理）的方法，将工作流程分解为可操作的步骤。

Result: 在模拟X平台上对52名专业事实核查员和52名普通用户进行实验评估，CANote显著提高了笔记质量；使普通用户能写出与专家水平相当的笔记；任务完成时间和认知负荷与手动撰写相当；用户满意度显著提升；但用户所有权和掌控感有所降低。

Conclusion: AI辅助工具CANote能有效降低众包事实核查的参与门槛，提升辟谣笔记质量，尤其是赋能非专业用户；但设计时需权衡用户对内容的控制感与辅助效果之间的关系。

Abstract: Crowdsourced fact-checking mechanisms, such as X's Community Notes, play a critical role in mitigating the spread of misinformation. However, drafting high-quality, evidence-based debunking notes imposes a substantial burden on contributors. We present CANote, an AI-assisted debunking note writing system featuring evidence correlation and structured co-drafting. CANote scaffolds the workflow by extracting subclaims from social media posts, providing provenance through explicit links between subclaims and retrieved evidence, and generating neutral, structural drafts to support human reasoning. We evaluated CANote against manual writing (N=52 fact-checkers, N=52 lay users) on simulated X platform, where we found CANote significantly improves note quality. Notably, CANote enables lay users to write notes that have comparable quality to those written by experts. While the task completion time and perceived cognitive load remain comparable to manual drafting, CANote significantly increases user satisfaction. However, this assistance introduces a trade-off, resulting in a reduced sense of user ownership and control over the debunking note.

</details>


### [79] [Moodie: An Early-Stage Design Exploration for Supporting Fear of Missing Out with LLM-based Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07231)
*Hsin-Yu Tsai,Jingxian Liao,Fu-Yin Cherng,Tzu-Hsiang Huang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于大语言模型的聊天机器人Moodie，旨在帮助用户调节情绪并减少社交媒体使用中的错失恐惧症（FoMO）。通过形成性研究和为期一周的初步评估（N=21），结果表明Moodie在减少FoMO方面与基线聊天机器人（GPT-4o）效果相当，但在用户参与度和社交连接感方面表现更优，引发了对专用聊天机器人与通用模型在心理健康支持中的优势比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏关注FoMO情绪和认知方面的可访问、交互式工具，因此需要开发专门针对FoMO的情绪调节支持工具。

Method: 通过形成性研究了解FoMO用户需求，开发基于大语言模型的聊天机器人Moodie，然后进行为期一周的初步评估研究（N=21），对比Moodie与基线聊天机器人（GPT-4o）的效果。

Result: Moodie与基线聊天机器人在减少FoMO方面效果相似，但Moodie在用户参与度和社交连接感方面表现更好。

Conclusion: 专用聊天机器人在心理健康支持中可能比通用模型具有优势，未来研究将包括聊天记录分析、原型优化和纵向评估。

Abstract: The excessive use of social media has led to the challenge known as Fear of Missing Out (FoMO). Existing studies fail to provide accessible, interactive tools that focus on the emotional and cognitive aspects of FoMO. This work presents Moodie, a chatbot designed using Large Language Models to support emotion regulation and reduce FoMO. We conducted a formative study to understand the needs of individuals with FoMO and developed Moodie. Then, we conducted a preliminary evaluative study (N=21) to observe how participants interact with Moodie and a baseline chatbot (GPT-4o) over one week. The results show that while both Moodie and a baseline chatbot reduced FoMO to a similar extent, Moodie resulted in greater engagement and social connection. This finding raises interesting questions about the advantages of purpose-built chatbots compared to general-purpose models for mental health support. Future research will include chat log analysis, prototype refinements, and longitudinal evaluations.

</details>


### [80] [A Model of Integrated Information Processing in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07283)
*Tim Schrills. Thomas Franke*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出集成信息处理（IIP）模型，一个以任务为中心、基于控制论的人机协作框架，通过统一建模语言描述人类与AI的耦合控制回路，定义输入充分性、参考一致性、输出可操作性三种信息处理质量，为人机交互界面设计与评估提供心理学理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HAII研究缺乏从心理学视角出发、以任务为中心的过程导向理论框架，未能将行动调节的认知机制与界面设计的具体杠杆联系起来；同时缺乏统一的人机建模语言，且现有模型只描述系统设计方式而未揭示其对人类行为的影响。

Method: 基于控制论构建IIP模型，将人类与AI系统均视为耦合的控制回路，使用统一建模语言表达人类与人工智能体的行动调节过程；通过三种信息处理质量（输入充分性、参考一致性、输出可操作性）刻画共享任务中的协同效能。

Result: IIP模型能够将界面设计选择（如XAI技术）映射到理论驱动的用户行为预期，指导界面设计与评估；模型覆盖三种信息处理质量，并已在示例用例中展示其对界面设计的启示。

Conclusion: IIP模型填补了从心理学视角连接认知机制与界面设计的理论空白，为日益自主化的AI系统的人机协同提供了可操作的设计与评估框架。

Abstract: For Human-AI Interaction (HAII) research to move forward, theoretical work linking psychological mechanisms to interface design is needed. Such work should extend rather than replace established HCI and automation research, adapting to the increasing autonomy and agency of AI systems. Building on prior frameworks focused on roles and levels in human interaction with automation, a gap remains from a psychological view: a task-centered, process-oriented account that links mechanisms of action regulation to concrete design and evaluation levers for human-AI coupling, expressed in a unified vocabulary for human and machine. Moreover, existing models may describe how a system is designed (e.g., function allocation in automation) but fall short in showing how this design affects human behavior. We present the Integrated Information Processing (IIP) model, a task-centered, cybernetic model that conceptualizes humans, machines, and their joint activity as coupled control loops. The IIP model uses a unified modeling language for human and artificial agents, making psychological models of action regulation accessible for AI system design. As a core feature, we argue that efficacy within a shared task is characterized by three integration qualities, input adequacy, reference consonance, and output operativity, which critically influence benchmarks of human-centeredness such as transparency and controllability. The model maps interface choices (e.g., XAI techniques) to theory-driven expectations of user behavior, guiding interface design and evaluation. To this end, we present (1) a continuity-preserving theoretical discourse that extends HAII to agency in AI; (2) the IIP model with three information-processing qualities; and (3) applications of the IIP model to exemplary use cases demonstrating implications for interface design.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [Depth over Fidelity in Fixed-Budget Noisy Evolution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06555)
*Sichen Wang,Zhipeng Lu*

Main category: cs.NE
