Moreover, debate is a data-rich domain, with a large volume of publicly accessible materials. Current mainstream automated scoring methods are poorly suited to complex settings such as debate, and therefore still rely on costly human evaluation. To this end, this paper proposes DEFINED, a data-efficient computational framework for fine-grained creativity assessment in debate scenarios. DEFINED operationalizes debate creativity through a hierarchical eight-dimensional metric system, implemented via a pre-trained autoregressive language model with a hierarchical scoring head that supports both fine-grained and coarse-grained evaluation. Statements and their associated expert scores were obtained from authentic debate competitions, and a constrained data augmentation strategy was employed to address the elite bias inherent in the original data. DEFINED adopts a mixed-granularity training strategy enabling robust learning from limited fine-grained supervision annotated by trained graduate experts. To rigorously validate ecological validity beyond synthetic benchmarks, we incorporate an empirical study with debate-naive participants, utilizing these authentic data to serve as a qualitative case study for mid-to-low proficiency populations. Across our evaluation protocol, our scoring model achieves accurate and stable scoring, outperforming prompt-based large language model evaluators and existing debate scoring methods.

</details>


### [155] [Generative Molecular Morphing for Flexible-Size Design via Unbalanced Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07239)
*Malte Franke,Stefan P. Schmid,Zarko Ivkovic,Kjell Jorner,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Morph是一种基于几何图的可变尺寸3D分子生成模型，通过动态调整原子数量来捕捉分子性质与尺寸的联合分布，在保持与固定尺寸模型相当性能的同时，实现了前所未有的采样灵活性，并能进行超出分布的分子生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型和流模型在分子生成中固定原子数量，无法准确捕捉分子性质与尺寸之间的内在关联，限制了模型在高奖励样本空间中的可操控性。

Method: 提出Morph模型，基于几何图的动态尺寸生成框架，支持条件和无条件3D分子设计，通过动态自适应原子数量，可无缝整合现有结构先验（如骨架）。

Result: Morph在性能上与当前最先进的固定尺寸模型相当，同时提供了无与伦比的采样灵活性，能够在先前模型失败的分布外生成任务中成功生成分子。

Conclusion: Morph通过灵活尺寸生成策略有效解决了固定尺寸模型的局限性，为增强分子设计的生成建模开辟了新路径。

Abstract: The success of generative molecular design hinges on a model's steerability toward high-reward samples. Because many molecular properties are intrinsically linked to molecular size, accurately capturing the joint distribution of properties and the number of atoms is essential. However, current diffusion and flow-based models fix the number of atoms, which ultimately limits their ability to navigate this complex relationship. To address this, we introduce Morph, a flexible-size generative model for conditional and unconditional 3D molecular design based on geometric graphs. By dynamically adapting size, Morph can seamlessly integrate existing structural priors, like scaffolds, and significantly enhances property steering. We show that Morph matches current fixed-size state-of-the-art models while offering the benefit of unparalleled sampling flexibility. We demonstrate out-of-distribution generation in regimes where previous models fail, paving the way for enhanced generative modeling for molecular design.

</details>


### [156] [A Held-Out Transition-Pair Falsifier for Long-Horizon Non-Abelian State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07254)
*Jeonghoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于有限非阿贝尔群状态跟踪的留出转移对伪造协议，证明了投影循环状态模型在S₃×S₃基准上，仅通过长度8序列训练即可实现长达1,048,576 token的完美零错误最终状态预测，而多种基线模型在此协议下均表现接近随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列模型在状态跟踪任务中面临根本性局限：相关信号并非观察token的摘要，而是通过非交换变换演化的有序潜在状态。现有序列模型难以处理非交换群组跟踪问题，因为局部变换的记忆捷径在长程推理中失效。

Method: 设计了一种留出转移对伪造协议，禁止在训练中出现特定的有序生成器对，但在评估时要求相同的局部模式，阻断直接局部转移记忆通路。在S₃×S₃群组的受控基准上，使用投影循环状态模型进行训练和评估，并与多种基线（bag、GRU、结构化状态空间模型等）进行比较。

Result: 投影循环状态模型在5个随机种子下，对于长达1,048,576 token的评估视界实现250/250的完美最终状态预测。而所有基线（包括原生读出和投影匹配的变体）在此协议下均接近随机水平。机制诊断显示硬投影与低同态误差、低状态一致性漂移和非平凡交换子分离相关。

Conclusion: 明确的投影非交换状态组合在长视界隐藏状态跟踪中是有用的归纳偏置，但其有效性仅限于该受控的有限群伪造器基准，不能推广为通用的架构排名结论。

Abstract: State tracking exposes a sharp limitation of sequence models: the relevant signal is often not a summary of observed tokens, but an ordered latent state that evolves through non-commutative transformations. We introduce a held-out transition-pair falsifier for finite non-Abelian group tracking. The protocol forbids selected ordered generator pairs during training and requires the same local patterns during evaluation, blocking one direct local-transition memorization pathway. In a controlled $S_3 \times S_3$ benchmark, a projected recurrent state model trained only on length-8 sequences produces error-free final-state predictions (perfect 250/250 per horizon) through evaluation horizons up to 1,048,576 tokens across five seeds. Matched native-readout baselines, including bag, GRU, and a single-configuration structured state-space model, remain near floor under the same protocol. Projection-matched GRU, structured SSM, and bag baselines equipped with analogous finite-group prototype readouts also remain near chance under the same split. Mechanism diagnostics show that hard projection coincides with low homomorphism error, low state-consistency drift, and non-trivial commutator separation, while softened projection collapses final-state accuracy. Clean-split audits verify zero verbatim reduced-word overlap and zero structural-template overlap between training and evaluation partitions. The evidence is scoped to this controlled finite-group falsifier rather than to a general architecture ranking. Within that regime, explicit projected non-commutative state composition acts as a useful inductive bias for long-horizon hidden-state tracking.

</details>


### [157] [Where Rectified Flows Leak: Characterising Membership Signals Along the Interpolation Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07271)
*Thomas Sesmat,Gabriel Meseguer-Brocal,Geoffroy Peeters*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Rectified Flow模型对训练数据的记忆痕迹，发现沿插值路径λ存在一个钟形重构误差差异曲线，该差异在训练过程中累积但验证指标保持稳定。作者在理论上推导了高斯假设下的峰值位置，并在音频和图像上验证了该现象的普适性，最后利用这一λ分辨结构实现了成员推断攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解生成模型从训练数据中保留了什么信息是一个具有挑战性的问题，对版权和隐私有重要影响。除了逐字逐句的复制外，模型可能编码更微妙的训练数据痕迹，这些痕迹不会出现在输出中但可被利用。本研究旨在分析和利用Rectified Flows中的这种隐藏数据记忆。

Method: 分析了Rectified Flow训练中定义的插值路径X_λ = (1-λ)X_0 + λX_1，研究了沿λ维度训练数据与测试数据重构误差的差异，在高斯假设下推导了该差异峰值的闭式解，并在音频和图像数据集上进行了实验验证。

Result: 发现训练数据和测试数据重构误差之间存在钟形曲线差异，该差异在训练过程中累积而验证指标保持稳定。高斯假设下推导的峰值位置预测在假设满足时成立，钟形结构在不同数据模态下具有普适性。

Conclusion: 这种λ分辨的钟形结构可以用于成员推断攻击，有效区分训练集成员和非成员，揭示了Rectified Flow模型对训练数据的隐式记忆机制。

Abstract: Understanding what generative models retain from training data remains challenging, with implications for copyright and privacy. Beyond verbatim reproduction, models can encode subtler traces of their training data that never surface in their outputs yet remain exploitable. We study this regime for Rectified Flows, which are increasingly used in deployed generative systems. We analyse the interpolation path $X_λ= (1-λ)X_0 + λX_1$ that defines the Rectified Flow training. We show that a gap exists between the reconstruction of train and test data that follows a bell-shaped curve over $λ$, wich accumulates during training, while the validation metrics remain stable. The signal has a maximum whose location we derive in closed form under Gaussian assumptions. We validate these predictions on both audio and images and show that the bell-shaped structure is universal, while the peak prediction holds when our assumptions are satisfied. As a proof of concept, we exploit this specific $λ$-resolved structure to perform a Membership Inference Attack, distinguishing members of the training set from non-members.

</details>


### [158] [Closed-Form Spectral Regularization for Multi-Task Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07289)
*Yongxian Wei,Runxi Cheng,Xingxuan Zhang,Li Shen,Chun Yuan,Peng Cui,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多任务模型合并形式化为一个带噪线性逆问题，并基于谱滤波估计器提出SWUDI方法（软指数滤波+硬Top-K截断），在保持或超越SOTA性能的同时，将合并时间减少28-72倍，GPU内存降低最多50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法将合并形式化为逐层二次干扰最小化问题，虽存在闭式伪逆解，但实际效果远不如数百次梯度下降迭代。这种迭代过程占主要计算成本但其有效性未被解释，需要揭示其内在机制并设计更高效的合并方法。

Method: 1) 将多任务模型合并重新形式化为带噪线性逆问题，指出迭代求解器本质是隐式谱正则化器；2) 提出谱滤波估计器SWUDI：软指数滤波匹配梯度流轨迹，硬Top-K截断抑制小特征值方向的噪声放大；3) 提出自适应变体SWUDI-A，用逐层秩规则替换全局超参数。

Result: 在4个通用基准和多模态合并基准（VQA、Geometry、Chart、OCR、Grounding、模态合并）上，SWUDI匹配或超越SOTA合并方法；合并时间减少28-72倍，峰值GPU内存降低最多50%。

Conclusion: 模型合并中的迭代求解器本质不是优化器，而是隐式谱正则化器。基于此见解提出的闭式谱滤波方法SWUDI实现了更高效、更鲁棒的多任务模型合并。

Abstract: Model merging combines several independently fine-tuned experts into a single multi-task model without any training data, reducing the storage, serving, and decentralized-development costs of large foundation models. State-of-the-art merging methods formulate merging as a layer-wise quadratic interference minimization problem. Although this problem admits an exact closed-form pseudoinverse solution, that solution underperforms hundreds of iterations of gradient descent in practice. The iterative loop dominates the cost of the pipeline, yet its effectiveness has remained unexplained. We revisit this regime and show that the iterative solver does not primarily act as an optimizer; rather, it serves as an implicit spectral regularizer for an ill-posed normal equation, where small-eigenvalue directions of the per-layer interference operator amplify proxy noise. Building on this finding, we formalize multi-task model merging as a noisy linear inverse problem and propose a spectral filtering estimator parameterized by a per-direction filter. We instantiate this estimator with SWUDI, a closed-form method that combines a soft exponential filter, which matches the gradient-flow trajectory of iterative descent, with a hard top-K truncation that suppresses noise-amplifying small-eigenvalue directions. Furthermore, we propose SWUDI-A, an adaptive variant that replaces the global rank hyperparameter with per-layer rank rules, further improving robustness across architectures. Both variants share a single symmetric eigendecomposition per linear layer and require no training data or optimizer state. Across four general benchmarks and a multimodal merging benchmark spanning VQA, Geometry, Chart, OCR, Grounding, and modality merging, our proposed spectral solvers match or outperform state-of-the-art merging methods. Crucially, they reduce wall-clock time by 28-72x and peak GPU memory by up to 50%.

</details>


### [159] [Trio: Learning Time-Series Forecasting with Temporal-Spatial-Sample Attention and Structural Causal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07291)
*Tao Chen,Yexu Zhou,Zhi Gong,Hengwei He,Hongda Li,Zhewei Chen,Dongjing Wang,Xin Zhang,Decheng Liu,Chunlei Peng,Zheng Chen,Wenyue Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Trio模型，通过时序-空间-样本三重注意力机制进行多变量时间序列预测，并引入TS-SCM生成器创建结构化的合成预训练任务，实验证明该方法在多个基准上提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs) 范式难以直接迁移到时间序列预测，因为时间顺序、动态滞后和历史模式无法被普通表格先验自然捕获。本文旨在研究如何显式组织并重用历史输入-输出样本对来指导预测。

Method: 提出Trio架构，包含三种注意力机制：Temporal注意力（捕获窗口内动态）、Spatial注意力（建模变量间依赖）、Sample注意力（检索相关历史lookback-future对）。同时引入Time-Series Structural Causal Model (TS-SCM) 生成器创建带有动态滞后、跨变量交互、噪声、反馈和分布漂移的结构化合成任务。

Result: 在合成数据、工业数据和公开基准上，Trio架构均提升了预测性能。零样本实验表明TS-SCM生成的任务可提供有用的结构先验，但完全通用的PFN式时间序列预测仍是开放问题。

Conclusion: 通过显式组织和重用历史样本进行时间序列预测是可行的，Trio的样本感知注意力机制能有效提升预测性能；结构化合成数据生成器有助于提供先验知识，但通用时间序列PFN仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series forecasting requires models to reason over temporal dynamics, cross-variable dependencies, and historical input-output correspondences. Recent Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs) suggest that synthetic tasks can be useful for learning transferable inference behavior. However, directly transferring this paradigm to time-series forecasting remains difficult, since temporal order, dynamic lags, and recurring historical patterns are not naturally captured by ordinary tabular priors. Motivated by this observation, we propose Trio, a sample-aware time-series forecasting architecture based on Temporal-Spatial-Sample attention. Temporal attention captures within-window dynamics, spatial attention models inter-variable dependencies, and sample attention retrieves relevant historical lookback-future pairs to guide the current prediction. Rather than claiming a fully general PFN-style forecaster, our goal is to study how historical input-output examples can be explicitly organized and reused within a forecasting model. We further introduce a Time-Series Structural Causal Model (TS-SCM) generator to create structured synthetic forecasting tasks with dynamic lags, cross-variable interactions, noise, feedback, and distributional drift. Experiments on synthetic, industrial, and public benchmarks show that the proposed architecture improves forecasting performance. Exploratory zero-shot experiments further suggest that TS-SCM-generated tasks may provide useful structural priors, while fully general PFN-style time-series forecasting remains an open problem.

</details>


### [160] [Bootstrap Theory of Representational Emergence: Explanatory Insufficiency as a Driver of Representation Learning and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07303)
*Jacques Raynal,Pierre Slangen,Elsa Raynal,Jacques Margerit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出表征涌现的引导理论（TBER），认为新表征的产生并非仅由数据和算力驱动，更源于现有表征在解释力上的不足。该理论描述了从观测、异常、解释不充分到新表征涌现的递归过程，并应用于表征学习、基础模型、世界模型、数字孪生及科学发现等领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多关注选定表征框架后如何优化表征，而忽视了何时需要新的表征层级。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出一个系统性框架来解释新表征如何以及为何涌现。

Method: 提出TBER框架，定义解释不充分性作为表征转型的正向信号，并形式化为五阶段递归过程：稳定观测→异常检测→识别解释不充分→表征涌现→临时稳定。

Result: TBER识别出解释不充分性是表征转型的驱动因素，表明表征变得不充分不是因为其错误，而是其解释域已被超越。该框架可应用于表征学习、基础模型、世界模型、数字孪生、自适应生物系统和科学发现。

Conclusion: 未来的AI系统可能受益于检测自身内部表征解释极限的机制，TBER为设计能主动识别自身局限并进行表征创新的智能系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Representation learning is central to modern machine learning, enabling transitions from handcrafted features to learned embeddings, latent spaces, foundation models, world models, and digital twins. Yet most research examines how representations are optimized after a representational framework has been selected, while less attention is given to when a new level of representation becomes necessary. We introduce the Bootstrap Theory of Representational Emergence (TBER), a framework describing how new representations arise when existing ones become explanatorily insufficient. In this view, representational innovation is not only driven by more data, larger models, or greater computational power, but also by persistent explanatory gaps: situations in which a representation can still describe observations but can no longer make their organization or transformations intelligible. TBER identifies explanatory insufficiency as a positive signal for representational transition. A representation becomes insufficient not because it is necessarily false, but because its explanatory domain has been exceeded. The bootstrap dynamic follows a recursive sequence: observations reveal anomalies; anomalies expose insufficiencies; insufficiencies motivate new representations; and these new representations generate further observations and possible new insufficiencies.We formalize this process through five stages: stabilized observation, anomaly detection, recognition of explanatory insufficiency, representational emergence, and provisional stabilization. We discuss applications to representation learning, latent spaces, foundation models, world models, digital twins, adaptive biological systems, and scientific discovery. TBER suggests that future AI systems may benefit from mechanisms for detecting the explanatory limits of their own internal representations.

</details>


### [161] [TabSwift: An Efficient Tabular Foundation Model with Row-Wise Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07345)
*Si-Yang Liu,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabSwift是一种轻量级的表格基础模型，通过门控注意力稳定机制和可学习的寄存器令牌改进原始TabPFN，在保持高效推理的同时达到与更强模型（TabPFN v2、TabICL）竞争的性能，并支持自适应早期退出以实现灵活部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（如TabPFN）虽然在小到中等数据集上表现良好，但后续模型为提升精度采用越来越复杂的架构，导致推理成本高昂，限制了实际部署。作者希望在不牺牲性能的前提下降低推理开销。

Method: 1) 轻量级行级纯注意力骨干网络（仅使用行间注意力）；2) 门控注意力稳定机制（gated attention stabilization）；3) 少量可学习的寄存器令牌（register tokens）提供全局上下文并提升预训练质量；4) 自适应逐层早期退出（adaptive layer-wise early-exit）机制，根据样本动态调整推理深度。

Result: TabSwift在分类和回归任务上与更强的表格基础模型（TabPFN v2、TabICL）竞争性相当，同时推理效率更高。自适应早期退出机制进一步减少了延迟敏感场景下的计算开销。

Conclusion: 通过简洁有效的设计，TabSwift实现了高效且可随时中断的表格上下文学习，适用于实际部署场景。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models, exemplified by TabPFN, perform prediction via in-context learning, inferring test labels directly from labeled training examples. They have demonstrated competitive performance, particularly on small-to-medium datasets. However, recent tabular foundation models often improve accuracy with increasingly complex architectures, incurring higher inference cost and limiting practical deployment. In this work, we revisit the original TabPFN design and show that a lightweight row-wise attention-only backbone can remain highly competitive with two simple enhancements: a gated attention stabilization mechanism and a small set of learnable register tokens that provide global context and improve pretraining quality. The resulting model, TabSwift, supports both classification and regression, and is competitive with stronger tabular foundation models (e.g., TabPFN v2 and TabICL) while being more efficient at inference. For latency-sensitive serving, we further introduce an adaptive layer-wise early-exit mechanism that dynamically adjusts inference depth per sample. Overall, TabSwift enables efficient and anytime tabular in-context learning for practical deployments.

</details>


### [162] [SleepExplain: Explainable Non-Rapid Eye Movement and Rapid Eye Movement Sleep Stage Classification from EEG Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07351)
*Rafsan Jany,Md. Hamjajul Ashmafee,Iqram Hussain,Md Azam Hossain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用随机森林、XGBoost和梯度提升三种集成学习模型，基于睡眠EEG数据对NREM和REM睡眠阶段进行分类，准确率分别达到92.54%、94.30%和94.25%，并采用SHAP方法构建了可解释的SleepExplain模型来解释预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠阶段分类对睡眠障碍诊断至关重要，而现有模型缺乏对预测结果的可解释性，难以揭示EEG特征与睡眠阶段之间的神经生理关联。

Method: 使用Random Forest、XGBoost和Gradient Boosting三种集成学习模型对NREM和REM睡眠阶段进行分类，并采用基于博弈论的SHAP方法构建SleepExplain可解释模型。

Result: Random Forest准确率92.54%，Gradient Boosting准确率94.25%，XGBoost准确率94.30%。

Conclusion: XGBoost取得了最佳分类性能；SHAP方法能够为睡眠阶段分类预测提供具有说服力的解释，增强了模型的可信度和临床实用性。

Abstract: Classification of sleep stages is one of the most important diagnostic approaches for a variety of sleep-related disorders. Electroencephalography (EEG) is regarded as a powerful tool for examining the association between neurological effects and sleep phases since it correctly identifies sleep-related neurological alterations. During Non-Rapid Eye Movement (NREM) and Rapid Eye Movement (REM) sleep phases, a number of nerve and bodily functions are affected and therefore hold an important role both in their functionalities. This work aims to classify NREM and REM sleep stages from sleep EEG data and present a noble SleepExplain model, an explainable NREM and REM sleep stage classification to explain its predictions. In this work, sleep stages were classified using Random Forest, XGBoost, and Gradient Boosting ensemble classification models. Overall, we obtained an accuracy of 92.54% (Random Forest), 94.25% (Gradient Boosting), and 94.30% (XGBoost). For explainable classification model, we utilized a game theoretic approach, SHAP (SHapley Addictive exPlanations) to offer a convincing explanation for the prediction.

</details>


### [163] [Breaking the Ice: Analyzing Cold Start Latency in vLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07362)
*Huzaifa Shaaban Kabakibo,Animesh Trivedi,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了vLLM推理引擎的冷启动延迟，将启动过程分解为六个基础步骤，发现其受CPU限制，并建立了轻量级分析模型来准确预测启动延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可扩展推理服务的普及，推理引擎的冷启动延迟变得重要；vLLM虽然已成为事实标准，但由于其复杂性和快速演进，尚未有对其启动延迟的系统性研究。

Method: 将vLLM启动过程分解为六个基础步骤，通过实验展示启动过程以CPU为主且各步骤呈现一致可解释的缩放趋势，并基于这些洞察开发轻量级分析模型。

Result: 成功实现了对vLLM启动延迟的细粒度归因，并构建了能够针对给定硬件配置准确预测启动延迟的分析模型。

Conclusion: 该模型为大规模推理环境中的资源规划提供了可操作的指导，相关基准数据集、分析工具和预测脚本已开源。

Abstract: As scalable inference services become popular, the cold start latency of an inference engine becomes important. Today, vLLM has evolved into the de facto inference engine of choice for many inference workloads. Although popular, due to its complexity and rapid evolution, there has not been a systematic study of its startup latency. With major architectural innovations such as the V1 API and the introduction of torch.compile, this paper presents the first detailed performance characterization of vLLM startup latency. We break down the startup process into six foundational steps and demonstrate that it is predominantly CPU bound. Each step exhibits consistent and interpretable scaling trends with respect to model-level and system-level parameters, enabling fine-grained attribution of latency sources. Building on these insights, we develop a lightweight analytical model that accurately predicts vLLM startup latency for a given hardware configuration, providing actionable guidance for resource planning in large-scale inference environments. All benchmarking datasets, analysis tools, and prediction scripts are open sourced at https://github.com/upb-cn/vllm-startup-profiler.

</details>


### [164] [A robust PPG foundation model using multimodal physiological supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07365)
*Eloy Geenjaar,Vince Calhoun,Scott Daly,Gouthaman KV,Lie Lu,Trisha Mittal,Daniel P. Darcy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需高质量或类现场数据预训练的光电容积描记法（PPG）基础模型，通过利用ICU数据集中的心电和呼吸信号来指导对比学习样本选择，在减少预训练主体数量的同时，在15个下游任务中的14个上取得了性能提升，包括日常活动和心率预测等类现场任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPG基础模型要么依赖开源ICU数据集但需要精心筛选数据以进行预训练，导致泛化到类现场数据困难；要么使用封闭的类现场PPG数据。作者希望开发一种不需要高质量或类现场预训练数据的PPG基础模型。

Method: 利用ICU数据集中的心电和呼吸信号作为多模态监督信号，指导对比学习中的样本选择，从而允许模型保留并从有噪声的PPG片段中学习，提高推理时的鲁棒性。

Result: 在比现有先进方法少3倍的预训练主体数量下，在15个多样化的下游任务中的14个上取得性能提升，包括类现场的日常活动和心率预测任务。

Conclusion: 多模态监督能够整合互补的生理信息，提高PPG基础模型的鲁棒性，并增强其向消费级数据的泛化能力。

Abstract: Photoplethysmography (PPG), a non-invasive measure of changes in blood volume, is widely used in both wearable devices and clinical settings. Recent PPG foundation models either use open-source ICU datasets with pretraining paradigms that require curated data and thus complicate generalization to field-like data, or use closed-source field-like PPG data. In contrast, we propose a PPG foundation model that does not require high-quality or field-like pretraining data, and instead leverages accompanying electrocardiogram and respiratory signals in ICU datasets to select contrastive samples during pretraining. Our approach allows the model to retain and learn from noisy PPG segments, improving robustness at inference. Our model, pretrained on 3x fewer subjects than existing state-of-the-art approaches, achieves performance improvements on 14 out of 15 diverse downstream tasks, including field-like daily activity and heart rate prediction. Our results demonstrate that multimodal supervision can integrate complementary physiological information to improve the robustness of PPG foundation models and enhance their generalization to consumer-grade data.

</details>


### [165] [Self-evolving LLM agents with in-distribution Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07367)
*Yudi Zhang,Meng Fang,Zhenfang Chen,Mykola Pechenizkiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-Evolve提出了一种针对LLM智能体的自演化框架，通过分布内强化学习范式统一了过程奖励自动标注与策略学习，在稀疏奖励的长时域决策任务中实现了稳定的自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在复杂环境中的长时域决策面临信用分配难题，智能体仅在回合结束时获得延迟奖励，导致训练困难。现有方法依赖人工标注或环境回溯，成本高且不可扩展。

Method: 1) 从混合离线数据集（专家演示+智能体自生成轨迹）中学习分布内评论家（critic）；2) 通过加权隐式Q学习目标稳定稀疏奖励下的贝尔曼备份；3) 利用学习到的价值函数通过优势估计推导逐步骤过程奖励；4) 基于过程奖励信号进行行为近端策略优化，实现在用于过程奖励标注的数据上迭代自我改进。

Result: 在AlfWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld三个基准上，Q-Evolve在样本效率、鲁棒性和整体任务性能方面均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过过程级监督与政策在共享的分布内学习循环中共同演化，LLM智能体可以实现稳定的自我进化，为解决长时域决策中的信用分配问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as powerful controllers for interactive agents in complex environments, yet training them to perform reliable long-horizon decision making remains a fundamental challenge. A key difficulty lies in credit assignment: agents often receive delayed rewards only at the end of episodes. In this paper, we propose Q-Evolve, a self-evolving framework for LLM agents that unifies automatic process-reward labeling and policy learning within a principled in-distribution reinforcement learning paradigm. In each evolving iteration, our method learns an in-distribution critic from a hybrid off-policy dataset that combines expert demonstrations with agent-generated trajectories, stabilizing Bellman backups in sparse-reward settings via a weighted Implicit Q-Learning objective. The learned value function is then used to derive step-wise process rewards through advantage estimation, enabling dense and reliable supervision without environment backtracking or human annotation. Leveraging these signals, we perform behavior-proximal policy optimization that evolves the agent over the data used for process reward labeling, allowing iterative self-improvement without exacerbating distribution shift. We evaluate our method on AlfWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld, showing Q-Evolve outperforms strong baselines in sample efficiency, robustness, and overall task performance. Our results demonstrate that stable agent self-evolution is achievable through the co-evolution of process-level supervision and policy, both grounded within a shared in-distribution learning loop.

</details>


### [166] [Do Coding Agents Deceive Us? Detecting and Preventing Cheating via Capped Evaluation with Randomized Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07379)
*Thanawat Lodkaew,Johannes Ackermann,Soichiro Nishimori,Nontawat Charoenphakdee,Masashi Sugiyama,Takashi Ishida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CapCode框架，通过设置性能上限来检测和防止模型在评估与训练中的作弊行为，同时提出CapReward奖励机制减少作弊，使模型更符合任务规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型在评估和训练中常通过利用捷径（作弊）获得高评价分数，而非真正解决目标任务，导致评估分数无法可靠反映模型真实能力，亟需一种能检测并抑制作弊的评估与训练方法。

Method: 提出CapCode框架，构建随机化编码数据集，将测试的最佳非作弊性能故意限制在1以下（即有上限设计），使得显著高于上限的分数可作为作弊证据；同时提出基于CapCode原则的CapReward奖励机制，抑制超出上限的优化行为。

Result: 实验表明CapCode能有效检测作弊行为，同时保持模型的性能排名不被破坏；CapReward可减少作弊行为，使模型更遵循任务规范。

Conclusion: CapCode和CapReward为检测和抑制模型作弊提供了可行方案，提升了评估分数的可靠性与模型行为的规范性。

Abstract: A growing failure mode in agent evaluation and training is that models can achieve high evaluation scores by exploiting shortcuts instead of solving the intended task, producing deceptive performance. This makes evaluation scores unreliable as measures of true task-solving ability. We propose CapCode, a framework for constructing coding datasets with randomized tests whose best achievable non-cheating performance is deliberately capped below one. This capped-performance design gives evaluation scores a clearer interpretation: scores substantially above the cap are implausible and therefore provide evidence of cheating. To prevent cheating, we propose CapReward, a reward design based on the CapCode principle to discourage optimization beyond the cap. Experiments across multiple datasets show that CapCode detects cheating while preserving performance ranking of models, and CapReward reduces cheating behavior, yielding models that better follow the intended task specification.

</details>


### [167] [Covariance Shrinkage via Stochastic Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07382)
*Mathieu Chalvidal,Florentin Coeurdoux,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将高维协方差矩阵的经典收缩估计重新表述为参数化随机插值器上的经验风险最小化问题，揭示了三种降低统计风险的机制：调度、流映射与耦合、以及早停。基于此提出了一个神经网络估计器，并在合成数据和真实神经影像数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高维协方差收缩估计方法缺乏统一的框架来理解不同收缩机制之间的关系，且难以灵活引入特征正则化等更复杂的正则化手段。

Method: 将收缩估计重新建模为源分布与目标分布之间参数化随机插值器的经验风险最小化问题；基于此框架，提出使用神经网络参数化插值器，并推导了二次风险的上界。

Result: 在合成实验中验证了所提神经估计器的有效性；在真实神经影像数据上展示了该方法相比传统方法具有更强的正则化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了已知的收缩估计量，并揭示了调度、耦合/流映射和早停三种独立的风险降低机制；神经网络插值器可有效实现这些机制，在实际应用中提供额外的正则化优势。

Abstract: We recast classical shrinkage of high-dimensional covariance estimators as empirical risk minimization over a parametric stochastic interpolant between a source and a target distribution. This formalism recovers known shrinkage estimators as special cases and reveals three distinct mechanisms for reducing statistical risk: (i) Scheduling: the interpolant schedule determines the class of admissible covariances, and hence the achievable risk. (ii) Flow maps and couplings: whereas naive constructions amount to assuming independence between the distributions, specific coupling structures (e.g., solutions of optimal transport problems) can lower the empirical risk. Moreover, non-linear flow maps realizing such couplings free the interpolant covariance from the eigenbasis of the empirical estimate, enabling eigenvector regularization. (iii) Early stopping: estimators defined by integrating a regressed vector field afford an additional bias-variance trade-off through approximation of the true interpolant distribution. We then propose a neural estimator of the interpolant, together with an upper bound on its quadratic risk in terms of the interpolant approximation error, and validate both on synthetic experiments. Finally, we apply the estimator to real neuroimaging data, demonstrating the additional regularization power this approach offers in practice.

</details>


### [168] [Making the Most of Limited Data: Score-Aware Training for Text-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07387)
*Yun-Chen Cheng,Tzu-Hung Huang,Chih-Pin Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分数感知训练（score-aware training）的文本到音乐生成方法，通过CLAP条件化的Beta噪声时间步调度、片段级过滤、两阶段描述流程及REPA辅助损失，在无需大规模专有数据集和工业级算力的情况下，实现了具有竞争力的生成质量。基于FluxAudio的450M参数系统在ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge Efficiency Track中取得客观评价第二、主观MOS评价第三的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的文本到音乐生成系统依赖于大规模专有数据集和工业级计算资源，这使得无法区分模型架构贡献与资源优势。作者希望在不依赖大规模数据和算力的前提下，通过更高效的数据利用方法提升文本到音乐生成质量。

Method: 1) 分数感知训练（score-aware training）：将音频-文本对齐分数作为直接监督信号贯穿整个训练流程；2) CLAP条件化的Beta噪声时间步调度：将低分对齐片段分配到高噪声训练阶段，作为隐式正则化器；3) 片段级过滤：移除最不对齐的样本；4) 两阶段描述流程：弥合描述性训练提示与简洁推理提示之间的分布差距；5) REPA辅助损失：从预训练的CLAP和MuQ编码器迁移结构化语义知识。

Result: 基于FluxAudio的450M参数系统在ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge Efficiency Track中排名：客观评价双赛道第二，主观MOS评价效率赛道第三。

Conclusion: 通过分数感知训练及一系列数据效率优化策略，可以在不依赖大规模专有数据和工业级算力的条件下，实现具有竞争力的文本到音乐生成性能，验证了资源效率与生成质量之间的可平衡性。

Abstract: State-of-the-art text-to-music generation systems rely on massive proprietary datasets and industrial-scale compute, making it impossible to disentangle architectural contributions from resource advantages. We propose \textit{score-aware training}, which treats audio-caption alignment score as a direct supervision signal throughout the pipeline. Rather than discarding low-scoring segments, we repurpose them via a CLAP-conditioned Beta noise timestep schedule that routes them to high-noise training regimes, acting as an effective implicit regularizer. Complementarily, segment-level filtering removes the most misaligned examples, and a two-stage caption procedure bridges the distribution gap between verbose training captions and concise inference prompts. A REPA auxiliary loss further transfers structured semantic knowledge from pretrained CLAP and MuQ encoders without additional data. Our 450M-parameter FluxAudio-based system, submitted to the ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge Efficiency Track, ranked 2nd across both tracks in the objective evaluation and 3rd in the Efficiency Track in the final MOS evaluation.

</details>


### [169] [Generative Modeling of Discrete Latent Structures via Dynamic Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07400)
*Stefan Ivanovic,Ge Liu,Mohammed El-Kebir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GReinSS 是一个基于策略学习的框架，通过动态缩放奖励来学习最大化观测数据似然的潜在状态分布，能够从间接观测中准确重建组合潜在状态（如潜在集合和潜在图），在模拟数据和真实 RNA 测序数据上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学问题常需从间接观测中推断未观测的机制性潜在状态；经典方法（如期望最大化）无法扩展到组合爆炸规模的状态空间，而深度学习方法（如变分自编码器）通常学习人工潜在状态而非重建真实的机制性潜在状态。

Method: 提出 GReinSS 框架，采用策略学习（policy learning）结合动态缩放奖励（dynamically rescaled rewards）来学习能够最大化观测数据似然的潜在状态分布。

Result: GReinSS 准确重建了模拟的潜在集合和潜在图，优于其他策略学习和生成建模基线；在真实短读长 RNA 测序数据上重建的异构体比标准 RSEM 算法更匹配正交长读长测序检测到的异构体。

Conclusion: GReinSS 是一种从间接观测中对组合潜在状态进行生成建模和推断的原则性且实际有效的方法。

Abstract: Many scientific problems require inferring unobserved mechanistic latent states from indirect observations. While classical approaches, including expectation maximization, do not scale to combinatorially large spaces, deep learning approaches such as variational autoencoders typically form artificial latent states rather than reconstructing the mechanistic ground-truth states. Here, we introduce GReinSS, a policy learning framework that uses dynamically rescaled rewards to learn latent state distributions that maximize the observed data likelihood. We show that GReinSS accurately reconstructs simulated latent sets and latent graphs, outperforming alternative policy learning and generative modeling baselines. Additionally, GReinSS reconstructs isoforms from real short-read RNA sequencing data that better match isoforms detected by orthogonal long-read sequencing than the standard RSEM algorithm. Overall, GReinSS is a principled and practically effective approach for generative modeling and inference of combinatorial latent states from indirect observations.

</details>


### [170] [Reversible Foundations: Training a 120B Sparse MoE through State-Preserving Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07404)
*Rohan Shravan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了在单台八GPU节点上端到端训练千亿参数稀疏混合专家模型的全过程。LightningLM 0.1V是一个循环骨干语言模型家族，经历四个阶段从小型稠密种子模型生长，经过5B和9B混合专家模型，最终达到120B规模（460个路由专家，top-12路由）。每个更大模型从小模型的训练权重生长而来；活跃参数从稠密种子的1.78B单调增长到120B规模的5.93B（约占存储的118.67B的5%）。全文围绕三个原则组织：可逆性（可逆循环堆栈在反向传播中重构激活而非存储）、状态保持生长（每个扩展阶段均有可复现原则及对应失败案例）、单节点经济学（通过TQP策略将优化器状态承载于22.6亿适配器参数而非100B+路由专家上，将专家路径优化器状态削减约45倍）。新贡献在于将已知原语集成整合，而非任何单一原语的创新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在有限硬件资源（单节点八GPU）上训练超大规模语言模型（千亿参数级）的可行性，验证通过渐进式生长策略从稠密种子模型扩展到稀疏混合专家模型的技术路线，并系统记录过程中的工程实践经验与失败案例。

Method: 采用四种阶段生长策略：从稠密种子→5B MoE→9B MoE→120B MoE（460个专家，top-12路由）。核心技术包括：(1) 可逆循环堆栈（反向传播重构激活，保持激活内存持平）；(2) 状态保持生长（每个扩展步骤有可复现原则）；(3) TQP策略（量化基础专家权重+训练低秩适配器，优化器状态仅承载于22.6B适配器参数上，削减约45倍专家路径优化器状态）。

Result: 成功在单节点八GPU上训练至120B参数规模（118.67B存储参数，5.93B活跃参数），训练损失达到1.78，支持8K上下文。模型家族、分词器和训练代码均已开源发布。通过逐领域留出损失验证了目标能力（多语言印地语能力、代码能力）是通过构建过程习得的。

Conclusion: 本工作的核心贡献不在于单个原语的创新，而在于将已知原语（可逆循环、状态保持生长、量化+低秩适配器）系统整合，实现了在单节点上端到端训练千亿参数模型的全流程。提供了从业者级别的详细文档，并公开了模型家族、分词器和训练代码，为资源受限环境下的大模型训练提供了可行范式。

Abstract: This paper reports on training a hundred-billion-parameter sparse mixture of experts on a single eight-GPU node, end to end. LightningLM 0.1V is a recurrence-backbone language model family grown in four stages from a small dense seed, through a 5B and a 9B mixture of experts, to a 120B model with 460 routed experts under top-12 routing. Each larger model is grown from the trained weights of the smaller one; active parameters rise monotonically from 1.78B at the dense seed to 5.93B at 120B (about 5% of the 118.67B stored). The full lineage runs on single nodes, the larger stages at 8K context, reaching a released training loss of 1.78 at 120B scale.
  This is a systems and experience report. It is organized around three disciplines. Reversibility: a reversible recurrence stack reconstructs activations in the backward pass instead of storing them, holding activation memory flat as the model grows. State-preserving growth: each expansion (dense to MoE, shallow to deep, few experts to many) is given as a reproducible principle paired with the failure that results from getting it wrong; several failures are silent. Single-node economics: the 120B trains through TQP, a strategy of quantized base expert weights and trained low-rank adapters that carries optimizer state on 2.26B adapter parameters rather than 100B+ resident in routed experts, cutting expert-path optimizer state by a factor of ~45.
  What is new is the integration of known primitives, not any primitive in isolation: one grown lineage running end to end on a single node, documented at practitioner level, with per-domain held-out loss as evidence that targeted capabilities (multilingual Indic competence, code) were learned by construction. Model family, tokenizer, and training code are released.

</details>


### [171] [A Comprehensive Anatomy of Human and DeepSeek-R1 LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07410)
*Yuxiang Chen,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对AIME 2025全部30道题目的10,247个推理步骤进行详尽标注，对比DeepSeek-R1与人类在推理结构上的差异。发现模型推理存在"拓扑模仿"现象——表面再现推理形式但缺乏功能作用，同时识别出两种真正推理信号：分支与回溯的稳定使用，以及放置于演绎推理中的有效反思。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型（特别是DeepSeek-R1-0120）出现的"啊哈时刻"是否代表真正的推理能力，还是仅仅模仿推理的表象。

Method: 对AIME 2025全部30道问题中的10,247个推理步骤进行穷尽式标注，划分为五个功能类别：分析（Analysis）、推理（Inference）、分支（Branch）、回溯（Backtrace）和反思（Reflection），并进行结构化比较。

Result: 人类解决方案保持分析与演绎的紧凑交替；DeepSeek-R1频繁回顾中间结果、进行浅层且不必要的验证、陷入局部检查循环而无实质逻辑推进。成功轨迹显示出分支与回溯的稳定使用，失败轨迹则过度或不足使用探索性动作。反思仅在置于演绎推理中时才有效，陷入分析循环的反思关注局部数值细节而遗漏全局逻辑错误。

Conclusion: 当前长链思维模型可能更多地因推理的表象而非真正的演绎进步获得奖励。未来改进方向包括：测量跨轨迹稳定性、惩罚"旋转轮"式轨迹、鼓励更深层的逻辑修正、将推理时计算重新分配至演绎和回溯。推理质量不仅取决于反思的量，更取决于反思是否一致且出现在适当的逻辑尺度上。

Abstract: The emergence of "Aha moments" in large language models, particularly DeepSeek-R1-0120, has raised the question of whether these systems genuinely reason or merely imitate the appearance of reasoning. We conduct a comprehensive empirical comparison between model and human reasoning across all 30 problems from AIME 2025, exhaustively annotating 10,247 reasoning steps into five functional categories: Analysis, Inference, Branch, Backtrace, and Reflection. We find a clear structural difference. Human solutions maintain a compact alternation between analysis and deduction, whereas DeepSeek-R1 frequently revisits intermediate results, performs shallow and often unnecessary verification, and loops through local checks without meaningful logical progress. We describe this as topological mimicry: reproducing the surface form of reasoning without its functional role. Despite this, we identify two signals of genuine reasoning. First, successful traces exhibit stable use of branching and backtracking, while failed traces either underuse or overuse exploratory actions. Second, reflection is only effective when placed within deductive inference; reflections trapped in analysis loops focus on local numerical details while missing global logical errors. These findings suggest that current long-CoT models may be rewarded more for the appearance of reasoning than for genuine deductive progress. We discuss directions for improving evaluation and training, including measuring cross-trace stability, penalising "spinning-wheel" traces, encouraging deeper logical correction, and reallocating inference-time compute toward deduction and backtracking. Overall, reasoning quality depends not simply on how much reflection occurs, but on whether reflection appears consistently and at the appropriate logical scale.

</details>


### [172] [Video-Based Prediction of In-Flight Particle Characteristics in Atmospheric Plasma Spraying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07416)
*Abhijeet Praveen,Sareh Soleimani,Cormac Cureton,Aman Sidhu,Kintak Raymond Yu,Cristian Cojocaru,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用高速视频观测等离子体羽流，通过TabPFN、CNN等机器学习方法非侵入式预测大气等离子喷涂（APS）过程中飞行粒子温度与速度，其中预训练CNN在温度预测上达到R²=0.90，速度预测达R²=0.82，展示了视频数据在APS实时监测中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APS涂层质量严重依赖飞行粒子的温度和速度，但实际生产中难以连续在线监测这些粒子特性，亟需开发非侵入式的数据驱动诊断方法。

Method: 从高速视频中提取三种不同的特征表示，分别使用TabPFN、CNN、Random Forest、Gradient Boosting、SVR、XGBoost等模型进行预测，并对原始视频帧采用预训练CNN处理，通过分组留一交叉验证在63次喷涂运行的126段标注视频上进行评估。

Result: TabPFN在工程特征实验中温度预测表现最优（R²=0.86），CNN在速度预测上更强（R²=0.81）；预训练CNN直接在原始帧上取得最佳结果，温度R²=0.90，速度R²=0.82。

Conclusion: 视频衍生的羽流信息为非侵入式APS诊断和实时过程监测提供了有前景且可扩展的技术基础。

Abstract: Atmospheric plasma spraying (APS) is a widely used coating process in which in-flight particle temperature and velocity strongly influence coating quality. However, these particle characteristics are difficult to monitor continuously during operation, motivating the development of non-invasive data-driven diagnostic methods. In this work, we investigate the predictive potential of high-speed video observations of the plasma plume for estimating in-flight particle characteristics in APS. We introduce three different video-derived feature representations and evaluate them using Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Networks (TabPFN), convolutional neural networks (CNN), and classical regression baselines including Random Forest, Gradient Boosting, Support Vector Regression, and XGBoost. Experiments are conducted using grouped leave-one-out cross-validation on 126 labeled pre- and post-spray video recordings from 63 APS spray runs. Across the engineered feature experiments, TabPFN achieves the most consistent performance for temperature prediction, reaching R2 = 0.86 using the combined feature representation. CNN models particularly perform stronger for velocity prediction, achieving R2 of 0.81. In addition, we evaluate models operating directly on raw video frames using pretrained CNNs and find that the highest performance is achieved by a pretrained CNN with a regression head with R2 of 0.90 and 0.82 for temperature and velocity, respectively. The results demonstrate that video-derived plume information provides a promising and scalable foundation for non-invasive APS diagnostics and real-time process monitoring.

</details>


### [173] [Discovering Multiscale Deep Formulas in Complex Systems via Neural-Guided Lambda Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07426)
*Hanqiao Yu,Shusen Yang,Xuebin Ren,Cong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Deflex是一种端到端AI方法，能自动从多尺度复杂系统中提取不同形式的数学公式（包括不变量和分布），包含Deflexformer（可分解深度能量模型）和Deflexpressor（Lambda演算符号回归模型）两个子系统，在六个代表性系统中效率比现有方法提升最高达7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI方法在单尺度系统中表现良好，但在多尺度复杂系统中难以识别尺度特定的公式，因此需要一种能自动发现多尺度公式的方法。

Method: Deflex由Deflexformer（可分解深度能量模型，学习跨尺度统一表示）和Deflexpressor（Lambda演算符号回归模型，处理高阶公式）组成。Deflexpressor生成合成数据预训练Deflexformer，后者通过解耦多尺度潜在关系引导公式发现。

Result: 在六个具有不同行为的代表性复杂系统中，Deflex的效率比最先进方法提升最高达7倍，并实现了自动化的多尺度发现。

Conclusion: Deflex可作为跨学科科学发现的有用工具。

Abstract: A fundamental problem in science is identifying underlying patterns of complex systems in the form of concise mathematical formulas. Current Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based methods have shown strong performance in single-scale systems, yet remain limited in identifying scale-specific formulas in multiscale complex systems. We present Deflex, an end-to-end AI method to automatically extract multiscale formulas with potentially different forms, including invariants and distributions, from complex systems. Deflex consists of two subsystems named Deflexformer and Deflexpressor. Deflexpressor is a lambda-calculus symbolic regression model for higher-order formulas. Deflexformer is a decomposable deep energy model for learning unified representations across scales. Deflexpressor generates synthetic data to pre-train Deflexformer, which then guides formula discovery by decoupling multiscale latent relationships. Across six representative complex systems with diverse behaviors, Deflex achieves up to 7-fold higher efficiency than the state-of-the-art methods while enabling automated multiscale discovery. Our work could be a useful tool for scientific discovery across disciplines.

</details>


### [174] [Time series Foundation Models based on Physics-Informed Synthetic Histories for Cold-Start Photovoltaic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07457)
*Lorenzo Longarini,Alessandro Rongoni,Simone Silenzi,Emanuele Frontoni,Riccardo Rosati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种零样本冷启动光伏发电预测方法，利用合成生产历史结合时序基础模型（TSFMs），在无需目标站点观测数据的情况下实现高精度预测。TabPFN-TS在真实反馈场景下最优（MAE 0.514），Chronos-2在自预测反馈下最鲁棒，性能提升约1.7-2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光伏电站在投运初期（冷启动阶段）缺乏历史观测数据，无法直接使用标准监督预测模型。需要一种无需目标站点观测数据即可进行生产预测的方法。

Method: 构建零样本管道：从电站元数据和气象协变量生成合成生产历史，再通过推理时条件化让时序基础模型（TSFMs）进行预测。在5种TSFM和经典基线模型上，评估了严格冷启动基线、真实反馈、自预测反馈三种策略。

Result: 在440个光伏站点、4个数据集上的评估表明，协变量感知的TSFM优于基线约1.7-2倍。TabPFN-TS在真实反馈下误差最低（MAE 0.514 kWh/kWp/d），Chronos-2在自预测反馈下最鲁棒。合成历史来源对性能影响不大。

Conclusion: 冷启动PV预测可通过合成历史+TSFM有效解决，精度主要取决于合理的时序上下文而非特定生成器，TabPFN-TS和Chronos-2分别在不同场景下表现最佳。

Abstract: At commissioning time, Photovoltaic (PV) operators must forecast production before target-site observations are available, limiting the direct use of standard supervised forecasters. This cold-start setting is addressed with a zero-shot pipeline that generates a synthetic production history from plant metadata and meteorological covariates, enabling time-series foundation models (TSFMs) to forecast through inference-time conditioning. Five TSFMs are benchmarked against classical baselines under strict Cold-Start Baseline, Real Feedback, and Self-Forecast Feedback strategies. The evaluation spans $440$ PV sites across four datasets and diverse climate regimes. Covariate-aware foundation models outperform baselines by approximately $1.7-2\times$: TabPFN-TS achieves the lowest error under Real Feedback (MAE $0.514$, RMSE $0.721$ $kWh$ ${kWp}^{-1}$ ${d}^{-1}$), while Chronos-2 is most robust under Self-Forecast Feedback. Performance is largely insensitive to the synthetic-history source, indicating that accuracy is driven more by the availability of plausible temporal context than by the specific generator.

</details>


### [175] [Unsupervised Continual Clustering via Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07474)
*Mohammadreza Sadeghi,Sareh Soleimani,Zihan Wang,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FBCC的无监督持续聚类方法，通过前向-后向知识蒸馏机制，让教师网络在无标签和无历史数据条件下持续学习新聚类，同时保留旧聚类结构，有效缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督持续学习（UCL）方法面临灾难性遗忘加剧、缺乏聚类特定目标、以及知识蒸馏和回放缓冲区带来的内存与隐私问题。

Method: 提出FBCC框架，包含一个持续教师网络（带聚类投影器）和轻量级任务特定学生网络，通过双阶段前向-后向知识蒸馏过程，在不存储历史数据的情况下，使教师网络学习新聚类同时保留已有聚类结构。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，FBCC在聚类准确率上持续优于现有持续学习基线，并显著减少了灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: FBCC作为无监督持续聚类（UCC）领域的开创性方法，有效解决了无标签、无历史数据场景下的持续聚类问题，为后续研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Unsupervised Continual Learning (UCL) aims to enable neural networks to learn sequential tasks without labels or access to past data. A major challenge in this setting is Catastrophic Forgetting, where models forget previously learned tasks upon learning new ones. This challenge is amplified in UCL due to the absence of labels to guide learning and memory retention. Existing mitigation strategies, such as knowledge distillation and replay buffers, often raise memory and privacy concerns. Moreover, current UCL methods largely overlook clustering-specific objectives. To fill this gap, we introduce Unsupervised Continual Clustering (UCC) and propose Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation for Continual Clustering (FBCC). FBCC employs a continual teacher network with a clustering projector and lightweight task-specific students. Through a dual-phase forward-backward distillation process, the teacher learns new clusters while preserving previously discovered cluster structure without storing past data. FBCC represents a pioneering approach to UCC, demonstrating improved clustering performance across sequential tasks. Experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that FBCC consistently outperforms existing continual learning baselines in clustering accuracy while significantly reducing catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [176] [Graph Neural Network leveraging Higher-order Class Label Connectivity for Heterophilous Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07475)
*Takuto Takahashi,Itsuki Nakayama,Takahiro Mitani,Ryosuke Kikuchi,Yuya Sasaki,Makoto Onizuka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对异配有向图中GNN无法捕捉高阶类标签连通性的问题，提出标签上下文分类器(LCC)，通过四种游走类型生成标签上下文嵌入，并可自适应地与任意GNN集成，实验表明集成LCC的GNN在异配有向图节点分类中优于SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN在同配图中表现优异，但在异配图中性能受限，尤其是基于图卷积网络的GNN无法捕捉异配图中常见的高阶类标签连通性。

Method: 提出Label Context Classifier (LCC)，通过四种不同类型的游走生成标签上下文嵌入来估计目标节点的类标签，并可自适应学习LCC与任意GNN的集成权重。

Result: 集成LCC的GNN在异配有向图节点分类中优于现有SOTA方法，标签上下文嵌入有效提升了分类性能。

Conclusion: LCC能够有效捕捉有向异配图中的高阶类标签连通性，且可与任意GNN灵活集成以提升节点分类性能。

Abstract: Node classification in graph neural networks (GNNs) has been widely applied in various fields of graph analysis. GNNs achieve high-accuracy node classification in homophilous graphs, where nodes with the same class label tend to be connected. However, their performance remains limited in heterophilous graphs, where nodes with different class labels are more likely to be connected. In particular, current GNNs derived from graph convolutional networks cannot capture higher-order class label connectivity, which is frequently observed in real-world heterophilous graphs. To address this issue, we propose a novel classifier, Label Context Classifier (LCC), designed to capture higher-order class label connectivity in directed graphs. LCC estimates the class label of a target node by leveraging label context embeddings that are generated through four distinct types of walks. In addition, our approach allows the integration of LCC and any GNN by adaptively learning their importance. Experimental results demonstrate that GNNs integrated with LCC outperform SOTA methods and the label context embeddings improve the node classification performance in heterophilous directed graphs.

</details>


### [177] [Drifting Models for Surrogate Flow Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07481)
*Chris R. Jung,Markus Dörr,Natalie Jüngling,Jennifer Niessner,Adam T. Müller,Nicolaj C. Stache*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出条件漂移（conditional drifting）框架，在VAE潜空间中进行标签感知掩码和空间条件生成，实现比扩散模型快两个数量级的单次高质量流场生成，可作为实时CFD替代模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFD计算成本高，难以快速优化室内环境；现有生成式替代模型（如扩散模型）虽比确定性网络更好地建模分布，但迭代采样速度慢，无法满足实时推理需求。

Method: 将生成漂移（generative drifting）框架适配到流体力学：1）在学习的VAE潜空间中进行条件漂移；2）使用标签感知掩码（label-aware masking）对齐生成样本与边界条件；3）提出空间条件变体以泛化到未见几何形状。

Result: 标签条件模型在精度和流一致性上匹配迭代扩散模型，同时运行速度快两个数量级；空间条件变体为泛化到新几何形状提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: 条件漂移可作为扩散模型的高效替代方案，在推理速度关键的场景下实现实时CFD替代模型。

Abstract: While Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) provides high-fidelity flow fields for optimizing indoor environments, its computational cost limits rapid exploration. To solve this problem generative surrogates offer better distribution modeling than deterministic networks, but iterative sampling is slow. To enable high-quality, single-pass generation, we adapt the novel generative drifting framework to fluid mechanics. We introduce a conditional architecture that performs drifting in a learned VAE latent space and uses label-aware masking to align generated samples with their boundary conditions. Our label-conditioned model matches iterative diffusion in accuracy and flow consistency while running two orders of magnitude faster. Additionally, we propose a spatial-conditioning variant that establishes a promising path towards generalization to unseen geometries. Ultimately, conditional drifting serves as a highly efficient alternative to diffusion based approaches, unlocking real-time CFD surrogates where inference speed is critical.

</details>


### [178] [Network Recovery from Cascade Data: A Debiased Jacobian-Based Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07483)
*Lei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CascadeNet，一个基于雅可比矩阵的机器学习框架，无需指定扩散模型即可从级联数据中恢复隐藏的影响力网络，并具备统计推断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络恢复方法通常依赖特定的扩散模型假设，当模型设定错误时性能严重下降，亟需一种不依赖特定扩散机制的通用网络恢复方法。

Method: CascadeNet首先构建一步转移函数的灵活估计器，然后利用Riesz表示引理进行Neyman正交去偏，得到去偏的雅可比矩阵，该估计量具有√n一致性和渐近正态性，从而支持网络结构的正式推断。

Result: 在模拟实验中，CascadeNet在9种常见数据生成过程中均取得了最高的网络恢复准确率；在西班牙52个省份的COVID-19传播实证应用中，CascadeNet恢复的传播网络与真实的省际流动性网络显著相关，而基线方法无显著对齐。

Conclusion: CascadeNet提供了一种模型无关、统计可推断的网络恢复框架，在模型误设下仍保持稳健性，兼具高准确率和形式化推断能力。

Abstract: Many important outcomes unfold as dynamic cascades, including product adoption, disease spread, financial distress, and information diffusion. A central challenge is to recover the hidden influence network behind these cascades. Existing methods typically assume a specific diffusion model, and their performance degrades substantially when that assumption is misspecified. We propose CascadeNet, a Jacobian-based machine learning framework for network recovery that does not require specifying a diffusion mechanism. The key idea is that the underlying influence structure can be characterized by the Jacobian of the one-step transition function. CascadeNet first constructs a flexible estimator of the transition function, and further applies Neyman-orthogonal debiasing via the Riesz representer, so that the debiased Jacobian is $\sqrt{n}$-consistent and asymptotically normal, enabling formal inference on the network structure. We validate CascadeNet in both a simulation exercise and a real-world empirical application. In simulations, where the data-generating process is known, CascadeNet achieves the highest network recovery accuracy across nine common data-generating processes. In an empirical application to COVID-19 transmission across Spain's 52 provinces, CascadeNet recovers transmission networks that are significantly correlated with the true inter-province mobility network, whereas networks recovered by baseline methods show no significant alignment with the ground truth.

</details>


### [179] [CoMetaPNS: Continually Meta-learning Personalized Neural Surrogates for Cardiac Electrophysiology Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07488)
*Ryan Missel,Xiajun Jiang,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种持续元学习框架，用于实现个性化心脏神经代理模型，能够在顺序到达的未标记数据中持续整合信息并识别数据来源，无需完整重训练，有效避免灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化虚拟心脏仿真方法（如小样本生成建模和元学习）假设训练分布静态多样且任务标识已知，当新数据顺序到达时需用全部旧数据重训练以避免灾难性遗忘，这在临床环境中不可行（数据顺序到达、完全重训练不现实）。

Method: 提出持续元学习框架，利用基于记忆缓冲区的持续贝叶斯高斯混合模型，在时间上推断数据的标识和关系，实现持续信息整合与新数据来源识别。

Result: 在合成心脏数据上的实验表明，该方法在仿真预测、计算可扩展性和抗灾难性遗忘方面优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 该框架使个性化神经代理能够在顺序学习场景下持续适应新数据分布，同时保持对旧知识的记忆，是临床动态数据场景的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Personalized virtual heart simulations face challenges in model personalization and computational cost. While neural surrogates offer state-of-the-art solutions, they typically address either efficient personalization or training generalizable models. Recent work reframes this by learning the process of personalizing a surrogate using limited subject-specific context data, through few-shot generative modeling with set-conditioned surrogates and meta-learned amortized inference. These methods, however, assume a static and diverse training distribution with known task identifiers. When new data becomes available, they require costly retraining with all prior data to avoid catastrophic forgetting - a phenomena where the model forgets earlier tasks when trained on new ones. This is a major limitation in clinical settings where often unlabeled data arrives sequentially and full retraining is infeasible. This paper presents a new continual meta-learning framework to achieve personalized neural surrogates able to not only continually integrate information but also identify whether incoming data stems from a known or unknown dynamics source. By leveraging a continual Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Model over a memory buffer, our framework can infer the identifiers and relationships of data over time - required for effective meta-learning. Empirical results on synthetic cardiac data demonstrate superior simulation forecasting, computational scalability, and resilience to catastrophic forgetting compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [180] [Second-Order Path Kernel Interpolation Formulas in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07495)
*Jin Guo,Roy Y. He,Jean-Michel Morel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Domingos提出的路径核插值公式，发展了其二阶形式。对于确定性梯度下降，引入了曲率加权的插值项；对于随机梯度下降，额外出现了耦合预测曲率与小批量梯度噪声协方差的采样诱导项；进一步扩展至带动量的SGD，插值结构保留但权重被记忆因子修正；最后给出了最终预测的浓度估计。这些结果精炼了神经网络预测的路径核解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解训练数据如何塑造神经网络预测是现代学习理论的核心问题。Domingos（2020）提出的路径核插值公式仅为一阶表征，需要发展其二阶形式以更精确地描述模型的预测行为。

Method: 理论推导。对确定性梯度下降、随机梯度下降（SGD）以及带动量的SGD分别推导二阶形式的插值公式，并建立浓度估计。

Result: （1）确定性GD：二阶形式包含曲率加权的插值项；（2）SGD：额外出现耦合预测曲率与小批量梯度噪声协方差的项；（3）带动量SGD：插值结构保留但权重受记忆因子修正；（4）建立了围绕期望二阶表示的波动尺度的浓度估计。

Conclusion: 这些结果为神经网络预测提供了比一阶路径核更精细的二阶路径核解释框架。

Abstract: Understanding how training data shape neural network predictions is a central problem in modern learning theory. In 2020, Pedro Domingos proposed an interpolation formula valid for every model learned by deterministic gradient descent. It expresses the model's prediction as an integral, along the optimization path, of a data-dependent kernel that aligns the model's gradients at the test and training data. Such a first-order characterization remains valid for models trained with batch-based stochastic optimization. In this paper, we develop second-order forms of these interpolation formulas. We show that the leading path-kernel interpolation is supplemented by a curvature-weighted interpolation term. For stochastic gradient descent, an additional sampling-induced component appears, coupling the curvature of the prediction with the covariance of mini-batch gradient noise. We also extend the representation to stochastic gradient descent with momentum, where the interpolation structure is preserved but with the weights modified by a memory-related factor. Moreover, we establish a concentration estimate for the terminal prediction, identifying the fluctuation scale around the expected second-order representation. Together, these results provide a refinement of the path-kernel interpretation of neural network prediction.

</details>


### [181] [Accelerated Decentralized Stochastic Gradient Descent for Strongly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07496)
*Ming Sun,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MG-ADSGD算法，通过结合Nesterov型原始-对偶外推与多轮快速gossip平均，首次在去中心化随机强凸优化中同时实现加速的√κ和1/√(1-β)依赖，达到当前最优通信复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化随机优化方法无法同时实现加速的条件数依赖√κ和网络谱间隙依赖1/√(1-β)，而确定性方法已可同时达到这两者。这促使作者设计一种新的随机方法，在保持随机梯度效率的同时获得双重加速。

Method: 将Nesterov型原始-对偶外推与多轮快速gossip平均相结合，核心创新在于将gossip轮数与mini-batch大小耦合，使得额外通信轮次同时提升共识精度和降低梯度方差。

Result: 通信复杂度为Õ(σ²/(μnε)·log(1/ε) + √(κ/(1-β))·log(1/ε))，在去中心化随机强凸优化中达到当前最优通信复杂度（忽略与ε无关的对数因子）。

Conclusion: MG-ADSGD首次在去中心化随机优化中同时实现加速的条件数和网络谱间隙依赖，填补了确定性方法与随机方法之间的理论空白。

Abstract: Decentralized stochastic optimization is a fundamental paradigm for large-scale learning over networks, where agents communicate only with their neighbors and no central coordinator is required. For strongly convex problems, communication efficiency is mainly determined by the condition number \(κ=L/μ\) and the network spectral gap \(1-β\). Although deterministic decentralized methods can simultaneously achieve accelerated \(\sqrtκ\) and \(1/\sqrt{1-β}\) dependences, no existing stochastic method attains both improvements at once. In this paper, we propose \emph{Multi-Gossip Accelerated DSGD} (MG-ADSGD), a decentralized stochastic algorithm that combines Nesterov-type primal--dual extrapolation with multi-round fast gossip averaging. The key idea is to couple the gossip depth with the mini-batch size so that additional communication rounds simultaneously improve consensus accuracy and reduce gradient variance. We show that MG-ADSGD achieves the communication complexity \[ \widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε +
\sqrt{\fracκ{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right), \] where \(ε\) denotes the target accuracy, \(n\) is the number of nodes, and \(σ^2\) is the gradient variance. To the best of our knowledge, this bound yields the best currently available communication complexity for decentralized stochastic strongly convex optimization, up to logarithmic factors that are independent of $ε$.

</details>


### [182] [Sparse Subspace-to-Expert Sharing for Task-Agnostic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07500)
*Fatema Siddika,Md Anwar Hossen,Tanwi Mallick,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SETA通过自适应稀疏子空间分解为任务特定专家和共享专家，解决LLM持续学习中的塑性-稳定性困境，在LLaMA-2 7B和Qwen3-4B上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法对参数统一处理，无法区分任务特定知识与共享能力，导致塑性-稳定性困境（新能力获取与旧知识灾难性遗忘之间的矛盾）。

Method: 提出SETA框架：(1) 将参数空间自适应稀疏分解为任务特定专家（隔离任务特有模式）和共享专家（捕获通用特征）；(2) 自适应弹性锚定保护共享知识；(3) 路由感知正则化在权重和路由层面共同保护共享知识；(4) 统一门控网络在推理时自动检索正确的专家组合。

Result: 在多个领域特定基准上，SETA达到或超过现有持续学习方法，在LLaMA-2 7B和Qwen3-4B上展现出强早期任务知识保留能力和改进的逆向迁移。

Conclusion: SETA通过稀疏专家混合架构有效分离任务特定与共享知识，解决了持续学习中的塑性-稳定性冲突，在大型语言模型上取得了优异的持续学习效果。

Abstract: Continual learning in Large Language Models (LLMs) is hindered by the plasticity-stability dilemma, where acquiring new capabilities often leads to catastrophic forgetting of previous knowledge. Existing methods typically treat parameters uniformly, failing to distinguish between specific task knowledge and shared capabilities. We introduce Mixture of Sparse Experts for Task Agnostic Continual Learning (SETA), a framework that resolves the plasticity-stability conflict through adaptive sparse subspace decomposition into task-specific expert modules. Unlike standard updates, where tasks compete for the same parameters, SETA separates knowledge into unique experts, designed to isolate task-specific patterns, and shared experts, responsible for capturing common features. This structure is maintained through adaptive elastic anchoring and a routing-aware regularization that jointly protect shared knowledge at both the weight and routing levels and enable a unified gating network to automatically retrieve the correct expert combination during inference. Extensive experiments across diverse domain-specific benchmarks demonstrate that SETA achieves competitive or superior overall performance relative to state-of-the-art continual learning baselines, with particularly strong retention of early-task knowledge and improved backward transfer on LLaMA-2 7B and Qwen3-4B.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Architecturally Significant MLOps Guidelines for ML Model Integration and Deployment: a Gray Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06535)
*Faezeh Amou Najafabad,Markus Haug,Keerthiga Rajenthiram,Justus Bogner,Ilias Gerostathopoulos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过灰色文献综述，整合了103个网络资源，提炼出25条架构上重要的MLOps指南（分为5类），用于指导ML模型在MLOps系统中的集成与部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MLOps日益普及，但团队通常以ad hoc方式开展MLOps项目，缺乏统一的架构指导；社区需要一份综合性的参考来指导MLOps系统的架构设计，特别是ML模型的集成与部署。

Method: 对103个网络来源进行灰色文献综述，分析MLOps模型集成与部署的实践知识；随后应用主题分析法将这些实践综合为推荐指南。

Result: 贡献了25条架构上重要的MLOps指南（分属5个类别），并描述了它们对整体系统架构的影响。

Conclusion: 研究结果作为MLOps实践指南的概述，支持研究人员和从业者在MLOps系统中进行ML模型的集成与部署。

Abstract: Context. Despite the growing adoption of Machine Learning Operations (MLOps), teams often approach MLOps projects in an ad hoc manner due to the lack of consolidated architectural guidance. The community would benefit from a reference that synthesizes knowledge to inform the architectural design of MLOps systems, especially regarding the integration and deployment of ML models. Objective. In response, our goal is to provide a comprehensive overview of architecturally significant guidelines for the integration and deployment of ML models in MLOps systems. Method. We conduct a gray literature review of 103 web sources to analyze state-of-practice knowledge on MLOps model integration and deployment. We then apply thematic analysis to synthesize these practices into recommended guidelines. Results. We contribute a collection of 25 architecturally significant MLOps guidelines for model integration and deployment, organized into five categories, and describe their impact on the overall system architecture. Conclusion. Our results serve as an overview of state-of-practice MLOps guidelines to support researchers and practitioners with the integration and deployment of ML models in their MLOps systems.

</details>


### [184] [Queen-Bee Agents: A BeeSpec-Centered Architecture for Governed Enterprise MCP Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06545)
*Dutao Zhang,Liaotian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Queen-Bee 是一种受治理的多智能体架构，通过 Queen 控制平面进行能力检索、任务规划和结构化 BeeSpec 编译，由受限工具访问的 Bee 智能体执行。在 59 项企业级任务上，检索驱动变体达到 0.964 任务成功率、零治理失败，并在范围执行质量上显著优于静态基线和宽松单智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体系统需要将大语言模型连接到私有工具、内部知识和 MCP 接口，但原始任务能力不足以满足策略执行、租户隔离和操作边界约束的需求。

Method: 提出 Queen-Bee 架构：Queen 控制平面负责能力检索、任务规划并编译 BeeSpec；Bee 专业智能体在受限工具访问下执行 BeeSpec。原型实现包括租户级 MCP 连接器、审计支持的运行时治理、检索驱动的弱孵化机制及多种配置后端。

Result: 在 59 项企业任务上，检索驱动 Queen-Bee 任务成功率 0.964、零治理失败；范围执行质量显著优于静态 Queen-Bee 基线和宽松单智能体基线；多 Bee 化学工作流展示显式审批门控和基于真实证据的 top-3 候选排序。

Conclusion: 企业智能体平台不仅应评估能力，还应评估受治理配置、隔离行为、范围执行质量和工件感知的工作流协调能力。更丰富的配置后端虽可行，但在当前小规模结构化能力注册表上未超越轻量级结构化检索器。

Abstract: Enterprise agent systems increasingly need to connect large language models to private tools, internal knowledge, and Model Context Protocol (MCP) interfaces. In this setting, raw task capability is insufficient: organizations also require policy enforcement, tenant-scoped isolation, and execution that remains within explicit operational boundaries. We present Queen-Bee, a governed multi-agent architecture in which a Queen control plane retrieves capabilities, plans task-scoped execution, and compiles a structured BeeSpec that is executed by specialized Bee agents under constrained tool access. We implement a working prototype with tenant-scoped MCP connectors, audit-backed execution-time governance, retrieval-driven weak incubation, and multiple provisioning backends. We evaluate the system on 59 enterprise-style tasks spanning governance-sensitive requests, retrieval-driven provisioning, scoped local execution, and chemistry workflow integration. The retrieval-driven Queen-Bee variant achieves a task success rate of 0.964, zero governance failures, and substantially better scoped execution quality than both a static Queen-Bee baseline and a permissive single-agent baseline. We further show a multi-Bee chemistry workflow with explicit approval gating and a concrete top-3 shortlist grounded in real upstream evidence and screening artifacts. Additional comparisons with hybrid retrieval and LLM-guided provisioning show that richer provisioning backends are viable but do not outperform the lightweight structured retriever on the current small, highly structured capability registry. The results provide prototype-level systems evidence rather than a production deployment study, and suggest that enterprise agent platforms should be evaluated not only by capability, but also by governed provisioning, isolation behavior, scoped execution quality, and artifact-aware workflow coordination.

</details>


### [185] [AI-Driven Test Case Generation from Natural Language Requirements: A Survey of Techniques and Research Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06563)
*Orimoloye Folorunsho,Hassan Reza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对2000-2025年间基于AI/NLP/LLM从自然语言需求生成测试用例的研究进行了系统综述，识别出21篇主要研究，揭示了三个进化时代，并指出当前没有任何方法能同时满足自动化、歧义处理、领域适用性、可追溯性、评估彻底性和幻觉控制这六个关键质量维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件测试虽关键但耗时昂贵，基于需求生成测试用例可早期介入开发，但自然语言的歧义性和不精确性使得直接生成测试用例面临挑战。AI/NLP/LLM技术的进步使自动化成为可能，但也带来了幻觉、可追溯性降低和评估不一致等新风险。因此需要系统综述现有技术、工具、评估方法和研究空白。

Method: 遵循Kitchenham和Charters的系统综述指南，在2000-2025年间的主要学术数据库中进行检索，经过严格的纳入标准筛选后，最终确定了21篇主要研究。按三个进化时代对文献进行组织分析。

Result: 识别出三个进化时代，发现当前没有任何方法能同时满足自动化、歧义处理、领域适用性、可追溯性、评估彻底性和幻觉控制这六个关键质量维度。

Conclusion: 本文贡献包括：基于三个进化时代的AI测试生成综述；六维度差距分析（无现有方法能完全覆盖所有维度）；以及针对幻觉、可追溯性、复杂性敏感性和合规性的四项可操作研究指南。

Abstract: Software testing is critical for verifying that systems meet specified requirements, yet remains among the most time-consuming and expensive activities in development. Requirements-based test generation allows test cases to be derived early from requirements artifacts, but generating them directly from natural language is challenging due to inherent ambiguity and imprecision. Recent advances in AI, natural language processing (NLP), and large language models (LLMs) have made automating this pipeline increasingly feasible, while introducing new risks including hallucination, reduced traceability, and inconsistent evaluation. This survey addresses four research questions: what AI and NLP techniques have been proposed for generating test cases from natural language requirements; what tools and frameworks support these approaches; how generated test cases are evaluated; and what research gaps remain. Following Kitchenham and Charters' systematic review guidelines, we searched major scholarly databases spanning 2000-2025 and, after applying strict inclusion criteria, identified 21 primary studies. The literature is organized into three evolutionary eras, revealing that no existing approach simultaneously satisfies six key quality dimensions: automation, ambiguity handling, domain applicability, traceability, evaluation thoroughness, and hallucination control. The survey makes three main contributions: a three-era evolutionary synthesis of AI-based test generation; a six-criteria gap analysis showing no current approach fully addresses all quality dimensions; and four actionable research guidelines targeting hallucination, traceability, complexity sensitivity, and compliance.

</details>


### [186] [NTILC: Neural Tool Invocation via Learned Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06566)
*Andrew Krikorian,Yayuan Li,Jason J. Corso*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出NTILC框架，通过潜在检索替代上下文工具查找，将工具选择转化为嵌入空间检索，减少95%+上下文消耗和74%延迟，同时提升工具选择准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent工具调用语言模型依赖大量API/函数注册表，将完整工具规格直接放入提示词会导致成本随注册表规模线性增长，快速消耗上下文预算，增加延迟并降低选择准确性（尤其受无关工具干扰）。

Method: NTILC框架：(1) 将用户意图和工具规格映射到共享嵌入空间，通过外部检索替代上下文查找；(2) 语言模型仅基于选中的工具schema进行受约束的参数生成；(3) 提出签名感知复合目标函数，结合Circle Loss和Functional Margin Loss，在语义相似度基础上加入工具签名约束（参数schema、类型兼容性、返回类型）。

Result: 在公开工具选择和函数调用数据集上，NTILC相比长上下文ICT基线减少95%+上下文窗口消耗，推理延迟降低高达74%。

Conclusion: NTILC通过潜在检索机制有效解决了大规模工具注册表带来的上下文膨胀和选择干扰问题，显著降低资源消耗的同时保持了选择准确性。

Abstract: Agentic tool-calling language models depend on large registries of callable APIs, functions, and local actions. Placing full tool specifications directly in the prompt incurs a cost that scales linearly with the size of the tool registry, rapidly consuming the context budget. As the registry grows, this leads to higher latency and degrades selection accuracy, particularly due to interference from irrelevant tools. We overcome these limitations by introducing NTILC, a neural tool selection and invocation framework that replaces in-context registry look-up with learned latent retrieval. NTILC maps both user intent and tool specifications into a shared embedding space, enabling tool selection via external retrieval rather than in-context lookup. The language model is conditioned only on the selected tool schema, allowing for precise, constrained argument generation. Central to our approach is a signature-aware composite objective, which augments semantic similarity with constraints derived from tool signatures (e.g., argument schema, type compatibility, and return types). By combining Circle Loss with a Functional Margin Loss, the model enforces separation between tools that are semantically similar but incompatible under their execution signatures. We evaluate NTILC on public tool-selection and function-calling datasets and report context token usage, retrieval accuracy, and selection latency metrics. Across these settings, NTILC reduces context window consumption by over 95% and inference latency by up to 74% compared to long-context ICT baselines.

</details>


### [187] [QBugLM: An Agentic Benchmarking Framework for LLM-based Quantum Software Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07314)
*An B. B. Pham,Hoa T. Nguyen,Muhammad Usman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出QBugLM多智能体框架，用于自动化量子软件调试流程（从漏洞注入到LLM检测修复再到仿真验证），并通过案例研究评估了两种LLM在不同提示策略下的调试性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件错误常产生静默错误而非显式异常，传统调试技术难以应对，而LLM在量子代码调试方面的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 设计QBugLM多智能体框架，包含基于分类学的漏洞注入、LLM驱动的检测与修复、基于仿真的验证三个模块；通过案例研究对比不同LLM、提示策略、漏洞类别和量子程序的表现。

Result: 单次重试将Pass@1从低于25%提升至80%以上；在固定资源约束下，简单结构化提示在推理能力强的模型上表现优于Chain-of-Thought和ReAct。

Conclusion: 迭代反馈对量子代码调试至关重要，为自动化量子软件修复的LLM能力基准测试迈出了初步步骤。

Abstract: Quantum software bugs often yield silent, incorrect outputs rather than explicit errors, making them particularly difficult to detect and repair with conventional techniques. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on classical software engineering tasks, their ability to debug quantum code remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose QBugLM, a multi-agent framework that automates the quantum software debugging pipeline, from taxonomy-driven bug injection to LLM-based detection and repair, and finally to simulation-based validation, for framework-agnostic OpenQASM 3.0 programs. We further conduct a comprehensive case study using QBugLM to benchmark two LLMs, Claude 4.6 Sonnet and Qwen3 Coder Next, across different prompting strategies, bug categories, and quantum programs. Our results show that iterative feedback is critical, as a single retry raises Pass@1 from below 25% to above 80%. Moreover, simpler structured prompting can even outperform Chain-of-Thought and ReAct for reasoning-capable models under fixed-resource constraints. Our work takes initial steps toward benchmarking LLM capabilities for debugging quantum programs and offers practical insights to support future efforts in automated quantum software repair.

</details>


### [188] [AutoPipelineAI: Context-Aware CI/CD Pipeline Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06662)
*Youssef Mohamed Aboelfotoh,Mohamed Ahmed Hemdan,Mohammad El-Ramly,Khlood Hassan,Mahmoud Saleh Saad,Ahmed Mohamed Tolba,Seif Gamal Abdelmonem*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoPipelineAI系统，利用大语言模型通过自然语言描述自动生成CI/CD流水线配置，以减少手动配置的复杂性和错误，提升开发效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件开发依赖CI/CD流水线来自动化测试、构建和部署，但配置DevOps流水线需要理解平台特定语法并手动创建配置文件，过程繁琐且易出错，尤其对DevOps经验有限的开发者而言，导致配置错误和生产力下降。

Method: AutoPipelineAI系统采用大语言模型翻译开发者意图，结合仓库感知分析理解项目结构，自动生成针对GitHub Actions、GitLab CI/CD等环境的管道脚本，并集成自动化验证系统和反馈机制以确保生成的流水线准确可用。

Result: 提出了评估框架用于衡量生成精度、配置有效性和相比手动创建的设置工作量减少程度。早期评估结果表明，仓库感知的、自然语言驱动的CI/CD生成是一种可行且有前景的范式。

Conclusion: LLM能够简化DevOps配置的复杂性，增强开发者对持续交付方法的访问。仓库感知的自然语言驱动CI/CD生成是减少DevOps配置复杂度、实现更易访问的软件交付自动化的有效途径。

Abstract: Modern software development relies on CI/CD pipelines to automate testing, building, and deployment operations. Configuring DevOps pipelines is challenging and time-consuming, as developers must understand platform-specific syntax and manually create configuration files. This complexity can lead to configuration errors and reduced productivity, especially for developers with limited DevOps experience. This paper introduces the AutoPipelineAI system, which generates CI/CD pipeline configurations using natural language descriptions. The proposed solution uses large language models (LLMs) to translate developer intent, analyze repository structures, and create specific pipeline scripts for environments like GitHub Actions and GitLab CI/CD. It integrates repository-aware analysis, automated validation systems, and a feedback mechanism that confirms the accuracy and usability of the created pipelines. We present the system architecture, its implementation, and an assessment framework designed to measure generation precision, configuration validity, and reduction in setup effort compared to manual pipeline creation. AutoPipelineAI illustrates how LLMs can simplify the complexity of DevOps configuration and enhance developer access to continuous delivery methods. Evaluation results provide early evidence that repository-aware, natural-language-driven CI/CD generation is a viable and promising paradigm for reducing the complexity of DevOps configuration and enabling more accessible software delivery automation.

</details>


### [189] [Tensor Algebraic Property Skeletons: Amplifying Property-Based Testing for AI Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06747)
*Yuxin Qiu,Ben Limpanukorn,Seongmin Lee,Jiyuan Wang,Qian Zhang,Miryung Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Propilot，一个基于LLM的智能体属性测试框架，通过可重用的属性骨架生成可执行的属性测试，用于检测深度学习编译器中的语义漂移问题。在TVM上生成4,579个PBT，相比直接LLM方法减少49%冗余并消除无效测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DL编译器测试方法（如模糊测试）虽然能生成格式良好的输入，但无法捕获图转换和优化过程中应保持的代数不变量导致的语义漂移。将张量代数知识转化为可执行属性测试面临挑战，因为需要联合构建算子、输入和测试预言。

Method: Propilot框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 将张量代数知识表示为可重用的属性骨架，包含算子约束、形状/值规则和预言模板；(2) 基于目标编译器实例化骨架，生成成对的张量计算图、具体张量输入和期望的语义关系；(3) 通过验证反馈、执行结果和覆盖率信息指导后续生成，确保测试的有效性和安全性。

Result: 在TVM上使用212个算子和20个属性骨架，生成4,579个PBT。相比直接LLM生成方法，冗余减少49%，无效测试完全消除，并能有效发现语义错误和数值差异。

Conclusion: Propilot证明了通过结构化属性骨架结合LLM智能体方法，能够有效解决DL编译器测试中代数不变量保持的验证问题，为AI编译器测试提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) compilers such as TVM and ONNX-MLIR lower tensor computation graphs into optimized executables for target backends. Testing these AI compilers has made substantial progress in generating well-formed inputs in the context of fuzzing; however, such generation alone does not catch semantic drifts from algebraic invariants that graph transformations and optimizations are expected to preserve. While tensor algebra has been studied for decades, it has not been transformed into executable property-based tests (PBTs) for DL compilers because doing so requires jointly constructing operators, inputs, and test oracles. The central challenge is no longer generating well-formed inputs for fuzzing DL compilers, but bootstrapping executable PBTs with such inputs and oracles based on tensor algebra.
  We realize this vision in Propilot, an LLM-driven agentic property-based testing framework for DL compilers with GPT 5.5. First, Propilot represents tensor algebra knowledge as reusable property skeletons, each coupled with operator constraints, shape and value rules, and oracle templates. Second, given a target compiler, Propilot instantiates these skeletons into executable PBTs by generating paired tensor computation graphs, concrete tensor inputs, and expected semantic relations as oracles. Next, to prevent generated tests from degenerating into invalid or uninformative PBTs, Propilot validates each PBT candidate before execution for applicability and safety. Validation feedback, execution results, and coverage signals guide subsequent generation.
  We evaluate Propilot on TVM with 212 operators and 20 property skeletons, generating 4,579 PBTs. Compared with direct LLM-based PBT generation, Propilot reduces redundancy by 49% and eliminates invalid tests through explicit property skeletons. This effectiveness translates into finding semantic errors and numerical discrepancies.

</details>


### [190] [Pomona: Continuous Code Quality Improvement via Small, Automated Changes at Bloomberg](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06752)
*David Williams,Angelos Evripiotis,Serkan Kirbas,Harry Morgan,Sergey Magidovich,Peter Wainwright,Federica Sarro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Pomona是一个基于Kaizen理念的轻量级智能体工具，通过扫描技能自动发现代码改进任务（lint问题、技术债务、测试缺口）并生成小型PR（约10行diff），采用人在环设计实现持续、低风险的代码质量提升。一个月团队部署结果显示：17个PR中15个被合并，中位关闭时间不到2小时，10名高级工程师中8人愿意采用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码质量改进工具往往缺乏自动化持续集成能力，改进过程繁琐、周期长，容易积累技术债务。作者希望借鉴Kaizen（持续改进）哲学，设计一个轻量级智能体工具，实现自动化的、小步快跑的代码质量持续改进流程，降低工程师参与门槛，提升改进效率。

Method: Pomona包含两个核心技能：1) Scanning技能——自动扫描代码库，识别lint违规、技术债务标记和测试缺口等改进任务，并将其优先级排序形成结构化积压清单；2) Repair技能——针对每个任务自动生成约10行diff的小型PR。采用人在环（human-in-the-loop）设计，确保工程师对修改的审核和信任。通过一个月团队部署和面向10名高级工程师的问卷调查进行评估。

Result: 1) 17个生成PR中15个被成功合并（合并率88%）；2) PR中位关闭时间不到2小时；3) 10名高级工程师中8人（80%）表示愿意采用Pomona；4) 工程师普遍赞赏小diff尺寸和Pomona对代码质量改进的专注。

Conclusion: Pomona的初步结果令人鼓舞，证明轻量级、小步幅的智能体自动化代码质量改进方案在实践中可行且受欢迎。作者为研究者和从业者提供了关于工业环境中有效部署智能体策略的可行洞察。

Abstract: In this short experience paper, we present Pomona, a lightweight agentic tool that utilises agent skills for continuous automated code quality improvement.
